FORBIDDEN_FIELDS = frozenset({"source", "code", "command", "shell", "sql", "script"})
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 60.0
# Keep a prepared request in place for short shared-submission waits. Waits
# longer than one hold, or accumulating beyond the total, still re-queue the
# step so a worker slot is not parked behind a long ramp.
PREPARED_HOLD_MAX_WAIT_SECONDS = 60.0
PREPARED_HOLD_TOTAL_SECONDS = 180.0


def _prepared_hold_seconds(exc, *, transport_mode, transport_deadline, held_seconds):
    """Seconds to wait in place with the prepared body, or None to re-queue."""
    from .batch_submission import WAIT_CODE
    wait = getattr(exc, 'retry_after_seconds', None)
    if (getattr(exc, 'code', None) != WAIT_CODE or not getattr(exc, 'retryable', False)
            or isinstance(wait, bool) or not isinstance(wait, (int, float))
            or not 0 < wait <= PREPARED_HOLD_MAX_WAIT_SECONDS
            or held_seconds + wait > PREPARED_HOLD_TOTAL_SECONDS):
        return None
    if transport_mode == 'batch':
        if transport_deadline is None:
            return None
        remaining = (transport_deadline - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
        if remaining <= wait + 30:
            return None
    return float(wait)


class _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat:
    """Keep one runtime lease alive for the whole external provider call."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        attempt_id: int,
        lease_token: str,
        worker_id: str,
        lease_seconds: int,
    ) -> None:
        self._attempt_id = attempt_id
        self._lease_token = lease_token
        self._worker_id = worker_id
        self._lease_seconds = lease_seconds
        self._stop = Event()
        self._error: BaseException | None = None
        # Monotonic time of the last renewal the database confirmed.  Start
        # from construction: the lease was freshly claimed/renewed then.
        self._last_renewed = time.monotonic()
        self._thread = Thread(
            target=self._run,
            name=f"workflow-lease-heartbeat-{attempt_id}",
            daemon=True,
        )

    def _run(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.wait(_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS):
            try:
                self.tick()
            except BaseException as exc:
                self._error = exc
                self._stop.set()
                return

    def tick(self) -> None:
        """Renew the lease; tolerate transient DB errors while the lease is safe.

        A lock wait timeout (1205), deadlock (1213) or dropped connection
        (2006/2013) must not abort an in-flight paid provider call: the lease
        is still valid, so retry at the next interval.  Stop only when the
        lease is explicitly lost (WorkflowRuntimeError) or the time since the
        last confirmed renewal reaches half the lease, where it may really
        expire before another attempt succeeds.
        """
        try:
            self._renew_once()
        except SQLAlchemyOperationalError as exc:
            unrenewed = time.monotonic() - self._last_renewed
            if unrenewed >= self._lease_seconds / 2:
                raise
            logger.warning(
                "workflow lease heartbeat transient database error; retrying "
                "attempt_id=%s unrenewed_seconds=%.0f error=%s",
                self._attempt_id, unrenewed, type(exc.orig or exc).__name__,
            )
            return
        self._last_renewed = time.monotonic()

    def _renew_once(self) -> None:
        heartbeat_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            heartbeat_runtime_step(
                heartbeat_db, self._attempt_id, self._lease_token,
                self._worker_id, lease_seconds=self._lease_seconds,
            )
            heartbeat_db.commit()
        except BaseException:
            try:
                heartbeat_db.rollback()
            except Exception:  # A dropped connection may also fail rollback.
                logger.debug("heartbeat rollback failed", exc_info=True)
            raise
        finally:
            heartbeat_db.close()

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        self._thread.join()
        if self._error is not None:
            raise self._error


def _first_seen_wait_committed(run_id: int, step_key: str) -> bool:
    """Request vs callback phase: decided by the committed wait, not attempt_no."""
    lookup_db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        return image_first_seen_wait_exists(lookup_db, run_id, step_key)
    finally:
        lookup_db.close()


class FixtureExecutionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        error_policy_kind: str = "fail",
        retryable: bool | None = None,
        retry_after_seconds: int | None = None,
        evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        failure_phase: str = "preflight",
    ):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.error_policy_kind = error_policy_kind
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds
        self.evidence = dict(evidence or {})
        self.failure_phase = failure_phase
        if retry_after_seconds is not None and (
            code.startswith("quota_pool_") or code == "model_provider_429"
        ):
            self.technical_error_type = "429"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FixtureResult:
    output_manifest: dict[str, Any]
    output_hash: str


def hash_manifest(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    import hashlib

    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _reject_forbidden(value: Any, *, path: str = "$") -> None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            if str(key).lower() in FORBIDDEN_FIELDS:
                raise FixtureExecutionError(
                    "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden",
                    f"fixture manifest 包含禁止字段：{path}.{key}",
                )
            _reject_forbidden(child, path=f"{path}.{key}")
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            _reject_forbidden(child, path=f"{path}[{index}]")


def _lookup_path(input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> Any:
    current: Any = input_manifest
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                "fixture_input_path_missing",
                f"fixture 输入字段不存在：{path}",
            )
        current = current[part]
    return current


def execute_fixture(
    kind: str,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    attempt_no: int,
) -> FixtureResult:
    _reject_forbidden(manifest)
    fixture_kind = manifest.get("fixture", kind)
    if fixture_kind != kind:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "fixture_kind_mismatch",
            "步骤执行器与脚本 manifest 的 fixture 类型不一致",
        )
    if kind not in {"identity", "transform", "route", "noop", "fail_once"}:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "fixture_kind_unsupported",
            f"未知 deterministic fixture：{kind}",
        )

    if kind == "identity":
        output = dict(input_manifest)
    elif kind == "transform":
        mapping = manifest.get("mapping")
        if not isinstance(mapping, Mapping):
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                "fixture_mapping_required",
                "transform fixture 必须声明 mapping 对象",
            )
        output: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for output_key, source in mapping.items():
            if not isinstance(output_key, str) or not output_key.strip():
                raise FixtureExecutionError(
                    "fixture_mapping_key_invalid",
                    "transform mapping 的输出字段必须是非空字符串",
                )
            if isinstance(source, str):
                output[output_key] = _lookup_path(input_manifest, source)
            else:
                output[output_key] = source
    elif kind == "route":
        branch_field = manifest.get("branch_field", "branch")
        branch = _lookup_path(input_manifest, branch_field)
        branches = manifest.get("branches", {})
        if not isinstance(branches, Mapping):
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                "fixture_branches_required",
                "route fixture 必须声明 branches 对象",
            )
        output = {
            "branch": branch,
            "target": branches.get(str(branch), manifest.get("default_target")),
        }
    elif kind == "fail_once":
        if attempt_no == 1:
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                "FIXTURE_FAIL_ONCE",
                "确定性 fixture 首次尝试失败，用于验证恢复",
                retryable=True,
            )
        output = dict(input_manifest)
        output["fixture"] = "fail_once"
    else:
        output = {
            "fixture": "noop",
            "input_hash": hash_manifest(input_manifest),
        }

    return FixtureResult(output_manifest=output, output_hash=hash_manifest(output))


def _composer_v3_output(
    *,
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    step_key: str,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_hash: str,
    raw_output: Mapping[str, Any],
    attempt_no: int,
    started_at: datetime | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    node = _composer_v3_node(run_snapshot, step_key)
    if node is None:
        return None
    try:
        return execute_native_node(
            NodeExecutionContext(
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                raw_fixture_output=raw_output,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
                started_at=started_at,
                input_fingerprint=input_hash,
            )
        )
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        error_policy = node.get("error_policy")
        policy_kind = (
            error_policy.get("kind", "fail")
            if isinstance(error_policy, Mapping)
            else "fail"
        )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            evidence=exc.evidence,
        ) from exc


def _composer_v3_node(
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    step_key: str,
) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    workflow = run_snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or manifest.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v3":
        return None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return None
    node = next(
        (
            item
            for item in nodes
            if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("node_instance_id") == step_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    if node is None:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "fixture_node_snapshot_missing",
            f"运行冻结快照中找不到节点实例：{step_key}",
        )
    return node


def _aggregate_case_result(
    *,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_fingerprint: str,
    attempt_no: int,
    started_at: datetime | None,
) -> FixtureResult:
    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "aggregate_config_invalid",
            "aggregate_case 节点配置损坏",
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
        )
    try:
        output = aggregate_case(input_manifest, config)
    except WorkflowCaseAggregationError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
        ) from exc
    try:
        validate_native_node_output(node, output)
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
            evidence=getattr(exc, "evidence", None),
        ) from exc
    node_result = canonical_node_result(
        node=node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest,
        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint,
        output=output,
        attempt_no=attempt_no,
        started_at=started_at,
    )
    return FixtureResult(
        output_manifest=node_result,
        output_hash=hash_manifest(node_result),
    )


def _node_error_policy(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    value = node.get("error_policy")
    return (
        str(value.get("kind") or "fail")
        if isinstance(value, Mapping)
        else "fail"
    )


def _model_failure(
    invocation_id: int | None,
    *,
    code: str,
    message: str,
    retryable: bool,
    error: BaseException,
    provider_dispatched: bool,
) -> None:
    if invocation_id is None:
        return
    failure_db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        fail_model_invocation(
            failure_db,
            invocation_id=invocation_id,
            error_code=code,
            error_message=message,
            request_correlation_id=getattr(
                error, "request_correlation_id", None
            ),
            upstream_status_code=getattr(
                error, "upstream_status_code", None
            ),
            evidence={
                "retryable": retryable,
                **({"wait_cap_hit": True, "wait_cap_seconds": float(error.wait_cap_seconds)}
                    if getattr(error, "wait_cap_hit", False) else {}),
                **({"usage_status": "provider_reported" if getattr(error, "provider_usage", None)
                    is not None else "pending_reconciliation"}
                    if getattr(error, "upstream_status_code", None) == 200 else {}),
                **({"finish_reason": error.finish_reason}
                    if getattr(error, "finish_reason", None) is not None else {}),
                **({"provider_error_code": error.provider_error_code}
                    if getattr(error, "provider_error_code", None) is not None else {}),
                **(
                    {
                        "failure_evidence": redact_workflow_secrets(
                            getattr(error, "evidence")
                        )
                    }
                    if isinstance(getattr(error, "evidence", None), Mapping)
                    and getattr(error, "evidence")
                    else {}
                ),
                **(
                    {
                        "retry_after_seconds": getattr(
                            error, "retry_after_seconds"
                        )
                    }
                    if getattr(error, "retry_after_seconds", None) is not None
                    else {}
                ),
                "technical_error_type": getattr(
                    error, "technical_error_type", type(error).__name__
                ),
                "attempt_count": int(getattr(error, "attempt_count", 0) or 0),
                # 留下可诊断的违反详情（异常链逐层信息），否则运营只看得到错误码
                "detail": _failure_detail(error),
                # schema 失配时附模型原文摘要（≤600 字），区分措辞/合同/供应商问题
                **_failure_raw_excerpt(error),
            },
            provider_dispatched=provider_dispatched,
            provider_usage=getattr(error, "provider_usage", None),
        )
        failure_db.commit()
    finally:
        failure_db.close()


def _transport_context(*, attempt_id, node, invocation_key, **_):
    from . import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery
    from .models import WorkflowModelInvocation, ContentIngressEvent, BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id) if attempt else None
        if run is None:
            raise FixtureExecutionError('model_step_attempt_missing', '模型步骤不存在', retryable=False)
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
        runtime = snapshot['runtime_context']
        queued_at = run.created_at
        if run.source_type == 'content_ingress_event':
            event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
            if event is None:
                raise FixtureExecutionError('model_material_queue_time_missing', '素材入队事实不存在', retryable=False)
            queued_at = event.received_at
        elif run.source_type == 'baseline_regression_item':
            item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id)
            regression = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, item.run_id) if item else None
            if regression is None:
                raise FixtureExecutionError('model_material_queue_time_missing', '回归素材入队事实不存在', retryable=False)
            queued_at = regression.created_at
        limits = runtime['real_execution_node_limits'][node['node_instance_id']]
        online_limit = limits['online_fallback_limits']['max_attempts']
        prefix = recovery.invocation_prefix(invocation_key)
        rows = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == node['node_instance_id'],
            WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key.startswith(prefix),
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status.in_(['reserved','succeeded','failed','rejected']),
        )).all()
        if any(row.status == 'reserved' for row in rows):
            raise FixtureExecutionError('model_transport_call_unresolved',
                '已有调用尚未完成结算，保留费用并停止重复派发', retryable=False)
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        from .effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time, latest_batch_window_restart, rows_after_restart
        queued_at = effective_queue_time(db, run_id=run.id, queued_at=queued_at, now=now)
        # 人工重试重开过传输窗口：只数窗口之后的账本行算「第几次机会」，之前的行仍占用
        # 保留号，保证新请求拿到没用过的账本身份（费用与旧行一概不动）。
        restart_at = latest_batch_window_restart(db, run_id=run.id)
        counted_rows = rows_after_restart(rows, restart_at)
        retired_rows = len(rows) - len(counted_rows)
        from .batch_experiment import batch_only_experiment, HOLD_CODE
        batch_only = batch_only_experiment(db, run)
        from .incremental_sla import online_fallback_allowed
        online_allowed = online_fallback_allowed(run)
        decision = recovery.transport_decision(counted_rows, prefix=prefix,
            queued_at=queued_at, now=now, online_limit=online_limit, batch_only=batch_only,
            online_allowed=online_allowed)
        if retired_rows:
            from dataclasses import replace as _replace
            decision = _replace(decision, reservation_no=decision.reservation_no + retired_rows)
        if decision.mode == 'held':
            raise FixtureExecutionError(HOLD_CODE,
                '批量并发实验：本节点批量尝试已用尽或已有在线记录，保留任务等待处理，未转在线',
                retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=900)
        deadline = recovery.utc(now if batch_only else queued_at)+timedelta(seconds=recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS)
        retry_at = recovery.resource_retry_at(rows, prefix=prefix, mode=decision.mode)
        if retry_at is not None:
            if decision.mode == 'batch':
                retry_at = min(retry_at, deadline)
            if retry_at > now:
                import math
                raise FixtureExecutionError(recovery.RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE,
                    ('供应商资源等待：批量并发实验不自动转在线，真实请求与未决费用仍计入预算' if batch_only else
                     '供应商资源等待：已排队退避；有效等待满 24 小时转在线，维护暂停扣除，真实请求与未决费用仍计入预算'),
                    retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=math.ceil((retry_at-now).total_seconds()),
                    evidence={'retry_at': retry_at.isoformat(), 'transport_mode': decision.mode,
                              'batch_deadline': deadline.isoformat()})
        return decision, deadline


from .workflow_model_executor import SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX  # noqa: E402  账本身份与执行器共用一个前缀


def _real_prompt_steps(**kwargs):
    """One immediate re-ask when the provider breaks its own frozen schema.

    Observed on production material at roughly 1%: the model returns an
    otherwise complete B aesthetic payload but omits a required field in a
    single dimension, with finish_reason stop rather than truncation. The step
    level retry already covers it, but it costs a whole attempt and its lease,
    and both attempts failing sends the run to a dead letter.

    The retry needs its own ledger identity: a model call is keyed by
    (step_attempt_id, invocation_key) and a settled failure may only be
    re-settled with the identical failure, so reusing the key would raise
    model_invocation_idempotency_conflict instead of re-asking. If the run has
    no request budget left for the extra call, the original schema failure is
    raised unchanged and behaviour degrades to the step level retry.
    """
    base = kwargs.get('invocation_key', 'root')
    if base.startswith(SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX):
        return (yield from _real_prompt_transport_steps(**kwargs))
    try:
        return (yield from _real_prompt_transport_steps(**kwargs))
    except FixtureExecutionError as first:
        if first.code != 'structured_output_schema_mismatch':
            raise
        try:
            return (yield from _real_prompt_transport_steps(**{
                **kwargs, 'invocation_key': SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX + base,
            }))
        except FixtureExecutionError as second:
            # Budget exhaustion is not a new diagnosis; keep the real one.
            if second.code.startswith('model_budget_'):
                raise first from None
            raise


def _real_prompt_transport_steps(**kwargs):
    from . import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery
    runtime = kwargs['run_snapshot'].get('runtime_context', {})
    try:
        enabled = recovery.policy(runtime) is not None
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError('model_transport_policy_invalid', str(exc), retryable=False) from exc
    if not enabled:
        return (yield from _real_prompt_attempt_steps(**kwargs))
    original_key = kwargs.get('invocation_key', 'root')
    prefix = recovery.invocation_prefix(original_key)
    while True:
        try:
            decision, deadline = _transport_context(**{**kwargs, 'invocation_key':original_key})
        except FixtureExecutionError:
            raise
        except (ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
            raise FixtureExecutionError('model_transport_policy_invalid', '冻结兜底参数缺失或不合法', retryable=False) from exc
        if decision.mode == 'exhausted':
            raise FixtureExecutionError('model_transport_attempts_exhausted',
                '批量及在线兜底尝试已耗尽，保留失败与费用记录', retryable=False)
        call_key = f'{prefix}{decision.mode}:{decision.attempt_no}'
        if decision.reservation_no > 1:
            call_key += f':reservation:{decision.reservation_no}'
        try:
            return (yield from _real_prompt_attempt_steps(**{
                **kwargs, 'invocation_key':call_key,
                'transport_mode':decision.mode, 'transport_deadline':deadline,
            }))
        except FixtureExecutionError as exc:
            if exc.code == 'model_batch_deadline_before_dispatch':
                continue
            if exc.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait' and decision.mode == 'batch':
                remaining = max(0, (deadline-datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds())
                if remaining <= 0:
                    continue
                raise FixtureExecutionError(exc.code, str(exc), retryable=True,
                    retry_after_seconds=min(exc.retry_after_seconds, remaining),
                    evidence={'transport_mode': 'batch', 'batch_deadline': deadline.isoformat()}) from exc
            if exc.code == 'model_provider_429':
                # Settlement has persisted the real HTTP 429. Re-enter the
                # ledger controller to park, releasing this worker's slot.
                continue
            if exc.code in recovery.BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_CODES:
                from .failure_recovery import PERMANENT_BUDGET_CODES
                if exc.code in PERMANENT_BUDGET_CODES and kwargs.get('run_snapshot', {}).get('runtime_context', {}).get('run_mode') == 'incremental':
                    raise FixtureExecutionError(exc.code,
                        f'预算待处置：{exc}；额度不会自动增加，请在失败处理页核验后限次续跑',
                        retryable=False, evidence={'budget_error_code': exc.code}) from exc
                raise FixtureExecutionError(recovery.BUDGET_WAIT_CODE,
                    f'预算待处理：{exc}；任务保留，等待费用对账或人工处理，预算不会自动增加',
                    retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=900,
                    evidence={'budget_error_code': exc.code}) from exc
            cause = exc.__cause__
            provider_unknown = (isinstance(cause, (DoubaoError, ProviderDeadlineExceeded))
                and getattr(cause, 'technical_error_type', None) in {'timeout','network','provider5xx'})
            can_retry = provider_unknown or exc.code in recovery.RECOVERABLE_FAILURE_CODES
            if not can_retry:
                if exc.error_policy_kind in {'continue', 'skip'}:
                    exc.error_policy_kind = 'fail'
                raise
            # Persisted invocation rows own the counters. No nested client retry
            # or workflow attempt reset can silently create a fourth batch call.
            delay = 5
            if provider_unknown:
                delay = exc.retry_after_seconds if exc.retry_after_seconds is not None else 300
            if decision.mode == 'batch':
                delay = min(delay, max(0, (deadline-datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()))
            if provider_unknown and delay > 0:
                # The physical invocation was settled as unknown (fee retained)
                # and its quota lease released before returning here. Park the
                # *same* step via fail_runtime_step; sleeping here would retain
                # both the async slot and the outer admission gate (ADR-0085).
                raise FixtureExecutionError(recovery.RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE,
                    str(exc), retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=delay,
                    evidence={'transport_mode': decision.mode,
                              'batch_deadline': deadline.isoformat(),
                              'recovery_error_code': exc.code,
                              'wait_cap_hit': getattr(cause, 'wait_cap_hit', False)}) from exc
            if delay > 0:
                import asyncio
                heartbeat = _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
                    lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'],
                    lease_seconds=DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS)
                yield ProviderWait(lambda: asyncio.sleep(delay), timeout_seconds=delay+30,
                    heartbeat=heartbeat.tick, heartbeat_interval=_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS)


def _real_prompt_attempt_steps(
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_client_type: Callable[[Any], Any],
    invocation_key: str = "root",
    frozen_input_override: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    input_fingerprint: str | None = None,
    invocation_attempt_no: int | None = None,
    complete_parent: bool = True,
    media_evidence_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    transport_mode: str | None = None,
    transport_deadline: datetime | None = None,
) -> Generator[ProviderWait, Any, FixtureResult]:
    policy_kind = _node_error_policy(node)
    wait_cap_applied = None
    # 调用 A 已判红线命中的素材不再花钱调 B：输出短路标记，收尾裁决会按红线
    # 策略直接给 L5（与老评测路径的 redline prefilter 行为一致）。
    short_circuit = None
    node_config_probe = node.get("config")
    if (
        isinstance(node_config_probe, Mapping)
        and node_config_probe.get("asset_key") == "prompt_b"
    ):
        precheck_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            probe_attempt = precheck_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            precheck_output = None
            if probe_attempt is not None:
                a_attempt = precheck_db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == probe_attempt.run_id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_a",
                        ProductionStepAttempt.status == "succeeded",
                    )
                    .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc())
                )
                if a_attempt is not None:
                    try:
                        envelope = json.loads(
                            a_attempt.output_manifest_json or "{}"
                        )
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        envelope = {}
                    candidate = envelope.get("output")
                    if isinstance(candidate, Mapping):
                        precheck_output = candidate
            short_circuit = redline_short_circuit_output(
                run_snapshot, node, precheck_output
            )
        finally:
            precheck_db.close()
    if short_circuit is not None:
        marker_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            attempt = marker_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            if attempt is None:
                raise FixtureExecutionError(
                    "model_step_attempt_missing",
                    "真实模型步骤不存在",
                    error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                    retryable=False,
                )
            node_result = canonical_node_result(
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                input_fingerprint=attempt.input_hash,
                output=short_circuit,
                attempt_no=attempt.attempt_no,
                started_at=attempt.started_at,
                extra={"redline_short_circuit": True},
            )
        finally:
            marker_db.close()
        return FixtureResult(
            output_manifest=node_result,
            output_hash=hash_manifest(node_result),
        )
    invocation_id: int | None = None
    timeout_recovery_attempt_no = None
    timeout_recovery_api_path = None
    ledger_terminal = False
    provider_dispatched = False
    try:
        def prepare_local():
            nonlocal timeout_recovery_attempt_no, timeout_recovery_api_path, wait_cap_applied
            preparation_db = SessionLocal()
            try:
                attempt = preparation_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
                run = (
                    preparation_db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
                    if attempt is not None
                    else None
                )
                if attempt is None or run is None:
                    raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                        "model_step_attempt_missing",
                        "真实模型步骤或运行不存在",
                    )
                from .runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
                preflight_model_pool(
                    preparation_db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
                    lease_token=lease_token, worker_id=worker_id,
                    transport_mode=transport_mode, transport_deadline=transport_deadline,
                    invocation_key=invocation_key, node=node,
                )
                # 媒体准备（下载、锚点/目标图重采样、压缩缓存）在锚点降像素等策略下
                # 可能超过 300s 默认租约；此前只有供应商调用阶段有心跳，
                # 准备阶段一旦超时步骤就被回收成 STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED（run 207 首波 14 个 B 步骤）。
                preparation_heartbeat = _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
                    attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    lease_token=lease_token,
                    worker_id=worker_id,
                    lease_seconds=DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS,
                )
                preparation_heartbeat.start()
                endpoint_before = getattr(run, "doubao_endpoint_id", None)
                try:
                    prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                        preparation_db,
                        run=run,
                        attempt=attempt,
                        node=node,
                        input_manifest=input_manifest,
                        invocation_key=invocation_key,
                        frozen_input_override=frozen_input_override,
                        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint,
                        invocation_attempt_no=invocation_attempt_no,
                        **({'transport_mode':transport_mode} if transport_mode is not None else {}),
                    )
                finally:
                    preparation_heartbeat.stop()
                timeout_recovery_attempt_no = attempt.attempt_no
                timeout_recovery_api_path = getattr(prepared.client_config, "api_path", None)
                if transport_mode == 'batch':
                    remaining = (transport_deadline-datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        raise FixtureExecutionError('model_batch_deadline_before_dispatch',
                            '素材已满24小时，下一次调度使用在线兜底', retryable=True)
                    # ADR-0084: the local provider wait is capped so a hung batch
                    # call (observed 80-160 min) releases its slot; the material's
                    # 24 h transport deadline is unchanged.
                    from .batch_inference import provider_wait_cap_seconds
                    wait_cap = provider_wait_cap_seconds()
                    if wait_cap is not None and wait_cap < remaining:
                        prepared.client_config.timeout_seconds = wait_cap
                        wait_cap_applied = wait_cap
                    else:
                        prepared.client_config.timeout_seconds = remaining
                # 豆包接入点在准备阶段冻结到运行上（resolve_run_endpoint）；准备会话默认只读关闭，
                # 不提交就会丢失冻结，改绑后同一运行的后续调用会切到新接入点。
                if getattr(run, "doubao_endpoint_id", None) != endpoint_before:
                    preparation_db.commit()
            finally:
                preparation_db.close()

            return prepared

        local_heartbeat = _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token, worker_id=worker_id, lease_seconds=DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS)
        if local_work_enabled.get():
            prepared = yield LocalWork(prepare_local, heartbeat=local_heartbeat.tick,
                                       heartbeat_interval=_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS)
        else:
            prepared = prepare_local()

        # This lease tracks Worker liveness, not the provider's response deadline.
        # The independent heartbeat renews it during a healthy 24-hour batch wait;
        # a killed Worker must become recoverable without waiting another day.
        # Provider HTTP timeout and the model quota lease remain unchanged.
        worker_lease_seconds = DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS
        from .batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
        submission_estimate = estimate_prepared_submission(prepared)
        ticket = None
        from .batch_submission_fairness import enabled as fair_enabled, register_ready
        if fair_enabled() and prepared.endpoint_id and prepared.endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-'):
            with SessionLocal() as ready_db:
                ticket = register_ready(ready_db, attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    lease_token=lease_token, worker_id=worker_id, node=node,
                    invocation_key=invocation_key)
                ready_db.commit()
        held_seconds = 0.0
        while True:
            # A short shared-submission wait keeps the prepared request body
            # instead of discarding it: losing the pool lock by a few seconds
            # used to re-queue the step and repeat download/hash/encode from
            # scratch. Long waits still release the worker slot as before.
            hold = None
            reservation_db = SessionLocal()
            try:
                try:
                    invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                        reservation_db,
                        attempt_id=attempt_id,
                        node=node,
                        run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                        invocation_key=invocation_key,
                        reserved_images=len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities),
                        dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity,
                        endpoint_id=prepared.endpoint_id,
                        submission_estimate=submission_estimate,
                        fair_ticket=ticket,
                        **({'transport_mode':transport_mode} if transport_mode is not None else {}),
                    )
                except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
                    reservation_db.rollback()
                    hold = _prepared_hold_seconds(
                        exc, transport_mode=transport_mode,
                        transport_deadline=transport_deadline, held_seconds=held_seconds)
                    if hold is None:
                        raise
                else:
                    if invocation.status != "reserved":
                        # A quota wait during the schema re-ask requeues the
                        # same step attempt. Its root call is already settled:
                        # replay that diagnosis so _real_prompt_steps resumes
                        # the distinct re-ask key, without sending or settling
                        # the paid root again.
                        ledger_terminal = True
                        if invocation.status in {"failed", "rejected"}:
                            stored = json.loads(invocation.evidence_json or "{}")
                            failure = stored.get("failure", {})
                            failure = failure if isinstance(failure, Mapping) else {}
                            evidence = failure.get("failure_evidence", {})
                            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                                invocation.error_code,
                                invocation.error_message,
                                retryable=(failure.get("retryable") is True
                                    and invocation.error_code != "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"),
                                evidence=evidence if isinstance(evidence, Mapping) else {},
                            )
                        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                            "model_invocation_already_settled",
                            "模型调用已完成结算，停止重复发送；请核验步骤结果",
                        )
                    heartbeat_runtime_step(
                        reservation_db,
                        attempt_id,
                        lease_token,
                        worker_id,
                        lease_seconds=worker_lease_seconds,
                    )
                    invocation_id = invocation.id
                    if prepared.media_bundle.items:
                        record_prepared_media(
                            reservation_db,
                            invocation_id,
                            prepared.media_bundle,
                            invocation_context=media_evidence_context,
                        )
                    reservation_db.commit()
            finally:
                reservation_db.close()
            if hold is None:
                break
            held_seconds += hold
            import asyncio
            hold_heartbeat = _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token, worker_id=worker_id,
                lease_seconds=DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS)
            yield ProviderWait(lambda delay=hold: asyncio.sleep(delay), timeout_seconds=hold+30,
                heartbeat=hold_heartbeat.tick, heartbeat_interval=_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS)

        def mark_provider_dispatched() -> None:
            nonlocal provider_dispatched
            provider_dispatched = True

        lease_heartbeat = _RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            worker_id=worker_id,
            lease_seconds=worker_lease_seconds,
        )
        node_config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
        node_config = node_config if isinstance(node_config, Mapping) else {}
        vote_samples = node_config.get("vote_samples")
        vote_samples = vote_samples if isinstance(vote_samples, int) and not isinstance(vote_samples, bool) and vote_samples > 1 else 1
        vote_temperature = node_config.get("vote_temperature")
        vote_temperature = vote_temperature if isinstance(vote_temperature, (int, float)) and not isinstance(vote_temperature, bool) else None
        pairwise_anchors = node_config.get("pairwise_anchors") is True
        pairwise_samples = node_config.get("pairwise_samples")
        pairwise_samples = pairwise_samples if isinstance(pairwise_samples, int) and not isinstance(pairwise_samples, bool) and pairwise_samples > 1 else 1
        if pairwise_anchors:
            level_thresholds = score_level_thresholds(run_snapshot)
            provider_call = lambda: execute_pairwise_anchors(
                prepared,
                level_thresholds=level_thresholds,
                pairwise_samples=pairwise_samples,
                user_prompt=node_config.get("pairwise_prompt"),
                vote_samples=vote_samples,
                vote_temperature=vote_temperature,
                client_type=model_client_type,
                on_provider_dispatch=mark_provider_dispatched,
            )
            # 主调用 + 逐张锚点两图比较（条目内最多 4 路并发）：等待上限按四倍放大
            wait_factor = 4.0 + (vote_samples - 1) * 0.5
        else:
            provider_call = lambda: execute_prompt_votes(
                prepared,
                samples=vote_samples,
                temperature=vote_temperature,
                client_type=model_client_type,
                on_provider_dispatch=mark_provider_dispatched,
            )
            # N 次并发采样共用一个等待：超时按单次上限放大，避免多采样被误判超时
            wait_factor = 1 + (vote_samples - 1) * 0.5
        real_result = yield ProviderWait(
            provider_call,
            timeout_seconds=prepared.client_config.timeout_seconds * wait_factor,
            heartbeat=lease_heartbeat.tick,
            heartbeat_interval=_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
        )
        # Third-party client test doubles or compatible private clients may not
        # expose the dispatch hook.  A successful return proves that the
        # provider phase completed, so all following DB failures are unknown.
        provider_dispatched = True

        heartbeat_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            heartbeat_runtime_step(
                heartbeat_db,
                attempt_id,
                lease_token,
                worker_id,
            )
            heartbeat_db.commit()
        finally:
            heartbeat_db.close()

        # Retry only the rolled-back database transaction, never the provider.
        # Media usage and invocation pricing remain atomic. A deterministic
        # parent rule error must preserve the already known provider response.
        for transaction_attempt in range(1, 5):
            settlement_db = SessionLocal()
            try:
                semantic_validation_error: Exception | None = None
                try:
                    semantic_conflicts = inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
                        prepared.output_contract.name,
                        real_result.node_result.get("output", {}),
                        contract_profile=frozen_inspiration_contract_profile(
                            run_snapshot
                        ),
                        output_schema=prepared.output_contract.schema,
                        run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                        node=prepared.node,
                    )
                except Exception as exc:
                    # Once a provider response and usage exist, validator bugs must
                    # never bypass actual settlement.  Fail closed after pricing.
                    semantic_conflicts = ()
                    semantic_validation_error = exc
                invocation_evidence = dict(real_result.invocation_evidence)
                if semantic_conflicts:
                    invocation_evidence["semantic_validation"] = {
                        "status": "rejected",
                        "reasons": list(semantic_conflicts),
                    }
                elif semantic_validation_error is not None:
                    invocation_evidence["semantic_validation"] = {
                        "status": "error",
                        "error_type": type(semantic_validation_error).__name__,
                    }
                if prepared.media_bundle.items:
                    media_pricing_snapshot = dict(
                        prepared.media_pricing_snapshot
                    )
                    if (
                        media_pricing_snapshot.get("version")
                        in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS
                    ):
                        # Image rows are only a component-level explanation of the
                        # request price.  The input tier is selected from the whole
                        # provider request, not independently per image.
                        media_pricing_snapshot["request_input_tokens"] = (
                            getattr(real_result, "media_request_input_tokens", None)
                            if getattr(real_result, "media_request_input_tokens", None) is not None
                            else real_result.input_tokens
                        )
                    settle_media_usage(
                        settlement_db,
                        invocation_id,
                        provider_usage=real_result.media_usage,
                        pricing_snapshot=media_pricing_snapshot,
                    )
                settled = settle_model_invocation(
                    settlement_db,
                    invocation_id=invocation_id,
                    request_correlation_id=real_result.request_correlation_id,
                    upstream_status_code=real_result.upstream_status_code,
                    input_tokens=real_result.input_tokens,
                    output_tokens=real_result.output_tokens,
                    evidence=invocation_evidence,
                    cached_input_tokens=(
                        real_result.cache_usage.cached_input_tokens
                    ),
                    cache_observation_status=real_result.cache_usage.status,
                    request_usages=getattr(real_result, "request_usages", None),
                )
                if settled.status != "succeeded":
                    settlement_db.commit()
                    ledger_terminal = True
                    raise FixtureExecutionError(
                        settled.error_code or "model_invocation_settlement_failed",
                        settled.error_message or "模型调用用量结算失败",
                        error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                        retryable=False,
                    )
                if semantic_validation_error is not None:
                    settlement_db.commit()
                    ledger_terminal = True
                    raise FixtureExecutionError(
                        "model_output_semantic_validation_failed",
                        "模型输出语义校验异常，已完成实际用量结算",
                        error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                        retryable=False,
                        evidence={
                            "error_type": type(semantic_validation_error).__name__,
                        },
                        failure_phase="post_settlement",
                    )
                if semantic_conflicts:
                    settlement_db.commit()
                    ledger_terminal = True
                    raise FixtureExecutionError(
                        "model_output_semantic_inconsistency",
                        "模型输出的决定性字段语义不一致",
                        error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                        retryable=True,
                        evidence={"reasons": list(semantic_conflicts)},
                        failure_phase="post_settlement",
                    )
                if complete_parent:
                    try:
                        # Roll back any partial parent/dispatch updates, while
                        # retaining the response, actual usage and media ledger.
                        with settlement_db.begin_nested():
                            complete_runtime_step(
                                settlement_db,
                                attempt_id,
                                lease_token,
                                real_result.node_result,
                            )
                    except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
                        settlement_db.commit()
                        ledger_terminal = True
                        raise FixtureExecutionError(
                            exc.code,
                            str(exc),
                            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                            retryable=False,
                            failure_phase="post_settlement",
                        ) from exc
                settlement_db.commit()
                ledger_terminal = True
            except Exception as settlement_error:
                settlement_db.rollback()
                if (
                    ledger_terminal
                    or transaction_attempt == 4
                    or not is_transient_database_transaction_error(settlement_error)
                ):
                    raise
            finally:
                settlement_db.close()
            if ledger_terminal:
                break
            logger.warning(
                "模型结果结算遇到数据库事务冲突，保留结果重试 "
                "invocation_id=%s retry=%s/3",
                invocation_id, transaction_attempt,
            )
            time.sleep(0.05 * (2 ** (transaction_attempt - 1)))
        return FixtureResult(
            output_manifest=real_result.node_result,
            output_hash=hash_manifest(real_result.node_result),
        )
    except FixtureExecutionError:
        raise
    except (DoubaoError, ProviderDeadlineExceeded) as exc:
        if getattr(exc, "outcome_unknown", False):
            from .batch_inference import timeout_retry_delay
            recovery_delay = timeout_retry_delay(
                enabled=transport_mode is None and os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_TIMEOUT_RETRY", "false") == "true",
                api_path=timeout_recovery_api_path,
                attempt_no=timeout_recovery_attempt_no,
                error_type=getattr(exc, "technical_error_type", None),
                complete_parent=complete_parent,
            )
            code = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            message = ("批量请求超时，原费用保留；冷却300秒后按冻结额度最多补跑一次"
                if recovery_delay is not None else
                "模型请求已发出但供应商结果无法确认，已禁止自动重试")
            if transport_mode is not None and getattr(exc, 'technical_error_type', None) in {'timeout', 'network', 'provider5xx'}:
                recovery_delay = 300
                message = "供应商结果未知，原费用保留；按冻结批量/在线次数与素材24小时截止恢复"
                if (transport_mode == 'batch' and wait_cap_applied is not None
                        and getattr(exc, 'technical_error_type', None) == 'timeout'):
                    # Local wait cap hit (ADR-0084): the provider may still be
                    # working and may bill; keep the liability, free the slot,
                    # and retry from checkpoint as a new physical request.
                    exc.wait_cap_hit = True
                    exc.wait_cap_seconds = wait_cap_applied
                    # 反复命中上限就拉开间隔（60→180→540→900），别把负载原样推回过载的入口。
                    from .batch_inference import wait_cap_resubmit_delay
                    recovery_delay = wait_cap_resubmit_delay(timeout_recovery_attempt_no)
                    message = (f"批量请求超过本地等待上限{int(wait_cap_applied)}秒仍无响应，"
                               f"记为结果未知并保留费用责任；释放槽位，{int(recovery_delay)}秒后按断点重新提交")
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=code,
                message=message,
                retryable=recovery_delay is not None,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=True,
            )
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                "model_batch_timeout_recovery" if recovery_delay is not None else code,
                message,
                error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                retryable=recovery_delay is not None,
                retry_after_seconds=recovery_delay,
            ) from exc
        code = f"model_provider_{exc.technical_error_type}"
        message = (
            "模型服务暂时不可用，可按工作流重试策略恢复"
            if exc.retryable
            else "模型服务拒绝了本次请求"
        )
        # 本地连接池排队超时：dispatch hook 已触发但请求从未发出，回退为未发出（rejected）。
        not_dispatched = getattr(exc, "provider_dispatched", True) is False
        _model_failure(
            invocation_id,
            code=code,
            message=message,
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            error=exc,
            provider_dispatched=not not_dispatched,
        )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            code,
            message,
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
        ) from exc
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        code = exc.code
        message = str(exc)
        retryable = exc.retryable
        if not ledger_terminal:
            if provider_dispatched:
                code = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
                message = (
                    "模型请求已返回但数据库结算失败，结果未知且禁止自动重试"
                )
                retryable = False
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=code,
                message=message,
                retryable=retryable,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=provider_dispatched,
            )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            code,
            message,
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
        ) from exc
    except ModelMediaUsageError as exc:
        if not ledger_terminal:
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=exc.code,
                message=str(exc),
                retryable=False,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=provider_dispatched,
            )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=False,
        ) from exc
    except WorkflowModelExecutorError as exc:
        if not ledger_terminal:
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=exc.code,
                message=str(exc),
                retryable=exc.retryable,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=provider_dispatched,
            )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            evidence=exc.evidence,
        ) from exc
    except SQLAlchemyOperationalError as exc:
        if provider_dispatched:
            code = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            message = "模型请求发出后数据库结算失败，结果未知且禁止自动重试"
            logger.warning(
                "真实模型节点在供应商调用后遇到数据库抖动：%s",
                type(exc).__name__,
            )
            if not ledger_terminal:
                _model_failure(
                    invocation_id,
                    code=code,
                    message=message,
                    retryable=False,
                    error=exc,
                    provider_dispatched=True,
                )
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                code,
                message,
                error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
                retryable=False,
            ) from exc
        # 预留/准备阶段明确尚未触达供应商，数据库锁超时可安全重试。
        code = INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE_CODE
        message = "数据库繁忙，本次未发出模型请求，稍后自动重试"
        logger.warning("真实模型节点遇到基础设施抖动：%s", type(exc).__name__)
        if not ledger_terminal:
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=code,
                message=message,
                retryable=True,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=False,
            )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            code,
            message,
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=True,
        ) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        code = (
            "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            if provider_dispatched
            else "model_execution_preflight_failed"
        )
        message = (
            "模型请求发出后发生未识别故障，结果未知且禁止自动重试"
            if provider_dispatched
            else "模型请求尚未发出，前置准备暂时失败，可按节点策略重试"
        )
        logger.exception("真实模型节点执行失败：%s", type(exc).__name__)
        if not ledger_terminal:
            _model_failure(
                invocation_id,
                code=code,
                message=message,
                retryable=not provider_dispatched,
                error=exc,
                provider_dispatched=provider_dispatched,
            )
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            code,
            message,
            error_policy_kind=policy_kind,
            retryable=not provider_dispatched,
        ) from exc


def _real_foreach_steps(
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    run_id: int,
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_client_type: Callable[[Any], Any],
    input_fingerprint: str,
    attempt_no: int,
    started_at: datetime | None,
) -> Generator[ProviderWait, Any, FixtureResult]:
    executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
    if executable is None or node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "foreach_item_executor_invalid",
            "foreach_image 缺少冻结提示词执行器",
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
        )

    def invoke(child: ForeachChildInvocation) -> Generator[ProviderWait, Any, Mapping[str, Any]]:
        try:
            result = yield from _real_prompt_steps(
                worker_id=worker_id,
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=executable,
                input_manifest=child.input_manifest,
                model_client_type=model_client_type,
                invocation_key=child.invocation_key,
                frozen_input_override=child.input_manifest,
                input_fingerprint=child.input_hash,
                invocation_attempt_no=child.attempt_no,
                complete_parent=False,
                media_evidence_context={
                    "parent_step_attempt_id": attempt_id,
                    "invocation_key": child.invocation_key,
                    "foreach_item_key": child.item_key,
                },
            )
        except FixtureExecutionError as exc:
            raise ForeachItemExecutionError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                retryable=bool(exc.retryable),
                retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
            ) from exc
        output = result.output_manifest.get("output")
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            raise ForeachItemExecutionError(
                "foreach_item_output_invalid",
                "foreach_image 子提示词没有返回结构化对象",
                retryable=False,
            )
        return output

    def heartbeat_parent() -> None:
        heartbeat_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            heartbeat_runtime_step(
                heartbeat_db,
                attempt_id,
                lease_token,
                worker_id,
            )
            heartbeat_db.commit()
        finally:
            heartbeat_db.close()

    try:
        output = yield from execute_foreach_parent_steps(
            SessionLocal,
            production_run_id=run_id,
            parent_step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            worker_id=worker_id,
            invoke_item=invoke,
            heartbeat_parent=heartbeat_parent,
        )
    except WorkflowForeachError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
            evidence=(
                {"foreach_output": exc.output_manifest}
                if exc.output_manifest
                else None
            ),
        ) from exc
    if output is None:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "foreach_children_still_active",
            "foreach_image 仍有其他 Worker 持有的子项租约",
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=True,
        )
    try:
        validate_native_node_output(node, output)
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
            evidence=getattr(exc, "evidence", None),
        ) from exc
    node_result = canonical_node_result(
        node=node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest,
        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint,
        output=output,
        attempt_no=attempt_no,
        started_at=started_at,
    )
    return FixtureResult(
        output_manifest=node_result,
        output_hash=hash_manifest(node_result),
    )


def _simulated_foreach_result(
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    run_id: int,
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_fingerprint: str,
    attempt_no: int,
    started_at: datetime | None,
) -> FixtureResult:
    executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
    if executable is None or node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "foreach_item_executor_invalid",
            "foreach_image 缺少冻结提示词执行器",
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
        )

    def invoke(child: ForeachChildInvocation) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
        try:
            envelope = execute_native_node(
                NodeExecutionContext(
                    run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                    node=executable,
                    input_manifest=child.input_manifest,
                    raw_fixture_output={"fixture": "noop"},
                    attempt_no=child.attempt_no,
                    started_at=started_at,
                    input_fingerprint=child.input_hash,
                )
            )
        except NodeExecutorError as exc:
            raise ForeachItemExecutionError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                retryable=False,
            ) from exc
        output = envelope.get("output")
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            raise ForeachItemExecutionError(
                "foreach_item_output_invalid",
                "foreach_image 模拟子提示词没有返回结构化对象",
                retryable=False,
            )
        return output

    def heartbeat_parent() -> None:
        heartbeat_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            heartbeat_runtime_step(
                heartbeat_db,
                attempt_id,
                lease_token,
                worker_id,
            )
            heartbeat_db.commit()
        finally:
            heartbeat_db.close()

    try:
        output = execute_foreach_parent(
            SessionLocal,
            production_run_id=run_id,
            parent_step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            worker_id=worker_id,
            invoke_item=invoke,
            heartbeat_parent=heartbeat_parent,
        )
    except WorkflowForeachError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
            evidence=(
                {"foreach_output": exc.output_manifest}
                if exc.output_manifest
                else None
            ),
        ) from exc
    if output is None:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            "foreach_children_still_active",
            "foreach_image 仍有其他 Worker 持有的模拟子项租约",
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=True,
        )
    try:
        validate_native_node_output(node, output)
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise FixtureExecutionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(node),
            retryable=False,
            evidence=getattr(exc, "evidence", None),
        ) from exc
    node_result = canonical_node_result(
        node=node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest,
        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint,
        output=output,
        attempt_no=attempt_no,
        started_at=started_at,
    )
    return FixtureResult(
        output_manifest=node_result,
        output_hash=hash_manifest(node_result),
    )


def _runtime_step_steps(
    worker_id: str,
    *,
    global_limit: int | None = None,
    model_client_type: Callable[[Any], Any] = DoubaoClient,
) -> Generator[ProviderWait, Any, bool]:
    claim_db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        resolved_limit = global_limit
        if resolved_limit is None:
            model = default_evaluation_model(claim_db)
            if model is not None:
                resolved_limit = incremental_global_concurrency_limit(
                    claim_db,
                    fallback_model=model,
                )
            else:
                scheduler_state = claim_db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1)
                resolved_limit = (
                    scheduler_state.global_limit
                    if scheduler_state is not None
                    else 1
                )
        attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
            claim_db,
            worker_id,
            global_limit=max(1, resolved_limit),
        )
        claim_db.commit()
        if attempt_id is None:
            return False
        claimed = claim_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
        if claimed is None or not claimed.lease_token:
            return False
        lease_token = claimed.lease_token
        input_manifest = json.loads(claimed.input_manifest_json)
        run = claim_db.get(ProductionRun, claimed.run_id)
        if run is None:
            return False
        run_snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
        script = claim_db.get(ScriptVersion, claimed.script_version_id)
        if script is None:
            return False
        script_manifest = json.loads(script.manifest_json)
        attempt_no = claimed.attempt_no
        input_hash = claimed.input_hash
        step_key = claimed.step_key
        step_type = claimed.step_type
        started_at = claimed.started_at
        model_execution_mode = run.model_execution_mode
        composer_node = _composer_v3_node(run_snapshot, step_key)
    finally:
        claim_db.close()

    try:
        runtime_context = run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
        from .incremental_routing import validate_run_route
        route_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            validate_run_route(route_db, run)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            raise FixtureExecutionError(exc.code, str(exc), error_policy_kind="manual_review", retryable=False) from exc
        except SQLAlchemyOperationalError as exc:
            if not is_transient_database_transaction_error(exc):
                raise
            # No node work or external request has started in this attempt.
            # Release the transaction before parking this same attempt; on
            # resume, ownership must be checked again, never assumed valid.
            route_db.rollback()
            raise FixtureExecutionError(
                ROUTE_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE,
                "路由核验遇到数据库锁等待，保留已完成步骤并稍后自动继续",
                error_policy_kind="retry", retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=5,
            ) from exc
        finally:
            route_db.close()
        if (
            isinstance(composer_node, Mapping)
            and composer_node.get("kind") == "image_first_seen_lookup"
            and isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
            and runtime_context.get("run_mode") == "incremental"
            and not _first_seen_wait_committed(run.id, step_key)
        ):
            wait_db = SessionLocal()
            try:
                begin_image_first_seen_wait(
                    wait_db,
                    attempt_id,
                    lease_token,
                )
                wait_db.commit()
            except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
                wait_db.rollback()
                raise FixtureExecutionError(
                    exc.code,
                    str(exc),
                    error_policy_kind=_node_error_policy(composer_node),
                    retryable=False,
                ) from exc
            finally:
                wait_db.close()
            return True
        if (
            isinstance(composer_node, Mapping)
            and composer_node.get("kind") == "aggregate_case"
        ):
            result = _aggregate_case_result(
                node=composer_node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                input_fingerprint=input_hash,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
                started_at=started_at,
            )
        elif (
            model_execution_mode == "real"
            and isinstance(composer_node, Mapping)
            and composer_node.get("kind") == "prompt"
        ):
            result = yield from _real_prompt_steps(
                worker_id=worker_id,
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=composer_node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=model_client_type,
            )
        elif (
            model_execution_mode == "real"
            and isinstance(composer_node, Mapping)
            and composer_node.get("kind") == "foreach_image"
        ):
            result = yield from _real_foreach_steps(
                worker_id=worker_id,
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_id=run.id,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=composer_node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=model_client_type,
                input_fingerprint=input_hash,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
                started_at=started_at,
            )
        elif (
            isinstance(composer_node, Mapping)
            and composer_node.get("kind") == "foreach_image"
        ):
            result = _simulated_foreach_result(
                worker_id=worker_id,
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_id=run.id,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=composer_node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                input_fingerprint=input_hash,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
                started_at=started_at,
            )
        else:
            fixture_kind = str(script_manifest.get("fixture", step_type))
            result = execute_fixture(
                fixture_kind,
                input_manifest,
                script_manifest,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
            )
            composer_output = _composer_v3_output(
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                step_key=step_key,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                input_hash=input_hash,
                raw_output=result.output_manifest,
                attempt_no=attempt_no,
                started_at=started_at,
            )
            if composer_output is not None:
                result = FixtureResult(
                    output_manifest=composer_output,
                    output_hash=hash_manifest(composer_output),
                )
    except FixtureExecutionError as exc:
        _report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            exc=exc,
        )
        return True

    try:
        _complete_runtime_step_with_retry(
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            output_manifest=result.output_manifest,
        )
    except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
        _report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=lease_token,
            exc=FixtureExecutionError(exc.code, str(exc), error_policy_kind="manual_review", retryable=False))
    return True


def _report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(
    *,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    exc: FixtureExecutionError,
    max_attempts: int = 4,
) -> None:
    """Persist one failed step, retrying the whole transaction on conflicts.

    与 ``_complete_runtime_step_with_retry`` 对称：失败回报同样会撞上 MySQL 死锁
    （fail_runtime_step 对 run/attempt/dispatch 逐个 FOR UPDATE，与并行 claim /
    回收器的加锁顺序可能交叉）。此前失败回报没有重试，死锁后整段回报丢失，
    步骤停在 running 直到租约到期，被回收器改写成 STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED、
    真实错误码与可重试语义一并被掩盖（137 上 run 5019 / prompt_single 实证）。
    每次重试必须用新 Session：MySQL 死锁后当前事务已被回滚。
    """

    for transaction_attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
        failure_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            from .batch_endpoint_recovery import DURABLE_WAIT_CODES
            durable_wait = exc.retryable and exc.code in DURABLE_WAIT_CODES
            if exc.evidence and not durable_wait:
                evidence_attempt = failure_db.get(
                    ProductionStepAttempt,
                    attempt_id,
                )
                if evidence_attempt is not None:
                    checkpoint = {
                        "schema_version": "runtime-checkpoint-v1",
                        "step_key": evidence_attempt.step_key,
                        "input_hash": evidence_attempt.input_hash,
                        "status": (
                            "failed_post_settlement"
                            if exc.failure_phase == "post_settlement"
                            else "failed_preflight"
                        ),
                        "failure": {
                            "code": exc.code,
                            "evidence": redact_workflow_secrets(
                                exc.evidence
                            ),
                        },
                    }
                    evidence_attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(
                        checkpoint
                    )
                    evidence_attempt.checkpoint_hash = hash_manifest(
                        checkpoint
                    )
            from .model_quota import LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES

            if exc.retryable and exc.code in LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES | DURABLE_WAIT_CODES | LOCAL_RUNTIME_WAIT_CODES:
                fail_runtime_step(failure_db, attempt_id, lease_token, exc.code,
                    str(exc), retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds)
                if durable_wait:
                    evidence_attempt = failure_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
                    checkpoint = json.loads(evidence_attempt.checkpoint_json or '{}')
                    checkpoint['_provider_resource_wait'] = {
                        'code': exc.code, **redact_workflow_secrets(exc.evidence)}
                    evidence_attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(checkpoint)
                    evidence_attempt.checkpoint_hash = hash_manifest(checkpoint)
            elif exc.error_policy_kind == "manual_review":
                block_runtime_step(
                    failure_db,
                    attempt_id,
                    lease_token,
                    exc.code,
                    str(exc),
                )
            else:
                failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
                    exc,
                    error_code=exc.code,
                    retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
                )
                attempt = failure_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
                retry_policy = (
                    runtime_step_retry_policy(failure_db, attempt)
                    if attempt is not None
                    else None
                )
                retry_available = bool(
                    failure.retryable
                    and attempt is not None
                    and retry_policy is not None
                    and attempt.attempt_no < retry_policy["max_attempts"]
                )
                if (
                    exc.error_policy_kind in {"continue", "skip"}
                    and not retry_available
                ):
                    skip_runtime_step_after_error(
                        failure_db,
                        attempt_id,
                        lease_token,
                        policy_kind=exc.error_policy_kind,
                        error_code=failure.error_code,
                        error_message=failure.error_message,
                    )
                else:
                    fail_runtime_step(
                        failure_db,
                        attempt_id,
                        lease_token,
                        failure.error_code,
                        failure.error_message,
                        retryable=failure.retryable,
                        retry_after_seconds=(
                            exc.retry_after_seconds
                            if exc.code == "model_batch_timeout_recovery" and failure.retryable
                            else failure.retry_after_seconds
                        ),
                    )
            failure_db.commit()
            return
        except Exception as report_exc:
            try:
                failure_db.rollback()
            except Exception:
                logger.warning(
                    "节点失败回报事务回滚失败 attempt_id=%s",
                    attempt_id,
                    exc_info=True,
                )
            if (
                transaction_attempt >= max_attempts
                or not is_transient_database_transaction_error(report_exc)
            ):
                raise
        finally:
            failure_db.close()
        delay = 0.05 * (2 ** (transaction_attempt - 1))
