        logger.warning(
            "节点失败回报遇到数据库事务冲突，使用新事务重试 "
            "attempt_id=%s code=%s retry=%s/%s delay=%.2fs",
            attempt_id,
            exc.code,
            transaction_attempt,
            max_attempts - 1,
            delay,
        )
        time.sleep(delay)


def _complete_runtime_step_with_retry(
    *,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    max_attempts: int = 4,
) -> None:
    """Persist one completed step, retrying the whole transaction on conflicts.

    MySQL aborts the active transaction after a deadlock.  Retrying only the
    nested SAVEPOINT leaves the leased step waiting for recovery, so every
    retry must use a fresh Session.  The external/fixture result is already in
    memory and is not executed again.
    """

    for transaction_attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
        complete_db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            complete_runtime_step(
                complete_db,
                attempt_id,
                lease_token,
                output_manifest,
            )
            complete_db.commit()
            return
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                complete_db.rollback()
            except Exception:
                logger.warning(
                    "节点完成事务回滚失败 attempt_id=%s",
                    attempt_id,
                    exc_info=True,
                )
            if (
                transaction_attempt >= max_attempts
                or not is_transient_database_transaction_error(exc)
            ):
                raise
        finally:
            complete_db.close()
        delay = 0.05 * (2 ** (transaction_attempt - 1))
        logger.warning(
            "节点完成遇到数据库事务冲突，使用新事务重试 "
            "attempt_id=%s retry=%s/%s delay=%.2fs",
            attempt_id,
            transaction_attempt,
            max_attempts - 1,
            delay,
        )
        time.sleep(delay)


def recover_runtime_once() -> int:
    from .native_baseline_regression import (
        reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents,
        reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children,
    )

    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        recovered = recover_expired_runtime_steps(db)
        reconciled = reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children(db)
        repaired_parents = reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents(db)
        db.commit()
        return recovered + reconciled + repaired_parents
    finally:
        db.close()


def _failure_raw_excerpt(error: BaseException) -> dict[str, str]:
    evidence = getattr(error, "evidence", None)
    if isinstance(evidence, dict):
        excerpt = evidence.get("raw_excerpt")
        if isinstance(excerpt, str) and excerpt.strip():
            return {"raw_excerpt": excerpt[:600]}
    return {}


def _failure_detail(error: BaseException, *, limit: int = 600) -> str:
    """把异常链的消息串起来（最多 4 层），供失败留证展示具体违反点。"""
    parts: list[str] = []
    current: BaseException | None = error
    depth = 0
    while current is not None and depth < 4:
        message = str(current).strip()
        if message and message not in parts:
            parts.append(message)
        current = current.__cause__  # 只跟显式 raise ... from，避免隐式上下文带入无关异常
        depth += 1
    return " <- ".join(parts)[:limit]


def _real_prompt_result(**kwargs):
    return drive_sync(_real_prompt_steps(**kwargs))


def _real_foreach_result(**kwargs):
    return drive_sync(_real_foreach_steps(**kwargs))


def process_runtime_step_once(worker_id, *, global_limit=None, model_client_type=DoubaoClient):
    return drive_sync(_runtime_step_steps(
        worker_id, global_limit=global_limit, model_client_type=model_client_type,
    ))


async def process_runtime_step_async(worker_id, *, runtime: BoundedRuntime,
                                     global_limit=None, model_client_type=DoubaoClient):
    return await runtime.run(_runtime_step_steps(
        worker_id, global_limit=global_limit, model_client_type=model_client_type,
    ))

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_foreach_executor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import re
import time
import uuid
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import or_, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .model_quota import LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES
from .batch_endpoint_recovery import DURABLE_WAIT_CODES, is_resource_rejection
from .models import WorkflowForeachItem, WorkflowModelInvocation


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

#: 子项结果落库遇到锁等待/死锁/断线时的重试次数与间隔（每次新会话）。
_SETTLEMENT_ATTEMPTS = 4
_SETTLEMENT_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS = 0.2

_SAFE_DOT_PATH = re.compile(
    r"^\$\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*)*$"
)
_SAFE_INVOCATION_SEGMENT = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$")
_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset({"succeeded", "failed", "skipped"})


class WorkflowForeachError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        retryable: bool = False,
        output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds
        self.output_manifest = deepcopy(dict(output_manifest or {}))


class ForeachItemExecutionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        retryable: bool,
        retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ForeachChildInvocation:
    item_id: int
    item_key: str
    ordinal: int
    attempt_no: int
    invocation_key: str
    lease_token: str
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
    input_hash: str


def _hash(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _mapping(value: Any, *, code: str, message: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(code, message)
    return value


def _config(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_node_invalid",
            "当前节点不是 foreach_image 节点",
        )
    return _mapping(
        node.get("config"),
        code="foreach_config_invalid",
        message="foreach_image 节点配置损坏",
    )


def _resolve_dot_path(value: Any, path: Any, *, code: str) -> Any:
    if not isinstance(path, str) or not _SAFE_DOT_PATH.fullmatch(path):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(code, "foreach_image 只允许安全对象字段路径")
    current = value
    for key in path[2:].split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or key not in current:
            raise WorkflowForeachError(code, f"foreach_image 路径不存在：{path}")
        current = current[key]
    return current


def _item_key(item: Mapping[str, Any], identity_path: Any) -> str:
    raw = _resolve_dot_path(
        item,
        identity_path,
        code="foreach_item_identity_missing",
    )
    if isinstance(raw, bool) or not isinstance(raw, (str, int)):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_item_identity_invalid",
            "foreach_image 子项身份必须是非空字符串或整数",
        )
    key = str(raw)
    if (
        not key
        or key != key.strip()
        or len(key) > 240
        or any(ord(character) < 32 for character in key)
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_item_identity_invalid",
            "foreach_image 子项身份必须是 1 到 240 位的安全文本",
        )
    return key


def _max_attempts(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    policy = node.get("retry_policy")
    value = policy.get("max_attempts") if isinstance(policy, Mapping) else 1
    if (
        isinstance(value, bool)
        or not isinstance(value, int)
        or not 1 <= value <= 100
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_retry_policy_invalid",
            "foreach_image 子项重试上限必须是 1 到 100",
        )
    return value


def _desired_items(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, int, dict[str, Any], str]], str, str]:
    config = _config(node)
    source = _resolve_dot_path(
        input_manifest,
        config.get("items_path"),
        code="foreach_items_path_invalid",
    )
    if not isinstance(source, list):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_items_not_array",
            "foreach_image 来源必须是数组",
        )
    max_items = config.get("max_items")
    if (
        isinstance(max_items, bool)
        or not isinstance(max_items, int)
        or not 1 <= max_items <= 10000
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_max_items_invalid",
            "foreach_image 最大子项数必须是 1 到 10000",
        )
    if len(source) > max_items:
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_max_items_exceeded",
            f"foreach_image 收到 {len(source)} 个子项，超过冻结上限 {max_items}",
        )
    desired: list[tuple[str, int, dict[str, Any], str]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for ordinal, raw_item in enumerate(source):
        item = _mapping(
            raw_item,
            code="foreach_item_invalid",
            message="foreach_image 的每个子项都必须是对象",
        )
        key = _item_key(item, config.get("item_identity_path"))
        if key in seen:
            raise WorkflowForeachError(
                "foreach_item_key_duplicate",
                f"foreach_image 子项身份重复：{key}",
            )
        seen.add(key)
        frozen = deepcopy(dict(item))
        desired.append((key, ordinal, frozen, _hash(frozen)))
    executor = _mapping(
        config.get("item_executor"),
        code="foreach_item_executor_invalid",
        message="foreach_image 缺少冻结子执行器",
    )
    return desired, _hash(source), _hash(executor)


def _invocation_evidence(invocation: WorkflowModelInvocation) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(invocation.evidence_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _reconcile_items_from_invocations(
    db: Session,
    rows: Sequence[WorkflowForeachItem],
    *,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    """Close the provider-success/child-settlement crash window from ledger facts."""

    for row in rows:
        if row.status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES or row.attempt_count < 1:
            continue
        key = _invocation_key(row.item_key, row.attempt_count)
        from .batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
        transport_prefix = invocation_prefix(key)
        invocation = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == row.production_run_id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == row.parent_node_instance_id,
                or_(
                    (WorkflowModelInvocation.step_attempt_id == row.parent_step_attempt_id)
                    & (WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key == key),
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key.startswith(transport_prefix),
                ),
            ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(1)
        )
        if invocation is None:
            continue
        evidence = _invocation_evidence(invocation)
        if invocation.status == "succeeded":
            semantic = evidence.get('semantic_validation')
            if isinstance(semantic, Mapping) and semantic.get('status') in {'rejected', 'error'}:
                # Paid success is not necessarily an accepted business result.
                # The transport recovery controller owns the remaining retries.
                continue
            result_evidence = evidence.get("result")
            output = (
                result_evidence.get("parsed_output")
                if isinstance(result_evidence, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            if isinstance(output, Mapping):
                frozen = deepcopy(dict(output))
                row.status = "succeeded"
                row.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(frozen)
                row.output_hash = _hash(frozen)
                row.finished_at = invocation.finished_at or now
                row.last_error_code = ""
                row.last_error_message = ""
            else:
                row.status = "failed"
                row.finished_at = now
                row.last_error_code = "foreach_invocation_evidence_missing"
                row.last_error_message = "已完成模型调用缺少可恢复结构化输出"
        elif invocation.status in {"failed", "rejected"}:
            failure_evidence = evidence.get("failure")
            retryable = (
                failure_evidence.get("retryable") is True
                if isinstance(failure_evidence, Mapping)
                else False
            )
            resource_wait = is_resource_rejection(invocation)
            if resource_wait and row.status == 'running':
                expiry = _aware(row.lease_expires_at)
                if expiry is not None and expiry > now:
                    # A previous 429 cannot steal a fresh owner's child while
                    # it prepares the next distinct provider request.
                    continue
            # Reconcile a crash after the HTTP settlement but before parking
            # the child. Already parked children must not decrement twice.
            if resource_wait and row.status == 'running':
                row.attempt_count = max(0, row.attempt_count - 1)
            row.status = (
                "retryable"
                if resource_wait or (retryable and row.attempt_count < row.max_attempts)
                else "failed"
            )
            row.finished_at = now if row.status == "failed" else None
            row.last_error_code = (
                invocation.error_code or "foreach_item_invocation_failed"
            )
            row.last_error_message = (
                invocation.error_message or "foreach_image 子模型调用失败"
            )
        elif invocation.status == "reserved":
            expiry = _aware(row.lease_expires_at)
            if expiry is not None and expiry <= now:
                row.status = "failed"
                row.finished_at = now
                row.last_error_code = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
                row.last_error_message = "模型调用结果未知，禁止自动重试"
        if row.status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES or row.status == "retryable":
            row.lease_owner = None
            row.lease_token = None
            row.lease_expires_at = None
    db.flush()


def materialize_foreach_items(
    db: Session,
    *,
    production_run_id: int,
    parent_step_attempt_id: int,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[WorkflowForeachItem]:
    """Freeze one ordered cohort without truncation or ordinal identities."""

    desired, _source_hash, _contract_hash = _desired_items(node, input_manifest)
    node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if not isinstance(node_id, str) or not node_id:
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_node_identity_invalid",
            "foreach_image 缺少节点身份",
        )
    current = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
            == parent_step_attempt_id
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal, WorkflowForeachItem.id)
    ).all()
    _reconcile_items_from_invocations(
        db,
        current,
        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    if current:
        expected = [
            (key, ordinal, input_hash)
            for key, ordinal, _item, input_hash in desired
        ]
        actual = [
            (item.item_key, item.ordinal, item.input_hash) for item in current
        ]
        if (
            actual != expected
            or any(item.production_run_id != production_run_id for item in current)
            or any(item.parent_node_instance_id != node_id for item in current)
        ):
            raise WorkflowForeachError(
                "foreach_materialization_conflict",
                "当前父步骤已冻结不同的 foreach_image 子项集合",
            )
        return list(current)

    previous = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.production_run_id == production_run_id,
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_node_instance_id == node_id,
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
            != parent_step_attempt_id,
        )
        .order_by(
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id,
            WorkflowForeachItem.id,
        )
    ).all()
    _reconcile_items_from_invocations(
        db,
        previous,
        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    reusable: dict[tuple[str, str], WorkflowForeachItem] = {}
    for item in previous:
        if item.status == "succeeded":
            reusable[(item.item_key, item.input_hash)] = item

    max_attempts = _max_attempts(node)
    created: list[WorkflowForeachItem] = []
    for key, ordinal, frozen, input_hash in desired:
        prior = reusable.get((key, input_hash))
        row = WorkflowForeachItem(
            production_run_id=production_run_id,
            parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
            parent_node_instance_id=node_id,
            item_key=key,
            ordinal=ordinal,
            status="succeeded" if prior is not None else "pending",
            attempt_count=0,
            max_attempts=max_attempts,
            input_manifest_json=canonical_json(frozen),
            input_hash=input_hash,
            output_manifest_json=(
                prior.output_manifest_json if prior is not None else "{}"
            ),
            output_hash=prior.output_hash if prior is not None else None,
            finished_at=prior.finished_at if prior is not None else None,
        )
        db.add(row)
        created.append(row)
    db.flush()
    return created


def _aware(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None or value.tzinfo is not None:
        return value
    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _invocation_key(item_key: str, attempt_count: int) -> str:
    segment = item_key
    if (
        _SAFE_INVOCATION_SEGMENT.fullmatch(segment) is None
        or len(f"item:{segment}:attempt:{attempt_count}") > 240
    ):
        segment = "sha256-" + hashlib.sha256(
            item_key.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
    return f"item:{segment}:attempt:{attempt_count}"


def _claim_child(
    db: Session,
    *,
    parent_step_attempt_id: int,
    worker_id: str,
    lease_seconds: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> ForeachChildInvocation | None:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
            == parent_step_attempt_id
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal, WorkflowForeachItem.id)
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    _reconcile_items_from_invocations(db, rows, now=now)
    for item in rows:
        expiry = _aware(item.lease_expires_at)
        if (
            item.status in {"leased", "running"}
            and expiry is not None
            and expiry <= now
        ):
            item.status = (
                "retryable"
                if item.attempt_count < item.max_attempts
                else "failed"
            )
            item.last_error_code = "FOREACH_ITEM_LEASE_EXPIRED"
            item.last_error_message = "foreach_image 子项租约已过期"
            item.lease_owner = None
            item.lease_token = None
            item.lease_expires_at = None
    candidate = next(
        (item for item in rows if item.status in {"pending", "retryable"}),
        None,
    )
    if candidate is None:
        return None
    candidate.attempt_count += 1
    token = uuid.uuid4().hex
    candidate.status = "running"
    candidate.lease_owner = worker_id
    candidate.lease_token = token
    candidate.lease_expires_at = now + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds)
    candidate.heartbeat_at = now
    if candidate.started_at is None:
        candidate.started_at = now
    candidate.last_error_code = ""
    candidate.last_error_message = ""
    db.flush()
    return ForeachChildInvocation(
        item_id=candidate.id,
        item_key=candidate.item_key,
        ordinal=candidate.ordinal,
        attempt_no=candidate.attempt_count,
        invocation_key=_invocation_key(
            candidate.item_key,
            candidate.attempt_count,
        ),
        lease_token=token,
        input_manifest=deepcopy(
            json.loads(candidate.input_manifest_json)
        ),
        input_hash=candidate.input_hash,
    )


def _settle_child_success(
    db: Session,
    child: ForeachChildInvocation,
    output: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(WorkflowForeachItem.id == child.item_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    frozen = deepcopy(dict(output))
    if (
        row is not None
        and row.status == "succeeded"
        and row.output_hash == _hash(frozen)
        and row.lease_token is None
    ):
        # A retried settlement whose previous commit did reach the database
        # (e.g. the connection dropped after COMMIT): already settled.
        return
    if (
        row is None
        or row.status != "running"
        or row.lease_token != child.lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_item_lease_stale",
            "foreach_image 子项租约已失效，拒绝接收旧结果",
        )
    row.status = "succeeded"
    row.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(frozen)
    row.output_hash = _hash(frozen)
    row.finished_at = now
    row.heartbeat_at = now
    row.lease_owner = None
    row.lease_token = None
    row.lease_expires_at = None
    row.last_error_code = ""
    row.last_error_message = ""
    db.flush()


def _settle_child_failure(
    db: Session,
    child: ForeachChildInvocation,
    error: ForeachItemExecutionError,
    *,
    now: datetime,
) -> bool:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(WorkflowForeachItem.id == child.item_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if (
        row is None
        or row.status != "running"
        or row.lease_token != child.lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_item_lease_stale",
            "foreach_image 子项租约已失效，拒绝接收旧失败结果",
        )
    local_wait = error.retryable and error.code in LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES | DURABLE_WAIT_CODES
    if local_wait:
        row.attempt_count = max(0, row.attempt_count - 1)
    terminal = not local_wait and (not error.retryable or row.attempt_count >= row.max_attempts)
    row.status = "failed" if terminal else "retryable"
    row.finished_at = now if terminal else None
    row.heartbeat_at = now
    row.lease_owner = None
    row.lease_token = None
    row.lease_expires_at = None
    row.last_error_code = error.code
    row.last_error_message = str(error)
    db.flush()
    return terminal


def _skip_remaining(
    db: Session,
    *,
    parent_step_attempt_id: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
            == parent_step_attempt_id,
            WorkflowForeachItem.status.in_(("pending", "retryable")),
        )
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    for row in rows:
        row.status = "skipped"
        row.finished_at = now
        row.last_error_code = "FOREACH_FAIL_CLOSED"
        row.last_error_message = "前序子项失败，按 fail_closed 策略停止"
    db.flush()


def _parent_output(
    db: Session,
    *,
    parent_step_attempt_id: int,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowForeachItem)
        .where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
            == parent_step_attempt_id
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal, WorkflowForeachItem.id)
    ).all()
    if any(row.status not in _TERMINAL_STATUSES for row in rows):
        return None
    _desired, source_hash, contract_hash = _desired_items(node, input_manifest)
    projected = []
    for row in rows:
        item: dict[str, Any] = {
            "item_key": row.item_key,
            "ordinal": row.ordinal,
            "status": row.status,
            "attempt_count": row.attempt_count,
            "input_hash": row.input_hash,
        }
        if row.status == "succeeded":
            item["output_hash"] = row.output_hash
            item["output"] = json.loads(row.output_manifest_json)
        else:
            item["error"] = {
                "code": row.last_error_code,
                "message": row.last_error_message,
            }
        projected.append(item)
    counts = {
        "total": len(rows),
        "succeeded": sum(row.status == "succeeded" for row in rows),
        "failed": sum(row.status == "failed" for row in rows),
        "skipped": sum(row.status == "skipped" for row in rows),
    }
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-foreach-output-v1",
        "node_instance_id": node.get("node_instance_id"),
        "source_hash": source_hash,
        "item_contract_hash": contract_hash,
        "counts": counts,
        "items": projected,
    }


def _settle_child_success_with_retry(
    session_factory: Callable[[], Session],
    child: ForeachChildInvocation,
    raw_output: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    """Persist a known child result; transient DB errors never make it unknown.

    A lock wait timeout, deadlock or dropped connection rolls back only the
    settlement transaction; the provider result is still in hand (and in the
    invocation ledger). Retry with a fresh session; if the database keeps
    refusing, leave the child running (non-terminal) and park the parent as a
    local runtime wait so ledger reconciliation settles it on resume.
    """
    from .runtime_queue import FOREACH_SETTLEMENT_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE

    for attempt in range(1, _SETTLEMENT_ATTEMPTS + 1):
        try:
            with session_factory() as db:
                _settle_child_success(
                    db,
                    child,
                    raw_output,
                    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                )
                db.commit()
            return
        except OperationalError as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "foreach child settlement transient database error "
                "item=%s attempt=%s/%s error=%s",
                child.item_key, attempt, _SETTLEMENT_ATTEMPTS,
                type(exc.orig or exc).__name__,
            )
            if attempt == _SETTLEMENT_ATTEMPTS:
                raise WorkflowForeachError(
                    FOREACH_SETTLEMENT_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE,
                    f"foreach_image 子项 {child.item_key} 结果落库遇到数据库临时错误，稍后对账补齐",
                    retryable=True,
                    retry_after_seconds=5,
                ) from exc
            if _SETTLEMENT_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS:
                time.sleep(_SETTLEMENT_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS * attempt)


def execute_foreach_parent_steps(
    session_factory: Callable[[], Session],
    *,
    production_run_id: int,
    parent_step_attempt_id: int,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    worker_id: str,
    invoke_item: Callable[[ForeachChildInvocation], Mapping[str, Any]],
    heartbeat_parent: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    lease_seconds: int = 300,
    max_claims: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Execute resumable child calls and return output only when all are terminal."""

    if max_claims is not None and (
        isinstance(max_claims, bool)
        or not isinstance(max_claims, int)
        or max_claims < 1
    ):
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_claim_limit_invalid",
            "foreach_image 本轮处理上限必须为正整数",
        )
    config = _config(node)
    failure_policy = config.get("failure_policy")
    if failure_policy not in {"fail_open", "fail_closed"}:
        raise WorkflowForeachError(
            "foreach_failure_policy_invalid",
            "foreach_image 失败策略不合法",
        )

    with session_factory() as db:
        materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=production_run_id,
            parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
        )
        db.commit()

    claims = 0
    while max_claims is None or claims < max_claims:
        with session_factory() as db:
            child = _claim_child(
                db,
                parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
                worker_id=worker_id,
                lease_seconds=lease_seconds,
                now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
            db.commit()
        if child is None:
            break
        claims += 1
        if heartbeat_parent is not None:
            heartbeat_parent()
        try:
            raw_output = yield from invoke_item(child)
            if not isinstance(raw_output, Mapping):
                raise ForeachItemExecutionError(
                    "foreach_item_output_invalid",
                    "foreach_image 子执行器必须返回对象",
                    retryable=False,
                )
            _settle_child_success_with_retry(session_factory, child, raw_output)
        except ForeachItemExecutionError as exc:
            with session_factory() as db:
                terminal = _settle_child_failure(
                    db,
                    child,
                    exc,
                    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                )
                if terminal and failure_policy == "fail_closed":
                    _skip_remaining(
                        db,
                        parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
                        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                    )
                db.commit()
            if exc.retryable and exc.code in LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES | DURABLE_WAIT_CODES:
                raise WorkflowForeachError(exc.code, str(exc), retryable=True,
                    retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds) from exc
            if terminal and failure_policy == "fail_closed":
                with session_factory() as db:
                    output = _parent_output(
                        db,
                        parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
                        node=node,
                        input_manifest=input_manifest,
                    )
                raise WorkflowForeachError(
                    "foreach_child_failed",
                    f"foreach_image 子项 {child.item_key} 达到失败上限",
                    output_manifest=output,
                ) from exc
        except WorkflowForeachError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            unknown = ForeachItemExecutionError(
                "foreach_item_outcome_unknown",
                "foreach_image 子项执行结果未知，禁止自动重试",
                retryable=False,
            )
            with session_factory() as db:
                _settle_child_failure(
                    db,
                    child,
                    unknown,
                    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                )
                if failure_policy == "fail_closed":
                    _skip_remaining(
                        db,
                        parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
                        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                    )
                db.commit()
            raise WorkflowForeachError(
                unknown.code,
                str(unknown),
            ) from exc

    with session_factory() as db:
        return _parent_output(
            db,
            parent_step_attempt_id=parent_step_attempt_id,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
        )


def execute_foreach_parent(session_factory, *, invoke_item, **kwargs):
    from .bounded_runtime import drive_sync

    def invoke_steps(child):
        # Preserve the public synchronous callback API without duplicating the
        # durable child claiming, idempotency, or settlement implementation.
        yield from ()
        return invoke_item(child)

    return drive_sync(execute_foreach_parent_steps(
        session_factory, invoke_item=invoke_steps, **kwargs,
    ))

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_manager_seed.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from copy import deepcopy

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .evaluation_components import create_component_asset
from .models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_assets import bind_prompt_version
from .workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition, create_workflow_version
from .evaluation_mechanism_migration import migrate_category_revision_history
from .vision_input_policy import (
    default_vision_input_policy,
    parse_vision_capability_profile,
)


_NATIVE_LEVEL_CATEGORIES = frozenset({"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"})


def _new_prompt_vision_policy(model: ModelRegistryEntry | None) -> dict:
    """Seed every newly composed image prompt with explicit high/high policy."""

    if model is None:
        return default_vision_input_policy().as_dict()
    try:
        raw_profile = json.loads(model.vision_capability_profile_json or "{}")
        capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            raw_profile,
            provider=model.provider,
            model_id=model.model_id,
        )
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return default_vision_input_policy().as_dict()
    return default_vision_input_policy(capability).as_dict()


def _prompt_working_copy_content(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "stage": prompt.stage,
        "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
    }


def _preferred_profile_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    stage: str,
    pointer_id: int | None,
) -> PromptVersion | None:
    pointed = db.get(PromptVersion, pointer_id) if pointer_id is not None else None
    if (
        pointed is not None
        and pointed.category_key == profile.category_key
        and pointed.stage == stage
        and pointed.status != "archived"
    ):
        return pointed
    published = db.scalar(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == profile.category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == stage,
            PromptVersion.status == "published",
        )
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc())
    )
    if published is not None:
        return published
    return db.scalar(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == profile.category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == stage,
            PromptVersion.status != "archived",
        )
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc())
    )


def _ensure_profile_prompt_component(
    db: Session,
    *,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    prompt: PromptVersion | None,
    asset_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> None:
    """Backfill legacy A/B prompts into the operator-managed component catalog."""

    if prompt is None:
        return
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == profile.category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )
    if asset is None:
        asset = create_component_asset(
            db,
            category_key=profile.category_key,
            asset_key=asset_key,
            kind="prompt",
            display_name=f"提示词 {prompt.stage}",
            prompt_stage=prompt.stage,
            description="由存量类目提示词幂等导入，可继续生成独立版本。",
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if asset.kind != "prompt":
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"类目 {profile.category_key} 的节点 {asset_key} 不是提示词组件"
        )

    working_copy = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
        )
    )
    if working_copy is None:
        working_copy = EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            content_json=canonical_json(_prompt_working_copy_content(prompt)),
            editor_schema_version="prompt-component-editor-v1",
            lock_version=1,
            updated_by=actor,
            base_prompt_version_id=prompt.id,
        )
        db.add(working_copy)
        db.flush()
    else:
        try:
            working_content = json.loads(working_copy.content_json)
        except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            working_content = {}
        if working_copy.base_prompt_version_id is None and not {
            "system_prompt",
            "rubric_version",
        }.issubset(working_content):
            working_copy.content_json = canonical_json(
                _prompt_working_copy_content(prompt)
            )
            working_copy.base_prompt_version_id = prompt.id
            working_copy.updated_by = actor

    historical_versions = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == profile.category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == prompt.stage,
            PromptVersion.status != "archived",
        )
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id)
    ).all()
    for version in historical_versions:
        bind_prompt_version(
            db,
            asset=asset,
            prompt_version=version,
            created_by=actor,
        )


def _model_for_profile(
    db: Session,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> ModelRegistryEntry | None:
    query = select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(
        ModelRegistryEntry.active.is_(True),
        ModelRegistryEntry.role == "main",
    )
    if profile.model_config_id is not None:
        matched = db.scalar(
            query.where(
                ModelRegistryEntry.source_model_config_id == profile.model_config_id
            )
        )
        if matched is not None:
            return matched
    existing = db.scalar(query.order_by(ModelRegistryEntry.id.asc()))
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    source = (
        db.get(ModelConfig, profile.model_config_id)
        if profile.model_config_id is not None
        else db.scalar(
            select(ModelConfig)
            .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
            .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
        )
    )
    if source is None or not source.active:
        return None
    entry = ModelRegistryEntry(
        role="main",
        name=source.name,
        provider=source.provider,
        protocol=source.protocol,
        capabilities_json=source.capabilities_json,
        description=source.description,
        base_url=source.base_url,
        api_path=source.api_path,
        model_id=source.model_id,
        encrypted_api_key=None,
        temperature=source.temperature,
        max_tokens=source.max_tokens,
        timeout_seconds=source.timeout_seconds,
        max_retries=source.max_retries,
        max_concurrency=source.max_concurrency,
        max_input_tokens=source.max_input_tokens,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=source.input_micros_per_million_tokens,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=source.output_micros_per_million_tokens,
        thinking_mode=source.thinking_mode or "auto",
        structured_output=source.structured_output,
        active=True,
        source_model_config_id=source.id,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(entry)
    db.flush()
    return entry


def _node(
    key: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    config: dict,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "key": key,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "config": config,
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "trace_id"],
    }


def _workflow_manifest(
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    *,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion | None,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion | None,
    rule: CategoryEvaluationV3Revision | None,
    model: ModelRegistryEntry | None,
) -> dict:
    category_key = profile.category_key
    model_id = model.id if model is not None else 0
    max_tokens = max(128, min(model.max_tokens, 65536)) if model is not None else 4096
    thinking_level = "none"
    vision_input_policy = _new_prompt_vision_policy(model)
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取上游数据",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.input"},
        )
    ]
    if prompt_a is not None:
        nodes.append(
            _node(
                "prompt_a",
                "prompt",
                "提示词 A",
                {
                    "asset_key": "prompt_a",
                    "prompt_ref": f"prompt:{prompt_a.stage}@{prompt_a.version}",
                    "model_registry_id": model_id,
                    "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                    "thinking_level": thinking_level,
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "vision_input_policy": deepcopy(vision_input_policy),
                },
            )
        )
    if rule is not None:
        nodes.append(
            _node(
                "rule_contract",
                "rule_contract",
                "规则与红线判断",
                {
                    "asset_key": "rule_contract",
                    "contract_ref": f"rule:{category_key}@r{rule.revision}",
                    "rule_revision_id": rule.id,
                },
            )
        )
    if prompt_b is not None:
        nodes.append(
            _node(
                "prompt_b",
                "prompt",
                "提示词 B",
                {
                    "asset_key": "prompt_b",
                    "prompt_ref": f"prompt:{prompt_b.stage}@{prompt_b.version}",
                    "model_registry_id": model_id,
                    "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                    "thinking_level": thinking_level,
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "vision_input_policy": deepcopy(vision_input_policy),
                },
            )
        )
    if rule is not None:
        nodes.append(
            _node(
                "level_matcher",
                "level_matcher",
                "等级撮合",
                {
                    "asset_key": "level_matcher",
                    "matcher_ref": f"matcher:{category_key}@r{rule.revision}",
                    "rule_revision_id": rule.id,
                },
            )
        )
    nodes.append(
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "写入下游",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.output"},
        )
    )
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "evaluation",
        "run_modes": [
            "labeling",
            "evaluation",
            "prompt_regression",
            "stock_regression",
        ],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": source["key"], "to": target["key"]}
            for source, target in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
    }


def _blocked_version(
    db: Session,
    definition: WorkflowDefinition,
    manifest: dict,
    *,
    errors: list[dict[str, str]],
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    encoded = canonical_json(manifest)
    row = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="imported-1",
        status="blocked",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v2",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["nodes"]),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["edges"]),
        input_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["input_schema"]),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["output_schema"]),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                **manifest["resource_policy"],
                "queue_class": manifest["queue_class"],
                "category_key": manifest["category_key"],
                "lane_key": manifest["lane_key"],
                "run_modes": manifest["run_modes"],
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=hashlib.sha256(encoded.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
        validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": False, "errors": errors}),
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def seed_workflow_manager(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str = "system:workflow-manager-import",
) -> None:
    from .knowledge_graph_category_seed import ensure_knowledge_graph_category
    from .native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    ensure_knowledge_graph_category(db, actor=actor)
    profiles = db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile)
        .where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == "active")
        .order_by(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key)
    ).all()
    for profile in profiles:
        if profile.category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            if _model_for_profile(db, profile, actor=actor) is None:
                continue
            ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            continue
        prompt_a = _preferred_profile_prompt(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            stage="A",
            pointer_id=profile.prompt_a_id,
        )
        prompt_b = _preferred_profile_prompt(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            stage="B",
            pointer_id=profile.prompt_b_id,
        )
        _ensure_profile_prompt_component(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            prompt=prompt_a,
            asset_key="prompt_a",
            actor=actor,
        )
        _ensure_profile_prompt_component(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            prompt=prompt_b,
            asset_key="prompt_b",
            actor=actor,
        )
        workflow_key = f"{profile.category_key}.evaluation"
        if db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowDefinition.id).where(
                WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key
            )
        ) is not None:
            if db.scalar(
                select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id).where(
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key
                    == profile.category_key
                )
            ) is not None:
                if profile.category_key in _NATIVE_LEVEL_CATEGORIES:
                    model = _model_for_profile(db, profile, actor=actor)
                    if prompt_a is not None and prompt_b is not None and model is not None:
                        from .native_workflow_migration import (
                            ensure_native_category_workflows,
                        )

                        ensure_native_category_workflows(
                            db, profile.category_key, actor
                        )
                    else:
                        migrate_category_revision_history(
                            db, profile.category_key, actor=actor
                        )
                else:
                    migrate_category_revision_history(
                        db, profile.category_key, actor=actor
                    )
            continue
        rule = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == profile.category_key,
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
            )
        )
        model = _model_for_profile(db, profile, actor=actor)
        definition = create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            name=f"{profile.display_name}评测工作流",
            description="从现有提示词与规则机制幂等导入，可在工作流管理器中追加新版本。",
            owner="operations",
            allowed_categories=[profile.category_key],
            created_by=actor,
        )
        manifest = _workflow_manifest(
            profile,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            rule=rule,
            model=model,
        )
        errors: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        if prompt_a is None:
            errors.append(
                {
                    "path": "nodes.prompt_a",
                    "code": "workflow_import_prompt_a_missing",
                    "message": "类目缺少现役提示词 A，请补充后创建新工作流版本",
                }
            )
        if model is None:
            errors.append(
                {
                    "path": "nodes.prompt",
                    "code": "workflow_import_model_missing",
                    "message": "类目缺少可用主模型，请配置后创建新工作流版本",
                }
            )
        if rule is None:
            errors.append(
                {
                    "path": "nodes.rule_contract",
                    "code": "workflow_import_rule_missing",
                    "message": "类目缺少现役规则合同，请补充后创建新工作流版本",
                }
            )
        if errors:
            _blocked_version(
                db,
                definition,
                manifest,
                errors=errors,
                actor=actor,
            )
            if rule is not None:
                migrate_category_revision_history(
                    db, profile.category_key, actor=actor
                )
            continue
        version = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version="imported-1",
            manifest=manifest,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        version.status = "active"
        version.validation_report_json = canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []})
        if rule is not None:
            if profile.category_key in _NATIVE_LEVEL_CATEGORIES:
                from .native_workflow_migration import (
                    ensure_native_category_workflows,
                )

                ensure_native_category_workflows(
                    db, profile.category_key, actor
                )
            else:
                migrate_category_revision_history(
                    db, profile.category_key, actor=actor
                )
    db.flush()

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_model_budget.py =====

def _env_budget(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    """读取环境变量覆盖的安全上限；非法或非正值一律回落默认值。

    做成可覆盖是为了让上限能在不改代码、不重新部署的前提下临时调整——
    这四个值是防误操作的安全阀，不是业务配额。
    """
    raw = os.getenv(name)
    if raw is None or not raw.strip():
        return default
    try:
        value = int(raw.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return default
    return value if value > 0 else default


def _env_budget_at_most(name: str, hard_cap: int) -> int:
    """Allow operators to lower a safety cap, never raise it at runtime."""

    return min(_env_budget(name, hard_cap), hard_cap)


# 真实模型评测（存量回归）的安全上限。运营的最大一轮是 3000 条素材，每条走
# A+B 两次调用 = 6000 次请求；实测单次调用输入约 1.5 万 token、输出约 1400
# token、成本约 0.034 元（doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215），一轮 3000 条约 200 元。
# 默认值在此之上留约 2-3 倍余量，既跑得下最大一轮，也让误操作有天花板。
# 原先的 12 次 / 10 元是开发期占位值，连 250 条都跑不起来。
#
# 注意作用域：这些是单轮存量回归的安全上限。冻结策略授权的真实增量运行也走
# 本模块，但另由下方跨运行的日共享安全阀约束，避免每个新 run 重置累计额度。
MAX_REAL_REQUESTS = _env_budget_at_most("MAX_REAL_REQUESTS", 10_000)
MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS = _env_budget("MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS", 250_000_000)
MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS = _env_budget("MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS", 25_000_000)
MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS = _env_budget_at_most(
    "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS", 1_000_000_000
)

# 2026-09-16 老大裁决：单轮存量回归这几道全局上限也取消，理由同下方的日门槛。
#
# 它们和「本轮自己的冻结预算」是两回事，别混淆：
#   * 冻结预算（runtime.real_execution_budget 的 max_requests/max_cost_micros…）
#     是这一轮自己的计划，防的是一轮跑飞、无限重试——**没有关掉，也不该关**；
#   * 下面这四个 MAX_REAL_* 是压在所有轮次头上的全局天花板。它们同样只准调低、
#     不准调高，于是「这一轮算下来要多少钱」一旦超过 1000 元，整轮根本建不起来，
#     而运营真实的最大一轮本来就会撞上去。挡掉的还是正常运行。
#
# 关掉的是「超过全局天花板就拒」这一下；每一轮仍然被它自己的冻结预算约束，
# 调用仍然逐条记账。需要恢复时把 REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED 设为真即可。
REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED = (
    os.getenv("REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", "").strip().lower()
    in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
)

# 无人值守增量评测的跨运行日安全阀。目标吞吐是每天 10 万素材，常见 A/B
# 两次调用约 20 万请求，因此请求和 token 留出 25% 余量；费用上限遵循已批准
# 的 1000 元硬边界（micros 为人民币微元）。这些限制跨 ProductionRun 汇总，
# 供应商 RPM/TPM 只负责速率，不能替代累计花费止损。
MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS = _env_budget_at_most(
    "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS", 250_000
)
MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_INPUT_TOKENS = _env_budget_at_most(
    "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_INPUT_TOKENS", 3_000_000_000
)
MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_OUTPUT_TOKENS = _env_budget_at_most(
    "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_OUTPUT_TOKENS", 512_000_000
)
MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_COST_MICROS = _env_budget_at_most(
    "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_COST_MICROS", 1_000_000_000
)

# 2026-09-16 老大裁决：这道日门槛取消，账本继续记，但不再据此拒绝请求。
#
# 它挡掉的从来不是误操作，是正常运行。上限 1000 元/天是硬编码的（上面那个
# _env_budget_at_most 只准调低不准调高），而同一份注释写明的目标吞吐是每天
# 10 万素材、约 20 万请求 ≈ 6800 元——也就是说它在正常一天跑到 15% 左右就
# 把整条无人值守链路停死。09-15 当天 4484 次请求、999,882,230/1,000,000,000
# 就是这么撞上的：91 条素材停在 model_budget_wait，既不会自己恢复（要等 UTC
# 翻天），也没有任何人会去「费用对账或人工处理」。
#
# 留下账本是有用的：花了多少、什么时候花的，照样看得见，也仍然是发布收据和
# 事后复盘的依据。去掉的只是「到点就拒」这一下。
# 需要重新启用时把这个值设为真即可，不必改代码也不必发版。
INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED = (
    os.getenv("INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", "").strip().lower()
    in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
)


# 基础设施抖动（数据库锁超时等）不是模型故障：它没有真的发出付费请求，
# 也不该把整轮回归判死。用独立错误码标记，熔断统计时排除。
INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE_CODE = "model_infrastructure_unavailable"


class WorkflowModelBudgetError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        status_code: int = 422,
        retryable: bool = False,
        retry_after_seconds: int | None = None,
        pool_key: str | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds
        self.pool_key = pool_key


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RealExecutionBudget:
    expected_requests: int
    expected_input_tokens: int
    expected_output_tokens: int
    expected_cost_micros: int
    max_requests: int
    max_input_tokens: int
    max_output_tokens: int
    max_cost_micros: int

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return asdict(self)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _PromptBudget:
    requests: int
    input_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    cost_micros: int

    def __add__(self, other: "_PromptBudget") -> "_PromptBudget":
        return _PromptBudget(
            requests=self.requests + other.requests,
            input_tokens=self.input_tokens + other.input_tokens,
            output_tokens=self.output_tokens + other.output_tokens,
            cost_micros=self.cost_micros + other.cost_micros,
        )

    def scaled(self, multiplier: int) -> "_PromptBudget":
        return _PromptBudget(
            requests=self.requests * multiplier,
            input_tokens=self.input_tokens * multiplier,
            output_tokens=self.output_tokens * multiplier,
            cost_micros=self.cost_micros * multiplier,
        )


_ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET = _PromptBudget(0, 0, 0, 0)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _PricingQuote:
    snapshot: dict[str, Any]
    version: str
    tier_key: str
    input_tokens: int
    cached_input_tokens: int
    uncached_input_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    uncached_input_cost_micros: int
    cached_input_cost_micros: int
    input_cost_micros: int
    output_cost_micros: int
    total_cost_micros: int
    full_price_cost_micros: int
    cache_savings_micros: int
    contract_hash: str


def _pricing_contract_value(model: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw = getattr(model, "pricing_contract_json", None)
    if raw is None:
        raw = getattr(model, "pricing_contract", {})
    if isinstance(raw, str):
        try:
            raw = json.loads(raw or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
                "模型阶梯计价合同不是合法 JSON",
            ) from exc
    if raw in (None, {}):
        return {}
    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "模型阶梯计价合同必须是对象",
        )
    try:
        return parse_pricing_contract(raw).to_dict()
    except ModelPricingError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc


def _flat_pricing_snapshot(model: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    input_price = int(
        getattr(model, "input_micros_per_million_tokens", 0) or 0
    )
    output_price = int(
        getattr(model, "output_micros_per_million_tokens", 0) or 0
    )
    if input_price <= 0 or output_price <= 0:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_unverified",
            "提示词节点所选模型尚未配置可核验计价",
        )
    return {
        "schema_version": "model-pricing-flat-v1",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "unit_tokens": 1_000_000,
        "input_micros_per_million_tokens": input_price,
        "output_micros_per_million_tokens": output_price,
    }


def _price_frozen_snapshot(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    cached_input_tokens: int = 0,
) -> _PricingQuote:
    version = snapshot.get("schema_version")
    if version in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS:
        try:
            contract = parse_pricing_contract(snapshot)
            priced = price_usage(
                contract,
                input_tokens=input_tokens,
                output_tokens=output_tokens,
                cached_input_tokens=cached_input_tokens,
            )
        except ModelPricingError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
        return _PricingQuote(
            snapshot=contract.to_dict(),
            version=contract.schema_version,
            tier_key=priced.tier_key,
            input_tokens=priced.input_tokens,
            cached_input_tokens=priced.cached_input_tokens,
            uncached_input_tokens=priced.uncached_input_tokens,
            output_tokens=priced.output_tokens,
            uncached_input_cost_micros=(
                priced.uncached_input_cost_micros
            ),
            cached_input_cost_micros=priced.cached_input_cost_micros,
            input_cost_micros=priced.input_cost_micros,
            output_cost_micros=priced.output_cost_micros,
            total_cost_micros=priced.total_cost_micros,
            full_price_cost_micros=priced.full_price_cost_micros,
            cache_savings_micros=priced.cache_savings_micros,
            contract_hash=priced.pricing_contract_hash,
        )
    if version == "model-pricing-flat-v1":
        input_price = snapshot.get("input_micros_per_million_tokens")
        output_price = snapshot.get("output_micros_per_million_tokens")
        if (
            not isinstance(input_price, int)
            or isinstance(input_price, bool)
            or input_price <= 0
            or not isinstance(output_price, int)
            or isinstance(output_price, bool)
            or output_price <= 0
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
                "冻结的兼容平价计价参数不完整",
                status_code=409,
            )
        canonical = {
            "schema_version": "model-pricing-flat-v1",
            "currency": "CNY",
            "unit_tokens": 1_000_000,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": input_price,
            "output_micros_per_million_tokens": output_price,
        }
        input_cost = math.ceil(input_tokens * input_price / 1_000_000)
        output_cost = math.ceil(output_tokens * output_price / 1_000_000)
        return _PricingQuote(
            snapshot=canonical,
            version="model-pricing-flat-v1",
            tier_key="flat",
            input_tokens=input_tokens,
            cached_input_tokens=0,
            uncached_input_tokens=input_tokens,
            output_tokens=output_tokens,
            uncached_input_cost_micros=input_cost,
            cached_input_cost_micros=0,
            input_cost_micros=input_cost,
            output_cost_micros=output_cost,
            total_cost_micros=input_cost + output_cost,
            full_price_cost_micros=input_cost + output_cost,
            cache_savings_micros=0,
            contract_hash=hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(canonical).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest(),
        )
    raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
        "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
        "冻结的模型计价快照版本不受支持",
        status_code=409,
    )


def _price_model_usage(
    model: Any,
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
) -> _PricingQuote:
    contract = _pricing_contract_value(model)
    snapshot = contract if contract else _flat_pricing_snapshot(model)
    return _price_frozen_snapshot(
        snapshot,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
    )


def _headroom(value: int) -> int:
    return value + math.ceil(value / 2)


def _headroom_with_full_item_retry(expected: int, per_item: int) -> int:
    """Keep proportional headroom without making one-item canaries unresumable."""

    return max(_headroom(expected), expected + per_item)


def _prompt_budget(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
) -> _PromptBudget:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
    model_snapshot = (
        model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
    )
    source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
    if not isinstance(source_id, int) or isinstance(source_id, bool):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_source_not_frozen",
            "提示词节点没有冻结可执行模型来源",
        )
    model = model_configs.get(source_id)
    if model is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_source_unavailable",
            "提示词节点冻结的模型来源不可用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if getattr(model, "benchmark_enabled", False) is not True:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_unverified",
            "提示词节点所选模型尚未由管理员核验真实调用计价",
        )
    input_tokens = int(getattr(model, "max_input_tokens", 0) or 0)
    output_tokens = config.get("max_tokens")
    if (
        not isinstance(output_tokens, int)
        or isinstance(output_tokens, bool)
        or output_tokens <= 0
        or input_tokens <= 0
        or output_tokens > int(getattr(model, "max_tokens", 0) or 0)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_token_limits_unverified",
            "提示词节点缺少经过核验的输入或输出 token 上限",
        )
    quote = _price_model_usage(
        model,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
    )
    return _PromptBudget(
        requests=1,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
        cost_micros=quote.total_cost_micros,
    )


def _component_content(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        return None
    content = snapshot.get("content")
    if isinstance(content, Mapping):
        return content
    content = snapshot.get("content_json")
    if isinstance(content, str):
        try:
            content = json.loads(content)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return None
    return content if isinstance(content, Mapping) else None


_ESTIMATE_REDACTED_KEYS = frozenset(
    {
        "api_key",
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "encrypted_api_key",
        "expected_result",
        "golden_truth",
        "password",
        "secret",
        "signed_url",
        "token",
        "truth",
    }
)


def _safe_estimate_value(value: Any, *, depth: int = 0) -> Any:
    """Bound and redact business input before estimating rendered text size."""

    if depth >= 12:
        return "<depth-limit>"
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {
            str(key): _safe_estimate_value(item, depth=depth + 1)
            for key, item in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda pair: str(pair[0]))
            if str(key).strip().lower() not in _ESTIMATE_REDACTED_KEYS
            and not str(key).strip().lower().endswith("_url")
        }
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_safe_estimate_value(item, depth=depth + 1) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        return {"binary_bytes": len(value)}
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    return str(type(value).__name__)


def _anchor_count(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return 0
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or config.get(
            "module_type_key"
        ) != "anchor_calibration":
            continue
        content = _component_content(node)
        anchors = content.get("anchors") if isinstance(content, Mapping) else None
        if content and content.get("enabled") is True and isinstance(anchors, list):
            return len(anchors)
    return 0


def _expected_image_tokens(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> int | None:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    if config.get("input_modality") != "image":
        return 0
    policy = config.get("vision_input_policy")
    roles = policy.get("roles") if isinstance(policy, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(roles, Mapping):
        return None

    def fixed_high(role: str) -> bool:
        value = roles.get(role)
        fidelity = value.get("fidelity") if isinstance(value, Mapping) else None
        return (
            isinstance(fidelity, Mapping)
            and fidelity.get("mode") == "detail"
            and fidelity.get("value") == "high"
        )

    if not fixed_high("target"):
        return None
    count = 1
    content = _component_content(node)
    if isinstance(content, Mapping) and content.get("stage") == "B":
        anchors = _anchor_count(manifest)
        if anchors and not fixed_high("anchor"):
            return None
        count += anchors
    return count * 1_280


def _resolve_budget_path(root: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> Any:
    if path == "$":
        return root
    if not path.startswith("$."):
        return None
    current: Any = root
    for part in path[2:].split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            return None
        current = current[part]
    return current


def _foreach_budget_items(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[Any] | None:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    items_path = config.get("items_path")
    if not isinstance(items_path, str):
        return None
    candidate_paths = [items_path]
    wrapped = re.fullmatch(
        r"\$\.upstream\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*\.output(?:\.(.*))?",
        items_path,
    )
    if wrapped is not None:
        suffix = wrapped.group(1)
        candidate_paths.append("$" + (f".{suffix}" if suffix else ""))
    for path in candidate_paths:
        candidate = _resolve_budget_path(input_manifest, path)
        if isinstance(candidate, list):
            return candidate
    return None


def _schema_token_bound(schema: Any, *, depth: int = 0) -> int:
    """Bound deterministic JSON output without pretending unbounded text is free."""

    if depth >= 10 or not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        return 64
    schema_type = schema.get("type")
    if schema_type == "string":
        maximum = schema.get("maxLength")
        return max(4, int(maximum) if isinstance(maximum, int) else 256)
    if schema_type in {"integer", "number", "boolean", "null"}:
        return 8
    if schema_type == "array":
        maximum = schema.get("maxItems")
        count = min(int(maximum), 10_000) if isinstance(maximum, int) else 16
        return 8 + count * (_schema_token_bound(schema.get("items"), depth=depth + 1) + 2)
    if schema_type == "object":
        properties = schema.get("properties")
        if not isinstance(properties, Mapping):
            return 256
        return 8 + sum(
            max(2, math.ceil(len(str(key).encode("utf-8")) / 3))
            + _schema_token_bound(child, depth=depth + 1)
            + 2
            for key, child in properties.items()
        )
    return 256


def _expected_upstream_tokens(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> int:
    node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if not isinstance(node_id, str):
        return 0
    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    edges_value = manifest.get("edges")
    nodes = {
        str(item["node_instance_id"]): item
        for item in (nodes_value if isinstance(nodes_value, list) else [])
        if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        and isinstance(item.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    # Nested foreach prompts execute on one child, not on the outer graph edge.
    frozen = nodes.get(node_id)
    if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) and frozen.get("kind") == "foreach_image":
        return 0
    incoming: dict[str, list[str]] = {key: [] for key in nodes}
    for edge in edges_value if isinstance(edges_value, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            continue
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str) and target in incoming:
            incoming[target].append(source)
    memo: dict[str, int] = {}

    def output_bound(current_id: str) -> int:
        if current_id in memo:
            return memo[current_id]
        current = nodes.get(current_id)
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping):
            return 256
        kind = current.get("kind")
        config = current.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if kind == "prompt":
            value = config.get("max_tokens")
            bound = int(value) if isinstance(value, int) and value > 0 else 2_048
        elif kind == "foreach_image":
            items = _foreach_budget_items(
                current,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                max_items=_foreach_max_items(current),
            )
            count = len(items)
            executor = config.get("item_executor")
            executor = executor if isinstance(executor, Mapping) else {}
            per_item = executor.get("max_tokens")
            per_item = int(per_item) if isinstance(per_item, int) and per_item > 0 else 2_048
            bound = 128 + count * (per_item + 128)
        elif kind == "aggregate_case":
            # Aggregate retains each child output and repeats selected features.
            bound = 512 + 2 * sum(output_bound(parent) for parent in incoming[current_id])
        elif kind == "upstream_connector":
            bound = 128 + math.ceil(
                len(canonical_json(_safe_estimate_value(input_manifest)).encode("utf-8")) / 3
            )
        else:
            bound = _schema_token_bound(current.get("output_schema"))
            if incoming[current_id]:
                bound = max(bound, 128 + sum(output_bound(parent) for parent in incoming[current_id]))
        memo[current_id] = bound
        return bound

    return sum(output_bound(parent) + 256 for parent in incoming.get(node_id, []))


# 「预计输出」与「输出预留」分开：预留永远按节点 max_tokens 冻结（执行器据此
# 向配额池扣留），预计则优先取同节点近期真实调用的 p95。此前两者都取上限，
# 输出单价又是输入的 6 倍，预览费用被夸大 6～14 倍，运营据此拒绝发起回归。
OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 200
OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_MIN_SAMPLES = 5
OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_PERCENTILE = 0.95

OutputExpectations = Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]


def _expected_output_tokens(
    expectation: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    cap: int,
) -> int:
    if not isinstance(expectation, Mapping):
        return cap
    value = expectation.get("output_tokens")
    if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value <= 0:
        return cap
    return min(value, cap)


def output_cap_basis(cap: int, *, sample_size: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "source": "output_cap",
        "sample_size": sample_size,
        "p95_output_tokens": None,
        "output_tokens_per_request": cap,
        "output_cap_per_request": cap,
    }


def _reprice_output(
    model: Any,
    budget: _PromptBudget,
    output_tokens: int,
) -> _PromptBudget:
    if output_tokens == budget.output_tokens:
        return budget
    quote = _price_model_usage(
        model,
        input_tokens=budget.input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
    )
    return _PromptBudget(
        requests=budget.requests,
        input_tokens=budget.input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
        cost_micros=quote.total_cost_micros,
    )


def _expected_prompt_budget(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
    *,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    output_expectation: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> _PromptBudget:
    """Price the expected rendered prompt while preserving max reservation."""

    conservative = _prompt_budget(node, model_configs)
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
    model_snapshot = (
        model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
    )
    source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
    model = model_configs.get(source_id)
    assert model is not None  # validated by _prompt_budget
    expected_output = _expected_output_tokens(
        output_expectation, conservative.output_tokens
    )
    conservative = _reprice_output(model, conservative, expected_output)
    content = _component_content(node)
    image_tokens = _expected_image_tokens(node, manifest)
    if content is None or image_tokens is None:
        return conservative
    system_prompt = content.get("system_prompt")
    user_prompt = content.get("user_prompt")
    if not isinstance(system_prompt, str) or not isinstance(user_prompt, str):
        return conservative
    prompt_payload = {
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
        "input": _safe_estimate_value(input_manifest),
        "output_contract": _safe_estimate_value(
            config.get("output_contract") or {}
        ),
    }
    text_tokens = max(
        1,
        math.ceil(len(canonical_json(prompt_payload).encode("utf-8")) / 3),
    )
    # The executor sends each direct predecessor envelope in workflow_context;
    # a template may also render previous_output, so reserve two copies.
    text_tokens += 2 * _expected_upstream_tokens(
        node,
        manifest,
        input_manifest,
    )
    input_tokens = text_tokens + image_tokens
    max_input_tokens = int(getattr(model, "max_input_tokens", 0) or 0)
    if input_tokens > max_input_tokens:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_input_exhausted",
            "预计渲染提示词与图片 token 超过模型输入上限",
        )
    quote = _price_model_usage(
        model,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=expected_output,
    )
    return _PromptBudget(
        requests=1,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=expected_output,
        cost_micros=quote.total_cost_micros,
    )


def _prompt_like_budget(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
    *,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    expected: bool = False,
    output_expectation: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> _PromptBudget:
    executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
    if executable is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_prompt_nodes_missing",
            "节点没有可执行提示词",
        )
    budget = (
        _expected_prompt_budget(
            executable,
            model_configs,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            manifest=manifest or {},
            output_expectation=output_expectation,
        )
        if expected and input_manifest is not None
        else _prompt_budget(executable, model_configs)
    )
    call_count = prompt_call_count(node, manifest or {})
    if call_count > 1:
        budget = budget.scaled(call_count)
    if node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
        return budget
    max_items = _foreach_max_items(node)
    if input_manifest is None:
        # Compatibility for old callers that can only provide a selection
        # count. The hard ceiling remains fail-closed when no frozen business
        # input exists from which to derive the exact fan-out.
        return budget.scaled(max_items)
    source = _foreach_budget_items(
        node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest,
        max_items=max_items,
    )
    if expected:
        total = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        for child in source:
            total += _expected_prompt_budget(
                executable,
                model_configs,
                input_manifest=child,
                manifest=manifest or {},
                output_expectation=output_expectation,
            )
        return total
    return budget.scaled(len(source))


def _foreach_max_items(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    config = node.get("config")
    max_items = config.get("max_items") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(max_items, int)
        or isinstance(max_items, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_items <= 10000
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_max_items_invalid",
            "foreach_image 缺少可核验的最大子项数",
        )
    return max_items


def _foreach_budget_items(
