    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    max_items: int,
) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    items_path = config.get("items_path")
    if not isinstance(items_path, str):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_items_path_invalid",
            "foreach_image 缺少可核验的子项路径",
        )
    candidate_paths = [items_path]
    wrapped = re.fullmatch(
        r"\$\.upstream\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*\.output(?:\.(.*))?",
        items_path,
    )
    if wrapped is not None:
        suffix = wrapped.group(1)
        candidate_paths.append("$" + (f".{suffix}" if suffix else ""))

    source: Any = None
    matched = False
    for candidate in candidate_paths:
        current: Any = input_manifest
        parts = candidate[2:].split(".") if candidate.startswith("$.") else []
        if candidate == "$":
            matched = True
            source = current
            break
        if not parts:
            continue
        for part in parts:
            if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
                break
            current = current[part]
        else:
            matched = True
            source = current
            break
    if not matched or not isinstance(source, list):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_budget_items_missing",
            "真实模型预算无法从冻结输入解析 foreach_image 子项",
        )
    if not source:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_budget_items_empty",
            "真实模型预算中的 foreach_image 子项不能为空",
        )
    if len(source) > max_items:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_max_items_exceeded",
            f"真实模型预算收到 {len(source)} 个子项，超过冻结上限 {max_items}",
        )
    if any(not isinstance(child, Mapping) for child in source):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "foreach_budget_item_invalid",
            "真实模型预算中的 foreach_image 子项必须是对象",
        )
    return list(source)


def _expected_prompt_request_budgets(
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
    *,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    output_expectation: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> list[_PromptBudget]:
    """Estimate every provider request so tier buckets remain exact."""

    executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
    if executable is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_prompt_nodes_missing",
            "节点没有可执行提示词",
        )
    if node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
        return [
            _expected_prompt_budget(
                executable,
                model_configs,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                manifest=manifest,
                output_expectation=output_expectation,
            )
        ]
    source = _foreach_budget_items(
        node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest,
        max_items=_foreach_max_items(node),
    )
    return [
        _expected_prompt_budget(
            executable,
            model_configs,
            input_manifest=child,
            manifest=manifest,
            output_expectation=output_expectation,
        )
        for child in source
    ]


def _configured_retry_attempts(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int | None:
    """Return an explicit node attempt ceiling, or None for legacy snapshots."""

    value = node.get("retry_policy")
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return None
    kind = value.get("kind")
    if kind == "none":
        return 1
    max_attempts = value.get("max_attempts")
    if (
        kind not in {"fixed", "exponential"}
        or not isinstance(max_attempts, int)
        or isinstance(max_attempts, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_attempts <= 5
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_retry_policy_invalid",
            "提示词节点缺少可核验的重试次数上限",
        )
    return max_attempts


def _descendants_before_join(
    outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]],
    start: str,
    join_id: str,
) -> set[str]:
    pending = [start]
    found: set[str] = set()
    while pending:
        current = pending.pop()
        if current == join_id or current in found:
            continue
        found.add(current)
        pending.extend(outgoing.get(current, []))
    return found


def _exclusive_prompt_groups(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    prompt_ids: set[str],
) -> list[list[set[str]]]:
    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    edges_value = manifest.get("edges")
    nodes = [item for item in nodes_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)] if isinstance(nodes_value, list) else []
    edges = [item for item in edges_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)] if isinstance(edges_value, list) else []
    outgoing: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for edge in edges:
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
            outgoing.setdefault(source, []).append(target)
    groups: list[list[set[str]]] = []
    already_grouped: set[str] = set()
    for node in nodes:
        if node.get("kind") != "branch":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if config.get("mode") != "exclusive":
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        branch_key = config.get("branch_key")
        branches = config.get("branches")
        if (
            not isinstance(node_id, str)
            or not isinstance(branch_key, str)
            or not isinstance(branches, list)
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in branches)
        ):
            continue
        join_id = next(
            (
                candidate.get("node_instance_id")
                for candidate in nodes
                if candidate.get("kind") == "join"
                and isinstance(candidate.get("config"), Mapping)
                and candidate["config"].get("join_for") == branch_key
                and isinstance(candidate.get("node_instance_id"), str)
            ),
            None,
        )
        if not isinstance(join_id, str):
            continue
        branch_targets = {
            edge.get("branch"): edge.get("to")
            for edge in edges
            if edge.get("from") == node_id
            and isinstance(edge.get("branch"), str)
            and isinstance(edge.get("to"), str)
        }
        route_prompts: list[set[str]] = []
        for branch in branches:
            target = branch_targets.get(branch)
            if not isinstance(target, str):
                route_prompts = []
                break
            route_prompts.append(
                _descendants_before_join(outgoing, target, join_id) & prompt_ids
            )
        covered = set().union(*route_prompts) if route_prompts else set()
        if (
            not covered
            or covered & already_grouped
            or sum(len(item) for item in route_prompts) != len(covered)
        ):
            continue
        groups.append(route_prompts)
        already_grouped.update(covered)
    return groups


def _sum_prompt_budgets(
    prompt_ids: set[str],
    budgets: Mapping[str, _PromptBudget],
) -> _PromptBudget:
    total = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
    for prompt_id in sorted(prompt_ids):
        total += budgets[prompt_id]
    return total


def _componentwise_max_prompt_budget(
    budgets: list[_PromptBudget],
) -> _PromptBudget:
    if not budgets:
        return _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
    return _PromptBudget(
        requests=max(item.requests for item in budgets),
        input_tokens=max(item.input_tokens for item in budgets),
        output_tokens=max(item.output_tokens for item in budgets),
        cost_micros=max(item.cost_micros for item in budgets),
    )


def real_execution_budget_for_selection(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    item_count: int,
    *,
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
    input_manifests: list[Mapping[str, Any]] | tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...] | None = None,
    enforce: bool = True,
    output_expectations: OutputExpectations | None = None,
) -> RealExecutionBudget:
    """Return a bounded budget using the worst reachable prompt path per item.

    ``output_expectations`` only shrinks the *expected* output/cost per node
    (keyed by node_instance_id); every ``max_*`` ceiling still reserves the
    frozen ``max_tokens`` so the quota ledger and retries stay fail-closed.
    """

    expectations = output_expectations or {}

    if item_count <= 0:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_items_empty",
            "真实模型评测至少需要一条素材",
        )
    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = [item for item in nodes_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)] if isinstance(nodes_value, list) else []
    prompt_nodes = {
        str(node["node_instance_id"]): node
        for node in nodes
        if node.get("kind") in {"prompt", "foreach_image"}
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    if not prompt_nodes:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_prompt_nodes_missing",
            "完整评测机制没有可执行提示词节点",
        )
    configured_attempts = {
        node_id: _configured_retry_attempts(node)
        for node_id, node in prompt_nodes.items()
    }
    online_models = {
        key: getattr(model, "_online_fallback_model", model)
        for key, model in model_configs.items()
    }
    fallback_nodes = {
        node_id for node_id, node in prompt_nodes.items()
        if getattr(model_configs.get(
            ((prompt_execution_node(node) or {}).get("config") or {})
            .get("model_snapshot", {}).get("source_model_config_id")
        ), "_online_fallback_model", None) is not None
    }
    exclusive_groups = _exclusive_prompt_groups(manifest, set(prompt_nodes))

    def one_item(
        input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    ) -> tuple[_PromptBudget, _PromptBudget, _PromptBudget]:
        expected_prompt_budgets = {
            node_id: _prompt_like_budget(
                node,
                model_configs,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                manifest=manifest,
                expected=input_manifest is not None,
                output_expectation=expectations.get(node_id),
            )
            for node_id, node in prompt_nodes.items()
        }
        reservation_prompt_budgets = {
            node_id: _prompt_like_budget(
                node,
                model_configs,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                manifest=manifest,
                expected=False,
            )
            for node_id, node in prompt_nodes.items()
        }
        configured_budgets = {
            node_id: reservation_prompt_budgets[node_id].scaled(
                configured_attempts[node_id] or 1
            )
            for node_id in prompt_nodes
        }
        for node_id in fallback_nodes:
            configured_budgets[node_id] = (
                reservation_prompt_budgets[node_id].scaled(3)
                + _prompt_like_budget(
                    prompt_nodes[node_id], online_models,
                    input_manifest=input_manifest, manifest=manifest, expected=False,
                ).scaled(configured_attempts[node_id] or 1)
            )
        legacy_retry_budgets = {
            node_id: (
                reservation_prompt_budgets[node_id]
                if configured_attempts[node_id] is None and node_id not in fallback_nodes
                else _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
            )
            for node_id in prompt_nodes
        }
        grouped: set[str] = set()
        per_item = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        configured_per_item = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        legacy_retry_per_item = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        for routes in exclusive_groups:
            grouped.update(set().union(*routes))
            per_item += _componentwise_max_prompt_budget(
                [
                    _sum_prompt_budgets(route, expected_prompt_budgets)
                    for route in routes
                ]
            )
            configured_per_item += _componentwise_max_prompt_budget(
                [
                    _sum_prompt_budgets(route, configured_budgets)
                    for route in routes
                ]
            )
            legacy_retry_per_item += _componentwise_max_prompt_budget(
                [
                    _sum_prompt_budgets(route, legacy_retry_budgets)
                    for route in routes
                ]
            )
        per_item += _sum_prompt_budgets(
            set(prompt_nodes) - grouped,
            expected_prompt_budgets,
        )
        configured_per_item += _sum_prompt_budgets(
            set(prompt_nodes) - grouped,
            configured_budgets,
        )
        legacy_retry_per_item += _sum_prompt_budgets(
            set(prompt_nodes) - grouped,
            legacy_retry_budgets,
        )
        return per_item, configured_per_item, legacy_retry_per_item

    if input_manifests is not None:
        if len(input_manifests) != item_count or any(
            not isinstance(item, Mapping) for item in input_manifests
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_budget_inputs_mismatch",
                "真实模型预算的冻结输入数量与所选素材数不一致",
            )
        item_budgets = [one_item(item) for item in input_manifests]
        per_item_budgets = [item[0] for item in item_budgets]
        configured_item_budgets = [item[1] for item in item_budgets]
        legacy_retry_item_budgets = [item[2] for item in item_budgets]
        expected = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        configured_max = _ZERO_PROMPT_BUDGET
        for item in per_item_budgets:
            expected += item
        for item in configured_item_budgets:
            configured_max += item
        largest_legacy_retry_item = _componentwise_max_prompt_budget(
            legacy_retry_item_budgets
        )
    else:
        per_item, configured_per_item, legacy_retry_per_item = one_item(None)
        expected = per_item.scaled(item_count)
        configured_max = configured_per_item.scaled(item_count)
        largest_legacy_retry_item = legacy_retry_per_item

    expected_requests = expected.requests
    expected_input_tokens = expected.input_tokens
    expected_output_tokens = expected.output_tokens
    expected_cost_micros = expected.cost_micros
    enforce = enforce and REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED
    if enforce and expected_requests > MAX_REAL_REQUESTS:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_requests_exhausted",
            "本轮预计模型请求数超过真实评测安全上限",
        )
    if enforce and expected_input_tokens > MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_input_exhausted",
            "本轮预计输入 token 超过真实评测安全上限",
        )
    if enforce and expected_output_tokens > MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_output_exhausted",
            "本轮预计输出 token 超过真实评测安全上限",
        )
    if enforce and expected_cost_micros > MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_cost_exhausted",
            "本轮预计模型费用超过真实评测人民币硬上限",
        )
    # Legacy snapshots do not freeze an attempt ceiling. Preserve the prior
    # 50% retry allowance (minimum one full item), but compute every dimension
    # from the conservative reservation budget. This keeps request, token and
    # cost ceilings mutually consistent even when the expected prompt estimate
    # is much smaller than the model context limit.
    legacy_retry_multiplier = max(1, math.ceil(item_count / 2))
    maximum = configured_max + largest_legacy_retry_item.scaled(
        legacy_retry_multiplier
    )
    return RealExecutionBudget(
        expected_requests=expected_requests,
        expected_input_tokens=expected_input_tokens,
        expected_output_tokens=expected_output_tokens,
        expected_cost_micros=expected_cost_micros,
        max_requests=min(
            MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
            maximum.requests,
        ),
        max_input_tokens=min(
            MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
            maximum.input_tokens,
        ),
        max_output_tokens=min(
            MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
            maximum.output_tokens,
        ),
        max_cost_micros=min(
            MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS,
            maximum.cost_micros,
        ),
    )


def prompt_call_count(node: Mapping[str, Any], manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    """一次提示词节点调用实际向供应商发几次请求：多次采样投票 ``vote_samples`` 次，
    成对锚点比较再对 anchor_calibration 里的每张锚点各发 ``pairwise_samples`` 次。

    预留与冻结预算都按这个次数放大；否则合计用量结算必超预留（09-19 run 350 全军覆没）。
    """
    config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    votes = config.get("vote_samples")
    votes = votes if isinstance(votes, int) and not isinstance(votes, bool) and votes > 1 else 1
    if config.get("pairwise_anchors") is not True:
        return votes
    pair = config.get("pairwise_samples")
    pair = pair if isinstance(pair, int) and not isinstance(pair, bool) and pair > 1 else 1
    anchors = 0
    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    for other in nodes_value if isinstance(nodes_value, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(other, Mapping):
            continue
        other_config = other.get("config") if isinstance(other.get("config"), Mapping) else {}
        snapshot = other_config.get("component_snapshot")
        snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        content: Any = snapshot.get("content")
        if content is None and isinstance(snapshot.get("content_json"), str):
            try:
                content = json.loads(snapshot["content_json"])
            except ValueError:
                content = None
        module_key = (content.get("module_type_key") if isinstance(content, Mapping) else None) or snapshot.get("module_type_key") or other_config.get("module_type_key") or other.get("module_type_key")
        if isinstance(content, Mapping) and isinstance(content.get("anchors"), list) and (
            module_key == "anchor_calibration" or str(other.get("node_instance_id") or "").startswith("anchor_calibration")
        ):
            anchors = len(content["anchors"])
            break
    return votes + anchors * pair


def real_execution_node_limits(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    model_configs: Mapping[int, Any],
    input_manifests: list[Mapping[str, Any]] | tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...] | None = None,
    output_expectations: OutputExpectations | None = None,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Freeze the reservation identity for each real prompt invocation."""

    expectations = output_expectations or {}

    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = (
        [item for item in nodes_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)]
        if isinstance(nodes_value, list)
        else []
    )
    result: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node in nodes:
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
        if executable is None or not isinstance(node_id, str):
            continue
        config = executable.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        model_snapshot = (
            model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        )
        source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
        prompt_budget = _prompt_budget(executable, model_configs)
        call_count = prompt_call_count(node, manifest)
        source_model = model_configs.get(source_id)
        assert source_model is not None  # validated by _prompt_budget
        quote = _price_model_usage(
            source_model,
            input_tokens=prompt_budget.input_tokens,
            output_tokens=prompt_budget.output_tokens,
        )
        result[node_id] = {
            "provider": str(model_snapshot.get("provider") or ""),
            "model_id": str(model_snapshot.get("model_id") or ""),
            "source_model_config_id": source_id,
            "max_input_tokens": prompt_budget.input_tokens,
            "max_output_tokens": prompt_budget.output_tokens,
            "estimated_cost_micros": quote.total_cost_micros,
            "call_count": call_count,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": int(
                getattr(source_model, "input_micros_per_million_tokens", 0)
                or 0
            ),
            "output_micros_per_million_tokens": int(
                getattr(source_model, "output_micros_per_million_tokens", 0)
                or 0
            ),
            "pricing_version": quote.version,
            "pricing_tier_key": quote.tier_key,
            "pricing_contract_hash": quote.contract_hash,
            "pricing_snapshot": quote.snapshot,
        }
        online_model = getattr(source_model, "_online_fallback_model", None)
        if online_model is not None:
            online_limits = real_execution_node_limits(
                {"nodes": [node]}, model_configs={source_id: online_model},
            )[node_id]
            # 单节点清单看不到 anchor_calibration，成对比较次数会漏算：按完整清单的次数冻结。
            online_limits["call_count"] = call_count
            online_limits["max_attempts"] = _configured_retry_attempts(node) or 1
            result[node_id]["online_fallback_limits"] = online_limits
        if input_manifests:
            expected_requests: list[_PromptBudget] = []
            for input_manifest in input_manifests:
                per_item = _expected_prompt_request_budgets(
                    node,
                    model_configs,
                    input_manifest=input_manifest,
                    manifest=manifest,
                    output_expectation=expectations.get(node_id),
                )
                for _ in range(call_count):
                    expected_requests.extend(per_item)
            expected_quotes = [
                _price_model_usage(
                    source_model,
                    input_tokens=expected.input_tokens,
                    output_tokens=expected.output_tokens,
                )
                for expected in expected_requests
            ]
            expected_max_index = max(
                range(len(expected_requests)),
                key=lambda index: (
                    expected_quotes[index].total_cost_micros,
                    expected_requests[index].input_tokens,
                    expected_requests[index].output_tokens,
                ),
            )
            expected_max = expected_requests[expected_max_index]
            expected_quote = expected_quotes[expected_max_index]
            tier_buckets: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
            for expected, request_quote in zip(
                expected_requests,
                expected_quotes,
                strict=True,
            ):
                bucket = tier_buckets.setdefault(
                    request_quote.tier_key,
                    {
                        "pricing_tier_key": request_quote.tier_key,
                        "request_count": 0,
                        "input_tokens": 0,
                        "output_tokens": 0,
                        "cost_micros": 0,
                    },
                )
                bucket["request_count"] += 1
                bucket["input_tokens"] += expected.input_tokens
                bucket["output_tokens"] += expected.output_tokens
                bucket["cost_micros"] += request_quote.total_cost_micros
            configured_attempts = _configured_retry_attempts(node)
            result[node_id].update(
                {
                    "expected_request_count": len(expected_requests),
                    "expected_input_tokens": sum(
                        expected.input_tokens
                        for expected in expected_requests
                    ),
                    "expected_output_tokens": sum(
                        expected.output_tokens
                        for expected in expected_requests
                    ),
                    "expected_cost_micros": sum(
                        quote.total_cost_micros
                        for quote in expected_quotes
                    ),
                    "expected_tier_buckets": list(tier_buckets.values()),
                    "configured_max_attempts": configured_attempts,
                    "retry_reserve_request_count": (
                        None
                        if configured_attempts is None
                        else len(expected_requests)
                        * (configured_attempts - 1)
                    ),
                    "expected_input_tokens_per_request": (
                        expected_max.input_tokens
                    ),
                    "expected_output_tokens_per_request": (
                        expected_max.output_tokens
                    ),
                    "expected_cost_micros_per_request": (
                        expected_quote.total_cost_micros
                    ),
                    "expected_pricing_tier_key": expected_quote.tier_key,
                    "expected_output_basis": dict(
                        expectations.get(node_id)
                        or output_cap_basis(prompt_budget.output_tokens)
                    ),
                }
            )
        if not result[node_id]["provider"] or not result[node_id]["model_id"]:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_identity_not_frozen",
                "提示词节点缺少冻结的模型身份",
            )
    return result


def _unquote_json_scalar(value: Any) -> str | None:
    """MySQL 的 json_extract 返回带引号的 JSON 字面量，SQLite 直接返回文本。"""

    if value is None:
        return None
    text_value = str(value)
    if len(text_value) >= 2 and text_value[0] == '"' and text_value[-1] == '"':
        try:
            return str(json.loads(text_value))
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return text_value[1:-1]
    return text_value


def _percentile(samples: list[int], fraction: float) -> int:
    ordered = sorted(samples)
    index = max(0, min(len(ordered) - 1, math.ceil(len(ordered) * fraction) - 1))
    return ordered[index]


def historical_output_expectations(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    sample_limit: int = OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_SAMPLE_LIMIT,
    min_samples: int = OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_MIN_SAMPLES,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Derive per-node expected output tokens from recent settled invocations.

    Match order: same node + model + prompt content (``component_hash``) first;
    otherwise same node + model with the same thinking on/off state; otherwise
    fall back to the frozen output cap. Thinking state is decisive because
    reasoning tokens are billed as output and dwarf the visible answer.
    """

    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = (
        [item for item in nodes_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)]
        if isinstance(nodes_value, list)
        else []
    )
    result: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node in nodes:
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
        if executable is None or not isinstance(node_id, str):
            continue
        config = executable.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        cap = config.get("max_tokens")
        if not isinstance(cap, int) or isinstance(cap, bool) or cap <= 0:
            continue
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        model_snapshot = (
            model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        )
        model_id = str(model_snapshot.get("model_id") or "")
        if not model_id:
            continue
        component = config.get("component_snapshot")
        component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
        content_hash = component.get("content_hash")
        thinking_off = (
            config.get("thinking_enabled") is not True
            or config.get("thinking_level") in (None, "none")
        )
        rows = db.execute(
            select(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.output_tokens,
                func.json_extract(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json, "$.component_hash"
                ),
                func.json_extract(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json, "$.thinking_mode"
                ),
            )
            .where(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == node_id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.model_id == model_id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "succeeded",
                WorkflowModelInvocation.output_tokens > 0,
            )
            .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc())
            .limit(sample_limit)
        ).all()
        exact: list[int] = []
        loose: list[int] = []
        for output_tokens, raw_hash, raw_thinking in rows:
            thinking_mode = _unquote_json_scalar(raw_thinking)
            if thinking_off and thinking_mode not in (None, "disabled"):
                continue
            if not thinking_off and thinking_mode in (None, "disabled"):
                continue
            loose.append(int(output_tokens))
            if (
                isinstance(content_hash, str)
                and _unquote_json_scalar(raw_hash) == content_hash
            ):
                exact.append(int(output_tokens))
        if len(exact) >= min_samples:
            samples, source = exact, "prompt_history"
        elif len(loose) >= min_samples:
            samples, source = loose, "node_history"
        else:
            result[node_id] = output_cap_basis(cap, sample_size=len(loose))
            continue
        p95 = _percentile(samples, OUTPUT_EXPECTATION_PERCENTILE)
        result[node_id] = {
            "source": source,
            "sample_size": len(samples),
            "p95_output_tokens": p95,
            "output_tokens": min(p95, cap),
            "output_tokens_per_request": min(p95, cap),
            "output_cap_per_request": cap,
        }
    return result


def real_execution_plan_for_manifest(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    item_count: int = 1,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    require_secret: bool = True,
    inference_route: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
    """Derive paid limits only from frozen prompt nodes and server model rows."""

    source_ids: set[int] = set()
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    for node in nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
        if executable is None:
            continue
        config = executable.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        model_snapshot = (
            model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        )
        source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
        if isinstance(source_id, int) and not isinstance(source_id, bool):
            source_ids.add(source_id)
    model_configs = {
        source_id: row
        for source_id in source_ids
        if (row := db.get(ModelConfig, source_id)) is not None
    }
    if len(model_configs) != len(source_ids):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_source_unavailable",
            "工作流冻结的模型来源不可用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if require_secret and any(
        not secret_reference_available(model.encrypted_api_key)
        for model in model_configs.values()
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_secret_unavailable",
            "真实模型密钥未保存或当前服务器无法读取",
            status_code=422,
        )
    from .batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
    model_configs = pricing_models_for_route(model_configs, inference_route)
    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count,
        model_configs=model_configs,
        input_manifests=(
            [input_manifest]
            if input_manifest is not None and item_count == 1
            else None
        ),
    )
    node_limits = real_execution_node_limits(
        manifest,
        model_configs=model_configs,
    )
    return budget.as_dict(), node_limits


def _begin_immediate_if_needed(db: Session) -> None:
    if db.in_transaction():
        return
    connection = db.connection()
    if connection.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        connection.exec_driver_sql("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")


def _stored_snapshot(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_run_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型运行快照损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_run_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型运行快照必须是对象",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return snapshot


def _positive_budget_value(budget: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> int:
    value = budget.get(key)
    if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 0:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型预算快照损坏",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return value


def _pricing_quote_from_limits(
    limits: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
) -> _PricingQuote:
    raw_snapshot = limits.get("pricing_snapshot")
    if isinstance(raw_snapshot, Mapping) and raw_snapshot:
        snapshot = dict(raw_snapshot)
    else:
        # Compatibility for already-frozen pre-v2 runtime snapshots.  This is
        # an explicit conversion of their own flat rates, never an inferred
        # official Doubao preset.
        snapshot = {
            "schema_version": "model-pricing-flat-v1",
            "currency": "CNY",
            "unit_tokens": 1_000_000,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": limits.get(
                "input_micros_per_million_tokens"
            ),
            "output_micros_per_million_tokens": limits.get(
                "output_micros_per_million_tokens"
            ),
        }
    quote = _price_frozen_snapshot(
        snapshot,
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
    )
    frozen_version = limits.get("pricing_version")
    frozen_tier = limits.get("pricing_tier_key")
    frozen_hash = limits.get("pricing_contract_hash")
    if frozen_version is not None and frozen_version != quote.version:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "冻结计价版本与价格快照不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if frozen_tier is not None and frozen_tier != quote.tier_key:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "冻结计价档位与价格快照不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if frozen_hash is not None and frozen_hash != quote.contract_hash:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "冻结计价摘要与价格快照不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return quote


def _failure_streak_limit() -> int:
    """熔断连击阈值，可用 MODEL_FAILURE_STREAK_LIMIT 覆盖，默认 4。

    原阈值 2 过于敏感：服务器一次约 2 分钟的高负载窗口里相邻两次超时就触发，
    run137 因此判死剩余 158 条——那轮真实的模型侧失败率只有约 3%。持续故障
    仍会止损，偶发抖动不再连坐整轮。
    """
    return max(2, _env_budget("MODEL_FAILURE_STREAK_LIMIT", 4))


def _is_sha256(value: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, str)
        and len(value) == 64
        and all(character in "0123456789abcdef" for character in value)
    )


def real_execution_runtime_kind(
    run: ProductionRun,
    runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> str:
    """Validate the immutable run identity used by every paid model boundary."""

    if run.model_execution_mode != "real" or run.environment != "dry_run":
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "当前运行没有真实模型评测授权",
            status_code=403,
        )
    budget = runtime.get("real_execution_budget")
    node_limits = runtime.get("real_execution_node_limits")
    if not isinstance(budget, Mapping) or not isinstance(node_limits, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "当前运行没有冻结真实模型预算",
            status_code=403,
        )
    if (
        run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item"
        and runtime.get("run_mode") == "stock_regression"
        and runtime.get("real_execution_confirmed") is True
    ):
        return "stock_regression"
    if (
        run.source_type == "content_ingress_event"
        and isinstance(run.source_id, int)
        and not isinstance(run.source_id, bool)
        and run.queue_class == "production_batch"
        and runtime.get("run_mode") == "incremental"
        and runtime.get("content_ingress_event_db_id") == run.source_id
        and runtime.get("real_execution_confirmed") is True
        and runtime.get("real_execution_authority")
        == "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy"
        and bool(node_limits)
        and _is_sha256(runtime.get("incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash"))
        and _is_sha256(runtime.get("content_ingress_payload_hash"))
        and _is_sha256(runtime.get("upstream_intake_payload_hash"))
    ):
        return "incremental"
    raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
        "real_model_execution_forbidden",
        "当前运行没有真实模型评测授权",
        status_code=403,
    )


def _real_execution_scope(
    run: ProductionRun,
    runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str, int | None, Any]:
    """Return the optional baseline id and the immutable ledger scope clause."""

    kind = real_execution_runtime_kind(run, runtime)
    if kind == "stock_regression":
        baseline_run_id = runtime.get("baseline_run_id")
        if not isinstance(baseline_run_id, int) or isinstance(
            baseline_run_id, bool
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_baseline_identity_missing",
                "真实模型运行缺少存量回归批次身份",
                status_code=409,
            )
        return (
            kind,
            baseline_run_id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.baseline_run_id == baseline_run_id,
        )
    return (
        kind,
        None,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
    )


def _lock_incremental_daily_ledger(
    db: Session,
    *,
    business_date_utc: date,
) -> WorkflowModelDailyBudget:
    """Create and lock one UTC-day ledger row without a startup race."""

    values = {
        "business_date_utc": business_date_utc,
        "scope": "incremental_real",
    }
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        statement = sqlite_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_nothing(
            index_elements=["business_date_utc", "scope"]
        )
    elif dialect == "mysql":
        statement = mysql_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_duplicate_key_update(
            scope=statement.inserted.scope
        )
    elif dialect == "postgresql":
        statement = postgresql_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(
            **values
        )
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_nothing(
            index_elements=["business_date_utc", "scope"]
        )
    else:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "incremental_daily_budget_database_unsupported",
            "当前数据库不支持无人值守增量评测日账本",
            status_code=409,
        )
    db.execute(statement)
    ledger = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget)
        .where(
            WorkflowModelDailyBudget.business_date_utc
            == business_date_utc,
            WorkflowModelDailyBudget.scope == "incremental_real",
        )
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if ledger is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "incremental_daily_budget_ledger_missing",
            "无人值守增量评测日账本初始化失败",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return ledger


def _count_incremental_daily_ledger(
    db: Session,
    *,
    business_date_utc: date,
    reserved_input: int,
    reserved_output: int,
    estimated_cost: int,
) -> None:
    """只记账、不判门槛：一条原子 upsert，不锁行、不回读。

    2026-09-16 慢查询日志（2 秒阈值，40 分钟）2,795 条里 2,782 条（99.5%）是这张表的
    upsert——执行 0.00 秒、等锁平均 2.58 秒、累计等锁 7,175 秒。全表只有「每 UTC 日一行」，
    每次真实调用都 ``FOR UPDATE`` 同一行，而且这把锁从 reserve_model_invocation 第 2366 行
    一直握到第 2587 行 return，中间还扫一遍 workflow_model_invocations。96 个并发预留者
    就全在这一行上串行。

    门槛关着时（生产默认，INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED=false）没有任何人读这行来做
    决定——全仓只有 _enforce 自己读它——所以锁读毫无用处。让数据库自己做加法，不回读。
    注意 InnoDB 仍会把这一行的 X 锁握到事务提交，所以 reserve_model_invocation 把这条
    upsert 放在配额池加锁之后、flush 之前：它只叠在本来就有的配额池行锁后面，不再
    把预算投影和配额判定串行到这一行上。SQLite/Postgres 用 ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE。
    """

    values = {
        "business_date_utc": business_date_utc,
        "scope": "incremental_real",
        "request_count": 1,
        "reserved_input_tokens": reserved_input,
        "reserved_output_tokens": reserved_output,
        "estimated_cost_micros": estimated_cost,
    }
    table = WorkflowModelDailyBudget.__table__
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    if dialect == "mysql":
        statement = mysql_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_duplicate_key_update(
            request_count=table.c.request_count + 1,
            reserved_input_tokens=table.c.reserved_input_tokens + reserved_input,
            reserved_output_tokens=table.c.reserved_output_tokens + reserved_output,
            estimated_cost_micros=table.c.estimated_cost_micros + estimated_cost,
        )
    elif dialect == "postgresql":
        statement = postgresql_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_update(
            index_elements=["business_date_utc", "scope"],
            set_={
                "request_count": table.c.request_count + 1,
                "reserved_input_tokens": table.c.reserved_input_tokens + reserved_input,
                "reserved_output_tokens": table.c.reserved_output_tokens + reserved_output,
                "estimated_cost_micros": table.c.estimated_cost_micros + estimated_cost,
            },
        )
    elif dialect == "sqlite":
        statement = sqlite_insert(WorkflowModelDailyBudget).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_update(
            index_elements=["business_date_utc", "scope"],
            set_={
                "request_count": table.c.request_count + 1,
                "reserved_input_tokens": table.c.reserved_input_tokens + reserved_input,
                "reserved_output_tokens": table.c.reserved_output_tokens + reserved_output,
                "estimated_cost_micros": table.c.estimated_cost_micros + estimated_cost,
            },
        )
    else:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "incremental_daily_budget_database_unsupported",
            "当前数据库不支持无人值守增量评测日账本",
            status_code=409,
        )
    db.execute(statement)
    # 原子 upsert 绕过了 ORM：同一会话里如果早已加载过这一行（比如同一进程刚走过一次
    # 开门槛的锁读路径），identity map 里那份对象还停在旧计数，之后不带 populate_existing
    # 的 select 会把陈旧对象原样还回来。只让这一行失效——不用 expire_all，调用方此刻
    # 正拿着 run / attempt 对象在改。
    for instance in list(db.identity_map.values()):
        if (isinstance(instance, WorkflowModelDailyBudget)
                and instance.business_date_utc == business_date_utc
                and instance.scope == "incremental_real"):
            db.expire(instance)


def _enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
    db: Session,
    *,
    reserved_input: int,
    reserved_output: int,
    estimated_cost: int,
) -> date:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if not INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED:
        # 不判门槛就不需要「先读再比」，也就不需要锁住那一行等别人。
        _count_incremental_daily_ledger(
            db,
            business_date_utc=now.date(),
            reserved_input=reserved_input,
            reserved_output=reserved_output,
            estimated_cost=estimated_cost,
        )
        return now.date()
    ledger = _lock_incremental_daily_ledger(
        db,
        business_date_utc=now.date(),
    )
    checks = (
        (
            ledger.request_count + 1,
            MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS,
            "incremental_daily_requests_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            ledger.reserved_input_tokens + reserved_input,
            MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_INPUT_TOKENS,
            "incremental_daily_input_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            ledger.reserved_output_tokens + reserved_output,
            MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
            "incremental_daily_output_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            ledger.estimated_cost_micros + estimated_cost,
            MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_COST_MICROS,
            "incremental_daily_cost_exhausted",
        ),
    )
    for projected, limit, code in checks:
        if projected > limit:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                code,
                "无人值守增量评测已达到当日共享安全上限，未发起本次请求",
                status_code=409,
            )
    ledger.request_count += 1
    ledger.reserved_input_tokens += reserved_input
    ledger.reserved_output_tokens += reserved_output
    ledger.estimated_cost_micros += estimated_cost
    db.flush()
    return now.date()


def _quota_concurrency_headroom(
    db: Session,
    quota_pool_id: int,
    *,
    runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
    transport_mode: str | None,
    endpoint_id: str | None,
) -> int:
    # The dispatched endpoint takes precedence over the original batch route:
    # an online fallback still belongs to a run whose frozen route is batch.
    if endpoint_id is not None:
        is_batch = endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-")
    elif transport_mode is not None:
        is_batch = transport_mode == "batch"
    else:
        route = runtime.get("inference_route")
        is_batch = isinstance(route, Mapping) and route.get("mode") == "batch"
    if not is_batch:
        return 0
    # Hold the same pool lock until acquisition commits so a concurrent cap
    # update cannot change the small-pool headroom decision between reads.
    pool_max = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaPool.max_concurrency).where(
        ModelQuotaPool.id == quota_pool_id,
    ).with_for_update())
    if pool_max is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable("quota_pool_missing", "模型配额池不存在")
    return batch_concurrency_headroom(pool_max)


def _protect_fallback_quota_lease(db: Session, quota: Any, runtime: Mapping[str, Any], transport_mode: str | None) -> Any:
    """Keep this batch call's concurrency slot throughout its provider wait."""
    route = runtime.get("inference_route")
    policy = route.get("fallback_policy") if isinstance(route, Mapping) else None
    if (transport_mode != "batch" or not isinstance(policy, Mapping)
            or policy.get("version") != "batch-online-fallback-v1"):
        return quota
    lease = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
        ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == quota.lease_token,
        ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
    ).with_for_update())
    if lease is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_quota_lease_missing", "批量模型调用缺少有效并发租约", status_code=409)
    minimum = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=86400 + 300)
    current = lease.expires_at
    current = current if current.tzinfo else current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    lease.expires_at = max(current, minimum)
    db.flush()
    return replace(quota, expires_at=lease.expires_at)


def _invocation_budget_usage(row: WorkflowModelInvocation) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """Known terminal usage consumes actual fees; unknown calls retain liability.

    Reservation evidence remains immutable.  A failed response can still have
    billable usage, and exceeding a reservation must never reduce its charge.
    """
    actual = (row.input_tokens, row.output_tokens, row.actual_cost_micros)
    # Numeric columns default to zero even for an unreported timeout. Only
    # settle_model_invocation writes the result evidence; zeros alone are not
    # proof that a dispatched request was free.
    settled = isinstance(_evidence_object(row).get("result"), Mapping)
    if settled and row.status in {"succeeded", "failed"} and all(
        isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value >= 0
        for value in actual
    ):
        return actual
    return (row.reserved_input_tokens, row.reserved_output_tokens, row.estimated_cost_micros)


def reserve_model_invocation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    attempt_id: int,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    invocation_key: str = "root",
    reserved_images: int = 0,
    dispatch_identity: str | None = None,
    paid_correction_confirmed: bool = False,
    endpoint_id: str | None = None,
    transport_mode: str | None = None,
    submission_estimate: dict | None = None,
    fair_ticket: str | None = None,
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    """Reserve one immutable prompt call before any provider side effect."""

    if endpoint_id is not None and (
        not isinstance(endpoint_id, str) or not endpoint_id.strip() or len(endpoint_id) > 80
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_endpoint_invalid",
            "模型调用接入点 ID 不合法",
            status_code=409,
        )

    if (
        not isinstance(invocation_key, str)
        or invocation_key != invocation_key.strip()
        or not invocation_key
        or len(invocation_key) > 240
        or any(ord(character) < 32 for character in invocation_key)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_key_invalid",
            "模型调用业务身份不合法",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if dispatch_identity is not None and (
        not isinstance(dispatch_identity, str)
        or len(dispatch_identity) != 64
        or any(character not in "0123456789abcdef" for character in dispatch_identity)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_dispatch_identity_invalid",
            "模型请求发送身份摘要不合法",
            status_code=409,
        )
    _begin_immediate_if_needed(db)
    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_step_attempt_missing",
            "真实模型节点尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    # 全部运行时写路径都按 run -> attempt 的顺序加锁。取消运行同样先锁
    # ProductionRun，因此它与供应商调用预留不会在“已扫描预留、尚未提交终态”
    # 的窗口交错，避免取消后仍出现新的付费调用。
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_production_run_missing",
            "真实模型运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_step_attempt_missing",
            "真实模型节点尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run_accepts_invocation = run.status in {"queued", "running"} or (
        paid_correction_confirmed and run.status == "succeeded"
    )
    if not run_accepts_invocation or attempt.status not in {
        "pending",
        "leased",
        "running",
        "retryable",
    }:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_run_terminal_state",
            "真实模型运行或步骤已经停止",
            status_code=409,
        )
    paid_correction_checkpoint: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    if paid_correction_confirmed:
        try:
            checkpoint_value = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json or "{}")
        except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            checkpoint_value = None
        if not (
            isinstance(checkpoint_value, Mapping)
            and checkpoint_value.get("schema_version")
            == "workflow-correction-paid-rerun-v1"
            and checkpoint_value.get("real_execution_confirmed") is True
            and isinstance(checkpoint_value.get("correction_key"), str)
            and bool(str(checkpoint_value.get("correction_key") or "").strip())
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_paid_correction_confirmation_invalid",
                "人工纠偏真实模型重跑缺少可审计的费用确认",
                status_code=409,
            )
        paid_correction_checkpoint = checkpoint_value
    stored_snapshot = _stored_snapshot(run)
    if canonical_json(dict(run_snapshot)) != canonical_json(stored_snapshot):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_run_snapshot_mismatch",
            "真实模型运行快照与数据库记录不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    runtime = stored_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    runtime = runtime if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else {}
    execution_kind, baseline_run_id, ledger_scope = _real_execution_scope(
        run,
        runtime,
    )
    if baseline_run_id is not None:
        # Sibling materials lock different ProductionRuns. Serialize their
        # shared budget check and reservation on the aggregate before reading
        # charges (MySQL runtime contract requires READ COMMITTED).
        if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "sqlite":
            db.execute(text(
                "UPDATE baseline_regression_runs SET id = id WHERE id = :run_id"
            ), {"run_id": baseline_run_id})
        owner = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun.id).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.id == baseline_run_id
        ).with_for_update())
        if owner is None:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_baseline_identity_missing", "真实模型预算所属回归不存在", status_code=409,
            )
    node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if not isinstance(node_id, str) or node_id != attempt.step_key:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_node_attempt_mismatch",
            "提示词节点与运行尝试不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    frozen_nodes = (
        ((stored_snapshot.get("workflow") or {}).get("manifest") or {}).get(
            "nodes"
        )
        if isinstance(stored_snapshot.get("workflow"), Mapping)
        else None
    )
    frozen_node = next(
        (
            item
            for item in frozen_nodes
            if isinstance(item, Mapping)
            and item.get("node_instance_id") == node_id
        ),
        None,
    ) if isinstance(frozen_nodes, list) else None
    executable = (
        prompt_execution_node(frozen_node)
        if isinstance(frozen_node, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if executable is None or canonical_json(dict(node)) != canonical_json(
        executable
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_node_snapshot_mismatch",
            "提示词节点与冻结工作流不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key == invocation_key,
        )
    )
    quota_owner_key = f"workflow:{run.id}:attempt:{attempt.id}"
    if invocation_key != "root":
        invocation_digest = hashlib.sha256(
            invocation_key.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        quota_owner_key = f"{quota_owner_key}:call:{invocation_digest}"
    limits_value = runtime.get("real_execution_node_limits")
    limits = (
        limits_value.get(node_id)
        if isinstance(limits_value, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if not isinstance(limits, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_node_limits_missing",
            "提示词节点没有冻结调用额度",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if transport_mode not in {None, "batch", "online"}:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_transport_mode_invalid", "模型传输方式不合法", status_code=409)
    if transport_mode == "online":
        route = runtime.get("inference_route")
        policy = route.get("fallback_policy") if isinstance(route, Mapping) else None
        online_route = policy.get("online_route") if isinstance(policy, Mapping) else None
        online_limits = limits.get("online_fallback_limits")
        if (not isinstance(policy, Mapping)
                or policy.get("version") != "batch-online-fallback-v1"
                or route.get("mode") != "batch"
                or not isinstance(online_route, Mapping)
                or online_route.get("mode") != "online"
                or not endpoint_id or endpoint_id != online_route.get("endpoint_id")
                or not endpoint_id.startswith("ep-") or endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-")
                or online_route.get("foundation_model") != limits.get("model_id")
                or not isinstance(online_limits, Mapping)):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_online_fallback_invalid", "在线兜底缺少匹配的冻结路由或计价", status_code=409)
        limits = online_limits
    reserved_input = _positive_budget_value(limits, "max_input_tokens")
    reserved_output = _positive_budget_value(limits, "max_output_tokens")
    estimated_cost = _positive_budget_value(limits, "estimated_cost_micros")
    pricing_quote = _pricing_quote_from_limits(
        limits,
        input_tokens=reserved_input,
        output_tokens=reserved_output,
    )
    if pricing_quote.total_cost_micros != estimated_cost:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "冻结模型计价无法复算预估费用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    # 多次采样投票 / 逐张锚点比较：一次节点调用发 call_count 次请求，预留与预估按次数放大
    # （计价仍按单次请求的档位；用量结算按合计校验）。
    call_count = limits.get("call_count", 1)
    if isinstance(call_count, bool) or not isinstance(call_count, int) or call_count < 1 or call_count > 128:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_call_count_invalid", "冻结的节点请求次数不合法", status_code=409)
    if call_count > 1:
        reserved_input *= call_count
        reserved_output *= call_count
        estimated_cost *= call_count
    provider = limits.get("provider")
    model_id = limits.get("model_id")
    source_model_config_id = limits.get("source_model_config_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(provider, str)
        or not provider
        or not isinstance(model_id, str)
        or not model_id
        or not isinstance(source_model_config_id, int)
        or isinstance(source_model_config_id, bool)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_identity_not_frozen",
            "提示词节点没有冻结模型身份",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if existing is not None:
        if dispatch_identity is not None:
            existing_evidence = _evidence_object(existing)
            reservation_evidence = existing_evidence.get("reservation")
            if (
                not isinstance(reservation_evidence, Mapping)
                or reservation_evidence.get("dispatch_identity")
                != dispatch_identity
            ):
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict",
                    "相同节点尝试已绑定不同的模型发送身份",
                    status_code=409,
                )
        if (
            existing.production_run_id != run.id
            or existing.node_instance_id != node_id
            or (transport_mode is not None and existing.endpoint_id != endpoint_id)
            or existing.provider != provider
            or existing.model_id != model_id
            or existing.reserved_input_tokens != reserved_input
            or existing.reserved_output_tokens != reserved_output
            or existing.estimated_cost_micros != estimated_cost
            or existing.pricing_snapshot_json
            != canonical_json(pricing_quote.snapshot)
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同节点尝试已绑定不同的模型调用身份",
                status_code=409,
            )
        if existing.status == "reserved" and existing.quota_pool_id is not None:
            try:
                quota = acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=existing.quota_pool_id,
                    owner_key=quota_owner_key,
                    reserved_tokens=reserved_input + reserved_output,
                    reserved_images=reserved_images,
                    concurrency_headroom=_quota_concurrency_headroom(
                        db, existing.quota_pool_id, runtime=runtime,
                        transport_mode=transport_mode,
                        endpoint_id=existing.endpoint_id or endpoint_id,
                    ),
                )
            except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    exc.code,
                    str(exc),
                    status_code=409,
                    retryable=exc.retryable,
                    retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
                    pool_key=exc.pool_key,
                ) from exc
            quota = _protect_fallback_quota_lease(db, quota, runtime, transport_mode)
            existing.quota_lease_token = quota.lease_token
            db.flush()
        return existing
    # Final admission fence also covers a decision made before an operator
    # enabled the experiment. Policy changes take the same run lock.
    from .batch_experiment import batch_only_experiment, HOLD_CODE
    if (batch_only_experiment(db, run)
            and (transport_mode != 'batch' or not endpoint_id or not endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-'))):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(HOLD_CODE,
            '批量并发实验已暂停本素材的在线调用，保留任务等待处理',
            status_code=409, retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=900)
    incremental_budget_date: date | None = None
    deferred_ledger: dict[str, int] | None = None
    if execution_kind == "incremental":
        if INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED:
            # 开门槛必须先判再预留，锁读留在前面。
            incremental_budget_date = _enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
                db,
                reserved_input=reserved_input,
                reserved_output=reserved_output,
                estimated_cost=estimated_cost,
            )
        else:
            # 只记账时把 upsert 推到本函数末尾（配额池行锁之后、flush 之前）。
            # InnoDB 的行锁一直握到提交，upsert 放在这里会把后面整段预算投影、
            # 配额池加锁都串在「每 UTC 日一行」上；挪到末尾后它只叠在已有的
            # 配额池行锁之后，不再多串行任何工作。
            incremental_budget_date = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
            deferred_ledger = {
                "reserved_input": reserved_input,
                "reserved_output": reserved_output,
                "estimated_cost": estimated_cost,
            }
    budget = runtime.get("real_execution_budget")
    if not isinstance(budget, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_budget_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型预算快照缺失",
            status_code=409,
        )
    charged = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            ledger_scope,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status.in_(
                {"reserved", "succeeded", "failed"}
            ),
        )
    ).all()
    from . import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery
    try:
        bounded_recovery = (
            recovery.policy(runtime) is not None and transport_mode in {"batch", "online"}
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_transport_policy_invalid", "冻结批量兜底参数不合法", status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    from .failure_recovery import recovery_authority
    try:
        operator_recovery = recovery_authority(db, run) if execution_kind == "incremental" else None
    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_recovery_authority_invalid", "恢复授权损坏，未发起调用", status_code=409) from exc
    if operator_recovery is not None:
        budget = operator_recovery["budget"]
    # run328: four sibling 429s killed 977 materials, including 961 never sent.
    # Frozen transport retries/24h fallback already bound recoverable errors;
    # the aggregate breaker must not preempt them. Reuse the locked budget
    # ledger, retaining EVERY charge below, including unknown liabilities.
    recent_terminal = []
    for item in sorted(charged, key=lambda item: item.id, reverse=True):
        if operator_recovery is not None and item.id <= operator_recovery["invocation_watermark"]:
            continue  # Only the failure streak restarts; all charges below remain counted.
        if item.status not in {"succeeded", "failed"} or item.error_code == INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE_CODE:
            continue
        if bounded_recovery and item.status == "failed":
            if item.error_code in recovery.RECOVERABLE_FAILURE_CODES:
                continue
            if item.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown":
                failure = _evidence_object(item).get("failure")
                # A transport timeout can recover; a DB settlement failure
                # without explicit retryability must still stop spending.
                if isinstance(failure, Mapping) and failure.get("retryable") is True:
                    continue
        recent_terminal.append(item)
        if len(recent_terminal) == _failure_streak_limit():
            break
    if len(recent_terminal) == _failure_streak_limit() and all(
        item.status == "failed" for item in recent_terminal
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_failure_streak_exhausted",
            f"连续{_failure_streak_limit()}次模型调用失败，后续请求暂停等待核验；未调用的素材保留待处理",
            status_code=409,
            retryable=True,
            retry_after_seconds=900,
        )
    cost_gate_event = None
    if baseline_run_id is not None and transport_mode == 'batch' and paid_correction_checkpoint is None:
        from .batch_experiment import experiment_cost_gate_event
        try:
            cost_gate_event = experiment_cost_gate_event(db, run, baseline_run_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError('model_budget_authority_invalid',
                '实验预算授权与冻结运行不一致，未发起模型请求', status_code=409) from exc
    # A confirmed HTTP 429 never entered inference: the provider refused it at
    # the entrance and bills nothing, and transport_decision already exempts it
    # from the batch/online attempts this frozen budget was sized for. Counting
    # it here as a request plus its full reserved liability parked materials in
    # model_budget_wait for good after ~7 overload rejections (2026-09-17: 35 of
    # 96 in-flight slots). The ledger rows and their reservation evidence stay.
    budgeted = [item for item in charged if not recovery.is_resource_rejection(item)]
    charged_usage = [_invocation_budget_usage(item) for item in budgeted]
    checks = (
        (
            "max_requests",
            len(budgeted) + 1,
            "model_budget_requests_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            "max_input_tokens",
            sum(item[0] for item in charged_usage) + reserved_input,
            "model_budget_input_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            "max_output_tokens",
            sum(item[1] for item in charged_usage) + reserved_output,
            "model_budget_output_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            "max_cost_micros",
            sum(item[2] for item in charged_usage) + estimated_cost,
            "model_budget_cost_exhausted",
        ),
    )
    for key, projected, code in checks:
        if key == 'max_cost_micros' and cost_gate_event is not None:
            continue
        if paid_correction_checkpoint is not None:
            # 纠偏重跑这条路上，MAX_REAL_* 不是「压在预算之上的天花板」，它本身
            # 就是预算——本轮的冻结额度早已被原始运行用掉，这里拿全局值当一份
            # 新额度。所以这一处保留：关掉它等于纠偏重跑完全没有上限，而它从来
            # 没有挡过谁（一次纠偏就几个调用）。
            limit = {
                "max_requests": MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
                "max_input_tokens": MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
                "max_output_tokens": MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
                "max_cost_micros": MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS,
            }[key]
        else:
            limit = _positive_budget_value(budget, key)
            # 本轮的冻结预算照旧生效；只是不再额外被全局天花板压一道。
            if key == "max_cost_micros" and REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED:
                limit = min(limit, MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS)
        if projected > limit:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                code,
                "真实模型评测预算不足，未发起本次模型请求",
                status_code=409,
            )
    model_config = db.get(ModelConfig, source_model_config_id)
    if model_config is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_source_unavailable",
            "提示词节点冻结的模型来源不可用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    quota = None
    if model_config.quota_pool_id is not None:
        try:
            quota = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=model_config.quota_pool_id,
                owner_key=quota_owner_key,
                reserved_tokens=reserved_input + reserved_output,
                reserved_images=reserved_images,
                concurrency_headroom=_quota_concurrency_headroom(
                    db, model_config.quota_pool_id, runtime=runtime,
                    transport_mode=transport_mode, endpoint_id=endpoint_id,
                ),
                batch_submission=provider == 'doubao' and bool(endpoint_id and endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-')),
                submission_estimate=submission_estimate,
                fair_ticket=fair_ticket,
                fairness_exempt=paid_correction_checkpoint is not None,
            )
        except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
                retryable=exc.retryable,
                retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
                pool_key=exc.pool_key,
            ) from exc
    if quota is not None:
        quota = _protect_fallback_quota_lease(db, quota, runtime, transport_mode)
    evidence = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-invocation-v1",
        "reservation": {
            "node_instance_id": node_id,
            "invocation_key": invocation_key,
            "provider": provider,
            "model_id": model_id,
            "max_input_tokens": reserved_input,
            "max_output_tokens": reserved_output,
            "estimated_cost_micros": estimated_cost,
            "call_count": call_count,
            "pricing_version": pricing_quote.version,
            "pricing_tier_key": pricing_quote.tier_key,
            "pricing_contract_hash": pricing_quote.contract_hash,
            "snapshot_hash": hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(dict(executable)).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest(),
        },
    }
    if dispatch_identity is not None:
        evidence["reservation"]["dispatch_identity"] = dispatch_identity
    if transport_mode is not None:
        evidence["reservation"]["transport_mode"] = transport_mode
    if endpoint_id is not None:
        evidence["reservation"]["endpoint_id"] = endpoint_id
    if cost_gate_event is not None:
        evidence['reservation']['cost_gate_override_audit_id'] = cost_gate_event.id
    if paid_correction_checkpoint is not None:
        evidence["reservation"]["paid_correction"] = {
            "real_execution_confirmed": True,
            "correction_key": paid_correction_checkpoint["correction_key"],
            "budget_scope": "explicit_confirmation_with_global_hard_caps",
        }
    if incremental_budget_date is not None:
        evidence["reservation"]["incremental_daily_budget"] = {
            "business_date_utc": incremental_budget_date.isoformat(),
            "scope": "incremental_real",
            "request_count": 1,
            "reserved_input_tokens": reserved_input,
            "reserved_output_tokens": reserved_output,
            "estimated_cost_micros": estimated_cost,
        }
    if quota is not None:
        if quota.submission_state is not None:
            evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'] = quota.submission_state
        evidence["reservation"]["quota_pool"] = {
            "pool_id": quota.pool_id,
            "pool_key": quota.pool_key,
            "reserved_images": quota.reserved_images,
            "reserved_tokens": quota.reserved_tokens,
            "lease_expires_at": platform_isoformat(quota.expires_at),
        }
    invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
        baseline_run_id=baseline_run_id,
        production_run_id=run.id,
        step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
        invocation_key=invocation_key,
        node_instance_id=node_id,
        status="reserved",
        provider=provider,
        model_id=model_id,
        endpoint_id=endpoint_id,
        quota_pool_id=quota.pool_id if quota is not None else None,
        quota_lease_token=quota.lease_token if quota is not None else None,
        reserved_input_tokens=reserved_input,
        reserved_output_tokens=reserved_output,
        estimated_cost_micros=estimated_cost,
        pricing_snapshot_json=canonical_json(pricing_quote.snapshot),
        pricing_tier_key=pricing_quote.tier_key,
        evidence_json=canonical_json(evidence),
    )
    db.add(invocation)
    if deferred_ledger is not None:
        _count_incremental_daily_ledger(
            db, business_date_utc=incremental_budget_date, **deferred_ledger
        )
    db.flush()
    return invocation


def _locked_invocation(
    db: Session,
    invocation_id: int,
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    """Serialize terminal transitions in run -> attempt -> invocation order."""

    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    initial = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
    if initial is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_missing",
            "模型调用台账记录不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        # SQLite ignores SELECT .. FOR UPDATE.  BEGIN IMMEDIATE is preferred
        # when this is the transaction's first statement; a no-op status write
        # upgrades an already-open deferred transaction to a writer.
        _begin_immediate_if_needed(db)
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial.production_run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == initial.step_attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None or attempt is None or attempt.run_id != run.id:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_owner_missing",
            "模型调用关联的运行或步骤不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        db.execute(
            text(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations "
                "SET status = status WHERE id = :invocation_id"
            ),
            {"invocation_id": invocation_id},
        )
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowModelInvocation)
        .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id == invocation_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if row is None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_missing",
            "模型调用台账记录不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if row.finished_at is not None and row.finished_at.tzinfo is None:
        # SQLite and MySQL DATETIME values are returned without timezone
        # metadata even though this audit timestamp is always stored as UTC.
        # Normalize the locked reload so an idempotent replay cannot change
        # the observable evidence representation within the same transaction.
        row.finished_at = row.finished_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return row


def _release_invocation_quota(
    db: Session,
    row: WorkflowModelInvocation,
) -> None:
    if row.quota_lease_token is None:
        return
    try:
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=row.quota_lease_token)
    except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=409,
            retryable=exc.retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
        ) from exc


def _evidence_object(row: WorkflowModelInvocation) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        current = json.loads(row.evidence_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_evidence_invalid",
            "模型调用证据损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(current, dict):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_evidence_invalid",
            "模型调用证据必须是对象",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return current


def _merged_evidence(
    row: WorkflowModelInvocation,
    key: str,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> str:
    current = _evidence_object(row)
    current[key] = dict(evidence)
    return canonical_json(current)


def _evidence_matches(
    row: WorkflowModelInvocation, section: str, evidence: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> bool:
    """幂等比较只看结算事实；`detail`（异常链诊断文本）不参与比较。"""
    try:
        stored = json.loads(row.evidence_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False
    existing = stored.get(section)
    if not isinstance(existing, Mapping):
        return False
    strip = lambda value: {k: v for k, v in value.items() if k != "detail"}
    return canonical_json(strip(existing)) == canonical_json(strip(evidence))


def _result_evidence_matches(
    row: WorkflowModelInvocation,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    allow_legacy_unreported: bool,
) -> bool:
    """Allow only the cache-neutral evidence shape added after legacy rows."""

    if _evidence_matches(row, "result", evidence):
        return True
    if not allow_legacy_unreported:
        return False
    cache_observation = evidence.get("cache_observation")
    if not isinstance(cache_observation, Mapping) or canonical_json(
        cache_observation
    ) != canonical_json(
        {
            "status": "unreported",
            "cached_input_tokens": None,
            "source": None,
        }
    ):
        return False
    try:
        stored = json.loads(row.evidence_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False
    existing = stored.get("result")
    if not isinstance(existing, Mapping) or "cache_observation" in existing:
        return False
    compatible = dict(evidence)
    compatible.pop("cache_observation", None)
    strip = lambda value: {k: v for k, v in value.items() if k != "detail"}
    return canonical_json(strip(existing)) == canonical_json(strip(compatible))


def _pricing_settlement_matches(
