    row: WorkflowModelInvocation,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    allow_legacy_v1: bool,
) -> bool:
    if _evidence_matches(row, "pricing_settlement", evidence):
        return True
    if not allow_legacy_v1:
        return False
    try:
        stored = json.loads(row.evidence_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False
    existing = stored.get("pricing_settlement")
    if (
        not isinstance(existing, Mapping)
        or existing.get("schema_version")
        != "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v1"
    ):
        return False
    legacy_equivalent = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v1",
        **{
            key: evidence.get(key)
            for key in (
                "pricing_version",
                "pricing_tier_key",
                "pricing_contract_hash",
                "input_tokens",
                "output_tokens",
                "actual_cost_micros",
            )
        },
    }
    stripped_existing = {
        key: value for key, value in existing.items() if key != "detail"
    }
    return canonical_json(stripped_existing) == canonical_json(legacy_equivalent)


def _cache_settlement_inputs(
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    cached_input_tokens: int | None,
    cache_observation_status: str,
    pricing_version: str,
) -> tuple[int | None, int, str]:
    statuses = {"hit", "miss", "unreported", "invalid"}
    if cache_observation_status not in statuses:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_cache_usage_invalid",
            "模型缓存观测状态不合法",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if cache_observation_status in {"hit", "miss"}:
        if (
            not isinstance(cached_input_tokens, int)
            or isinstance(cached_input_tokens, bool)
            or cached_input_tokens < 0
            or cached_input_tokens > input_tokens
            or (cache_observation_status == "hit" and cached_input_tokens == 0)
            or (cache_observation_status == "miss" and cached_input_tokens != 0)
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_cache_usage_invalid",
                "模型缓存 token 与观测状态不一致",
                status_code=409,
            )
    elif cached_input_tokens is not None:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_cache_usage_invalid",
            "未上报或无效的缓存观测不能携带缓存 token",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if pricing_version == "model-pricing-v3" and cache_observation_status in {
        "hit",
        "miss",
    }:
        return (
            cached_input_tokens,
            int(cached_input_tokens),
            "provider_cache_reported",
        )
    if pricing_version == "model-pricing-v3":
        return cached_input_tokens, 0, "conservative_full_input"
    return cached_input_tokens, 0, "legacy_no_cache_rate"


def _reserved_call_count(row: WorkflowModelInvocation) -> int:
    """冻结在预留证据里的节点请求次数（投票 / 成对比较）；缺失或异常按 1。"""

    try:
        reservation = _evidence_object(row).get("reservation")
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError:
        return 1
    value = reservation.get("call_count", 1) if isinstance(reservation, Mapping) else 1
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 1 or value > 128:
        return 1
    return value


def _split_evenly(total: int, parts: int) -> list[int]:
    base, remainder = divmod(total, parts)
    return [base + (1 if index < remainder else 0) for index in range(parts)]


def _allocate_cached_tokens(cached: int, inputs: list[int]) -> list[int]:
    """把合计缓存 token 按各请求输入比例分摊，保证每份不超过该请求输入。"""

    total_input = sum(inputs)
    if cached <= 0 or total_input <= 0:
        return [0 for _ in inputs]
    shares = [cached * value // total_input for value in inputs]
    left = cached - sum(shares)
    for index, value in enumerate(inputs):
        if left <= 0:
            break
        room = min(value - shares[index], left)
        shares[index] += room
        left -= room
    return shares


def _settlement_request_usages(
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    call_count: int,
    request_usages: Any,
) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """一次节点调用里每个供应商请求的 (输入, 输出) token。

    阶梯价按单个请求的输入长度选档；多次请求的节点（投票 / 成对比较）不能把合计
    当成一次请求选档。优先用执行器上报的逐请求用量；只有合计时按冻结次数均分，
    与预留“单次档位 × 次数”的口径一致。
    """

    if request_usages is not None:
        if not isinstance(request_usages, (list, tuple)) or not request_usages:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_usage_invalid", "逐请求用量必须是非空列表", status_code=409)
        pairs: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        for entry in request_usages:
            values = (
                (entry.get("input_tokens"), entry.get("output_tokens"))
                if isinstance(entry, Mapping)
                else (None, None)
            )
            if any(not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool) or v < 0 for v in values):
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_usage_invalid", "逐请求用量不合法", status_code=409)
            pairs.append((int(values[0]), int(values[1])))
        if sum(p[0] for p in pairs) != input_tokens or sum(p[1] for p in pairs) != output_tokens:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_usage_invalid", "逐请求用量与合计用量不一致", status_code=409)
        return pairs
    if call_count <= 1:
        return [(input_tokens, output_tokens)]
    return list(zip(_split_evenly(input_tokens, call_count), _split_evenly(output_tokens, call_count)))


def _price_request_usages(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    requests: list[tuple[int, int]],
    cached_input_tokens: int,
) -> tuple[_PricingQuote, list[dict[str, Any]] | None]:
    if len(requests) == 1 or snapshot.get("schema_version") not in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS:
        # 单次请求或兼容平价（与输入长度无关）：保持原有合计计价。
        return (
            _price_frozen_snapshot(
                snapshot,
                input_tokens=sum(r[0] for r in requests),
                output_tokens=sum(r[1] for r in requests),
                cached_input_tokens=cached_input_tokens,
            ),
            None,
        )
    cached_shares = _allocate_cached_tokens(cached_input_tokens, [r[0] for r in requests])
    quotes = [
        _price_frozen_snapshot(
            snapshot,
            input_tokens=request_input,
            output_tokens=request_output,
            cached_input_tokens=cached_share,
        )
        for (request_input, request_output), cached_share in zip(requests, cached_shares)
    ]
    tiers: list[str] = []
    for quote in quotes:
        if quote.tier_key not in tiers:
            tiers.append(quote.tier_key)
    first = quotes[0]
    total = lambda name: sum(getattr(quote, name) for quote in quotes)  # noqa: E731
    aggregate = _PricingQuote(
        snapshot=first.snapshot,
        version=first.version,
        tier_key="+".join(tiers)[:80],
        input_tokens=total("input_tokens"),
        cached_input_tokens=total("cached_input_tokens"),
        uncached_input_tokens=total("uncached_input_tokens"),
        output_tokens=total("output_tokens"),
        uncached_input_cost_micros=total("uncached_input_cost_micros"),
        cached_input_cost_micros=total("cached_input_cost_micros"),
        input_cost_micros=total("input_cost_micros"),
        output_cost_micros=total("output_cost_micros"),
        total_cost_micros=total("total_cost_micros"),
        full_price_cost_micros=total("full_price_cost_micros"),
        cache_savings_micros=total("cache_savings_micros"),
        contract_hash=first.contract_hash,
    )
    per_request = [
        {
            "input_tokens": quote.input_tokens,
            "cached_input_tokens": quote.cached_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": quote.output_tokens,
            "pricing_tier_key": quote.tier_key,
            "cost_micros": quote.total_cost_micros,
        }
        for quote in quotes
    ]
    return aggregate, per_request


def settle_model_invocation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    invocation_id: int,
    request_correlation_id: str | None,
    upstream_status_code: int | None,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any],
    cached_input_tokens: int | None = None,
    cache_observation_status: str = "unreported",
    request_usages: Any = None,
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    """Settle one reserved provider call with its actual, safe evidence.

    ``request_usages`` 是多次请求节点逐请求的 ``{input_tokens, output_tokens}``；
    阶梯价逐请求选档后求和。缺省时按冻结的 ``call_count`` 均分合计用量。
    """

    if input_tokens < 0 or output_tokens < 0:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_usage_invalid",
            "模型 token 用量不能为负数",
            status_code=409,
        )
    row = _locked_invocation(db, invocation_id)
    try:
        pricing_snapshot = json.loads(row.pricing_snapshot_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "模型调用冻结计价快照损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(pricing_snapshot, dict):
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "模型调用冻结计价快照必须是对象",
            status_code=409,
        )
    pricing_version = pricing_snapshot.get("schema_version")
    reported_cached_tokens, billed_cached_tokens, settlement_basis = (
        _cache_settlement_inputs(
            input_tokens=input_tokens,
            cached_input_tokens=cached_input_tokens,
            cache_observation_status=cache_observation_status,
            pricing_version=(
                pricing_version if isinstance(pricing_version, str) else ""
            ),
        )
    )
    settlement_requests = _settlement_request_usages(
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
        call_count=_reserved_call_count(row),
        request_usages=request_usages,
    )
    actual_quote, per_request_pricing = (
        _price_request_usages(
            pricing_snapshot,
            requests=settlement_requests,
            cached_input_tokens=billed_cached_tokens,
        )
        if pricing_snapshot
        else (None, None)
    )
    actual_cost_micros = (
        actual_quote.total_cost_micros if actual_quote is not None else 0
    )
    actual_tier_key = (
        actual_quote.tier_key if actual_quote is not None else row.pricing_tier_key
    )
    pricing_evidence = (
        {
            "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2",
            "settlement_basis": settlement_basis,
            "cache_observation_status": cache_observation_status,
            "reported_cached_input_tokens": reported_cached_tokens,
            "pricing_version": actual_quote.version,
            "pricing_tier_key": actual_quote.tier_key,
            "pricing_contract_hash": actual_quote.contract_hash,
            "input_tokens": actual_quote.input_tokens,
            "cached_input_tokens": actual_quote.cached_input_tokens,
            "uncached_input_tokens": actual_quote.uncached_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": actual_quote.output_tokens,
            "uncached_input_cost_micros": (
                actual_quote.uncached_input_cost_micros
            ),
            "cached_input_cost_micros": (
                actual_quote.cached_input_cost_micros
            ),
            "input_cost_micros": actual_quote.input_cost_micros,
            "output_cost_micros": actual_quote.output_cost_micros,
            "full_price_cost_micros": actual_quote.full_price_cost_micros,
            "cache_savings_micros": actual_quote.cache_savings_micros,
            "actual_cost_micros": actual_cost_micros,
            **(
                {
                    "request_count": len(per_request_pricing),
                    "per_request": per_request_pricing,
                }
                if per_request_pricing is not None
                else {}
            ),
        }
        if actual_quote is not None
        else None
    )
    usage_exceeds_reservation = (
        input_tokens > row.reserved_input_tokens
        or output_tokens > row.reserved_output_tokens
    )
    terminal_status = "failed" if usage_exceeds_reservation else "succeeded"
    terminal_error_code = (
        "model_usage_exceeds_reservation" if usage_exceeds_reservation else ""
    )
    if row.status in {"succeeded", "failed"}:
        if (
            row.status == terminal_status
            and row.error_code == terminal_error_code
            and row.request_correlation_id == request_correlation_id
            and row.upstream_status_code == upstream_status_code
            and row.input_tokens == input_tokens
            and row.output_tokens == output_tokens
            and row.total_tokens == input_tokens + output_tokens
            and row.actual_cost_micros == actual_cost_micros
            and row.pricing_tier_key == actual_tier_key
            and _result_evidence_matches(
                row,
                evidence,
                allow_legacy_unreported=(
                    cache_observation_status == "unreported"
                    and cached_input_tokens is None
                ),
            )
            and (
                pricing_evidence is None
                or _pricing_settlement_matches(
                    row,
                    pricing_evidence,
                    allow_legacy_v1=(
                        cache_observation_status == "unreported"
                        and cached_input_tokens is None
                    ),
                )
            )
        ):
            return row
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict",
            "相同模型调用已使用不同结果完成结算",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if row.status != "reserved":
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_settlement_conflict",
            "当前模型调用状态不能结算为成功",
            status_code=409,
        )
    _release_invocation_quota(db, row)
    row.request_correlation_id = request_correlation_id
    row.upstream_status_code = upstream_status_code
    row.input_tokens = input_tokens
    row.cached_input_tokens = int(billed_cached_tokens)
    row.output_tokens = output_tokens
    row.total_tokens = input_tokens + output_tokens
    row.evidence_json = _merged_evidence(row, "result", evidence)
    if pricing_evidence is not None:
        row.evidence_json = _merged_evidence(
            row,
            "pricing_settlement",
            pricing_evidence,
        )
    row.actual_cost_micros = actual_cost_micros
    row.pricing_tier_key = actual_tier_key
    row.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if usage_exceeds_reservation:
        row.status = "failed"
        row.error_code = "model_usage_exceeds_reservation"
        row.error_message = "模型实际 token 用量超过冻结预留上限"
    else:
        row.status = "succeeded"
        row.error_code = ""
        row.error_message = ""
    db.flush()
    return row


def fail_model_invocation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    invocation_id: int,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    request_correlation_id: str | None = None,
    upstream_status_code: int | None = None,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    provider_dispatched: bool = True,
    provider_usage: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    """Terminalize one reserved provider call without erasing its reservation."""

    row = _locked_invocation(db, invocation_id)
    terminal_status = "failed" if provider_dispatched else "rejected"
    safe_error_code = error_code[:80]
    safe_error_message = error_message[:4_000]
    safe_evidence = dict(evidence or {})
    if provider_usage is not None:
        if not provider_dispatched:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError("model_usage_invalid", "未发出的请求不能包含供应商用量")
        safe_evidence["provider_usage"] = dict(provider_usage)
    if row.status in {"failed", "rejected"}:
        if (
            row.status == terminal_status
            and row.error_code == safe_error_code
            and row.error_message == safe_error_message
            and row.request_correlation_id == request_correlation_id
            and row.upstream_status_code == upstream_status_code
            and _evidence_matches(row, "failure", safe_evidence)
        ):
            return row
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict",
            "相同模型调用已使用不同错误完成结算",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if row.status != "reserved":
        raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
            "model_invocation_failure_conflict",
            "当前模型调用状态不能结算为失败",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if provider_usage is not None:
        settle_model_invocation(db, invocation_id=invocation_id,
            request_correlation_id=request_correlation_id,
            upstream_status_code=upstream_status_code,
            input_tokens=provider_usage["input_tokens"],
            output_tokens=provider_usage["output_tokens"],
            cached_input_tokens=provider_usage.get("cached_input_tokens"),
            cache_observation_status=provider_usage.get("cache_observation_status", "unreported"),
            request_usages=provider_usage.get("request_usages"),
            evidence={"provider_failure": safe_error_code})
    if not provider_dispatched:
        current_evidence = _evidence_object(row)
        reservation = current_evidence.get("reservation")
        daily = (
            reservation.get("incremental_daily_budget")
            if isinstance(reservation, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if isinstance(daily, Mapping):
            expected = {
                "scope": "incremental_real",
                "request_count": 1,
                "reserved_input_tokens": row.reserved_input_tokens,
                "reserved_output_tokens": row.reserved_output_tokens,
                "estimated_cost_micros": row.estimated_cost_micros,
            }
            if any(daily.get(key) != value for key, value in expected.items()):
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    "incremental_daily_budget_evidence_invalid",
                    "无人值守增量评测日账本预留证据损坏",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            raw_date = daily.get("business_date_utc")
            try:
                business_date_utc = date.fromisoformat(raw_date)
            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    "incremental_daily_budget_evidence_invalid",
                    "无人值守增量评测日账本日期证据损坏",
                    status_code=409,
                ) from exc
            ledger = _lock_incremental_daily_ledger(
                db,
                business_date_utc=business_date_utc,
            )
            amounts = {
                "request_count": 1,
                "reserved_input_tokens": row.reserved_input_tokens,
                "reserved_output_tokens": row.reserved_output_tokens,
                "estimated_cost_micros": row.estimated_cost_micros,
            }
            if any(
                getattr(ledger, key) < amount
                for key, amount in amounts.items()
            ):
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    "incremental_daily_budget_release_underflow",
                    "无人值守增量评测日账本回退将产生负数，已拒绝结算",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            for key, amount in amounts.items():
                setattr(ledger, key, getattr(ledger, key) - amount)
            current_evidence["daily_budget_release"] = {
                "business_date_utc": business_date_utc.isoformat(),
                "scope": "incremental_real",
                "reason": "provider_not_dispatched",
                **amounts,
            }
            row.evidence_json = canonical_json(current_evidence)
    from .batch_submission import is_scoped_batch_rejection
    if (row.quota_pool_id is not None and upstream_status_code == 429
            and not is_scoped_batch_rejection(row, safe_evidence)):
        raw_retry_after = safe_evidence.get("retry_after_seconds")
        retry_after = (
            raw_retry_after
            if isinstance(raw_retry_after, int)
            and not isinstance(raw_retry_after, bool)
            and raw_retry_after > 0
            else 60
        )
        if row.provider == "doubao":
            # A short provider hint is a minimum, not a signal that capacity
            # has recovered. Cool the shared pool so sibling workers do not
            # immediately replace a rejected request. Preserve longer hints.
            retry_after = max(60, retry_after)
        try:
            cool_down_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=row.quota_pool_id,
                retry_after_seconds=retry_after,
                reason="provider_rate_limited",
            )
        except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
                retryable=exc.retryable,
                retry_after_seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds,
            ) from exc
    _release_invocation_quota(db, row)
    row.status = terminal_status
    row.error_code = safe_error_code
    row.error_message = safe_error_message
    row.request_correlation_id = request_correlation_id
    row.upstream_status_code = upstream_status_code
    row.evidence_json = _merged_evidence(row, "failure", safe_evidence)
    row.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    db.flush()
    return row

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_model_executor.py =====

class WorkflowModelExecutorError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        retryable: bool = False,
        evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.evidence = dict(evidence or {})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreparedPromptInvocation:
    run_id: int
    attempt_id: int
    node_instance_id: str
    invocation_key: str
    node: Mapping[str, Any]
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
    input_fingerprint: str
    attempt_no: int
    started_at: datetime | None
    system_prompt: str
    user_prompt: str
    image_path: Path | None
    mime_type: str | None
    output_budget: int
    reasoning_effort: str | None
    structured_output: bool
    output_contract: PromptOutputContract
    component_hash: str
    model_snapshot_hash: str
    output_contract_key: str | None
    expected_asset_id: str | None
    client_config: Any = field(repr=False)
    media_bundle: PreparedMediaBundle = field(repr=False)
    media_dispatch_identities: tuple[str, ...]
    media_pricing_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]
    image_samples: tuple[
        ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None], ...
    ] | None
    serialized_provider_request: SerializedDoubaoRequest | None = field(
        repr=False
    )
    dispatch_identity: str
    vision_policy_hash: str | None
    endpoint_id: str | None = None
    joint_input_identity: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    retry_feedback: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RealPromptResult:
    node_result: dict[str, Any]
    invocation_evidence: dict[str, Any]
    request_correlation_id: str | None
    upstream_status_code: int | None
    input_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    total_tokens: int
    media_usage: dict[str, Any]
    cache_usage: CacheUsageObservation
    # 逐请求 {input_tokens, output_tokens}：阶梯价按单个请求选档（投票/成对比较）。
    request_usages: tuple[dict[str, int], ...] | None = None
    # media_usage 所属那次请求的输入 token（图片明细的档位用它，而不是多次请求合计）。
    media_request_input_tokens: int | None = None


def _paid_acceptance_configuration() -> tuple[str, int] | None:
    campaign_id = os.getenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CAMPAIGN_ID", "").strip()
    raw_limit = os.getenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CALL_LIMIT", "").strip()
    if not campaign_id and not raw_limit:
        return None
    if not campaign_id or not raw_limit:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "acceptance_guard_configuration_invalid",
            "付费验收门禁配置不完整，未发起模型请求",
        )
    try:
        limit = int(raw_limit)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "acceptance_guard_configuration_invalid",
            "付费验收调用上限无效，未发起模型请求",
        ) from exc
    return campaign_id, limit


def _reserve_paid_acceptance_call(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
) -> CallReservation | None:
    configuration = _paid_acceptance_configuration()
    if configuration is None:
        return None
    campaign_id, limit = configuration
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        attempt_key = f"attempt:{prepared.attempt_id}"
        if prepared.invocation_key != "root":
            attempt_key += ":call:" + _sha256(prepared.invocation_key)
        reservation = reserve_acceptance_call(
            db,
            campaign_id=campaign_id,
            attempt_key=attempt_key,
            limit=limit,
        )
        if reservation.reused:
            raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
                "acceptance_attempt_already_reserved",
                "相同运行步骤已经预留过付费调用，拒绝重复请求",
            )
        db.commit()
        return reservation
    except PaidAcceptanceGuardError as exc:
        db.rollback()
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    finally:
        db.close()


def _settle_paid_acceptance_call(
    reservation: CallReservation | None,
    *,
    outcome: str,
    provider_correlation_id: str | None,
) -> None:
    if reservation is None:
        return
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        settle_acceptance_call(
            db,
            reservation_id=reservation.reservation_id,
            provider_correlation_id=provider_correlation_id,
            outcome=outcome,
        )
        db.commit()
    except PaidAcceptanceGuardError as exc:
        db.rollback()
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "acceptance_settlement_failed",
            "付费验收调用已发生，但本地门禁结算失败，已停止后续执行",
        ) from exc
    finally:
        db.close()


def _sha256(value: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _json_object(value: str, *, code: str, message: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        decoded = json.loads(value)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(code, message) from exc
    if not isinstance(decoded, dict):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(code, message)
    return decoded


def _mapping(value: Any, *, code: str, message: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(code, message)
    return value


def _frozen_manifest_node(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any], node_instance_id: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    workflow = _mapping(
        snapshot.get("workflow"),
        code="model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行缺少冻结工作流",
    )
    manifest = _mapping(
        workflow.get("manifest"),
        code="model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行缺少冻结工作流定义",
    )
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型运行缺少冻结节点列表",
        )
    frozen = next(
        (
            item
            for item in nodes
            if isinstance(item, Mapping)
            and item.get("node_instance_id") == node_instance_id
        ),
        None,
    )
    if not isinstance(frozen, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_node_missing",
            "当前步骤没有匹配的冻结提示词节点",
        )
    return frozen


def _frozen_node(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any], node_instance_id: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    frozen = _frozen_manifest_node(snapshot, node_instance_id)
    executable = prompt_execution_node(frozen)
    if executable is None:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_node_missing",
            "当前步骤没有匹配的冻结提示词执行器",
        )
    return executable


def _runtime_thinking_controls(
    *,
    provider: str,
    default_thinking_mode: str,
    thinking_level: str,
    thinking_enabled: bool,
) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    # Historical frozen workflow manifests do not contain ``thinking_enabled``.
    # They intentionally resolve to disabled so that thinking is opt-in only.
    if not thinking_enabled or thinking_level == "none":
        return "disabled", None
    if thinking_level == "auto":
        return (
            default_thinking_mode
            if default_thinking_mode in {"auto", "enabled", "disabled"}
            else "auto",
            None,
        )
    if is_volcengine_ark_provider(provider):
        return "enabled", thinking_level
    return "auto", thinking_level


def _previous_output(upstream: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    if len(upstream) == 1:
        return next(iter(upstream.values()))
    return dict(upstream)


def _redact_model_context(value: Any) -> Any:
    safe = redact_workflow_secrets(value)
    if isinstance(safe, Mapping):
        return {
            str(key): _redact_model_context(child)
            for key, child in safe.items()
            if str(key).lower() not in _MODEL_CONTEXT_FORBIDDEN_FIELDS
            and not str(key).lower().startswith("expected_")
            and not str(key).lower().startswith("truth_")
        }
    if isinstance(safe, list):
        return [_redact_model_context(child) for child in safe]
    return safe


def _business_upstream_outputs(
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    upstream = input_manifest.get("upstream")
    if not isinstance(upstream, Mapping):
        return {}
    outputs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for node_id, result in upstream.items():
        if not isinstance(result, Mapping):
            continue
        output = result.get("output")
        if isinstance(output, Mapping):
            outputs[str(node_id)] = _redact_model_context(output)
            continue
        # Compatibility for historical attempts created before runtime-managed
        # nodes adopted the canonical result envelope.  Only the two frozen
        # server-owned schemas are accepted; arbitrary raw predecessor objects
        # remain excluded from provider context.
        if result.get("schema_version") in {
            "workflow-case-aggregate-v1",
            "workflow-foreach-output-v1",
        }:
            outputs[str(node_id)] = _redact_model_context(result)
    return outputs


def _render_prompt(
    template: str,
    replacements: Mapping[str, str],
    *,
    code: str,
) -> str:
    rendered = template
    for placeholder, value in replacements.items():
        rendered = rendered.replace(placeholder, value)
    unresolved = sorted(set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(rendered)))
    if unresolved:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            code,
            "提示词包含无法安全替换的占位符：" + "、".join(unresolved),
        )
    return rendered


def _workflow_manifest(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    workflow = _mapping(
        snapshot.get("workflow"),
        code="model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行缺少冻结工作流",
    )
    return _mapping(
        workflow.get("manifest"),
        code="model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行缺少冻结工作流定义",
    )


def _rule_module_content(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    module_type_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    manifest = _workflow_manifest(snapshot)
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
            "真实模型运行缺少冻结节点列表",
        )
    matches = []
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or config.get(
            "module_type_key"
        ) != module_type_key:
            continue
        matches.append(config)
    if not matches:
        return None
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_rule_module_snapshot_ambiguous",
            f"工作流重复配置规则模块：{module_type_key}",
        )
    config = matches[0]
    component = _mapping(
        config.get("component_snapshot"),
        code="model_rule_module_snapshot_invalid",
        message=f"规则模块 {module_type_key} 缺少冻结内容",
    )
    raw_content = component.get("content_json")
    content_hash = component.get("content_hash")
    if (
        component.get("kind") != "rule_module"
        or component.get("module_type_key") != module_type_key
        or not isinstance(raw_content, str)
        or not isinstance(content_hash, str)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_rule_module_snapshot_invalid",
            f"规则模块 {module_type_key} 的冻结身份损坏",
        )
    content = _json_object(
        raw_content,
        code="model_rule_module_snapshot_invalid",
        message=f"规则模块 {module_type_key} 的冻结内容损坏",
    )
    if canonical_component_hash(content) != content_hash:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_rule_module_snapshot_invalid",
            f"规则模块 {module_type_key} 的冻结摘要损坏",
        )
    return content


def _prompt_stage(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    config = node.get("config")
    component = config.get("component_snapshot") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
    content = component.get("content") if isinstance(component, Mapping) else None
    stage = content.get("stage") if isinstance(content, Mapping) else None
    return str(stage or "")


def _prior_prompt_a_output(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = _workflow_manifest(snapshot)
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []
    prompt_a_ids = []
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        if (
            isinstance(node_id, str)
            and (asset_key == "prompt_a" or _prompt_stage(node) == "A")
        ):
            prompt_a_ids.append(node_id)
    prompt_a_ids = list(dict.fromkeys(prompt_a_ids))
    if len(prompt_a_ids) != 1:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_prompt_a_ambiguous",
            "原生 A/B 评测没有唯一的提示词 A 节点",
        )
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == prompt_a_ids[0],
            ProductionStepAttempt.status == "succeeded",
        )
        .order_by(
            ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc(),
            ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc(),
        )
    ).all()
    if not attempts:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_prompt_a_missing",
            "提示词 B 缺少已成功且可回放的提示词 A 结果",
        )
    prior_attempt = attempts[0]
    if (
        not isinstance(prior_attempt.output_hash, str)
        or _sha256(prior_attempt.output_manifest_json) != prior_attempt.output_hash
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_prompt_a_invalid",
            "提示词 A 的冻结结果摘要不一致",
        )
    result = _json_object(
        prior_attempt.output_manifest_json,
        code="model_authoritative_prompt_a_invalid",
        message="提示词 A 的冻结结果损坏",
    )
    output = result.get("output")
    if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_prompt_a_invalid",
            "提示词 A 的冻结结果不是结构化对象",
        )
    return deepcopy(dict(output))


def _authoritative_prompt_b(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    component: Mapping[str, Any],
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_config: Mapping[str, Any],
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    category_key = str(run.category_key or "")
    from .prompt_precheck_context import precheck_context_mode

    try:
        context_mode = precheck_context_mode(node_config, category_key=category_key)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_precheck_context_invalid", str(exc),
        ) from exc
    if category_key not in {"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su", "model_3d_su_joint"}:
        return None
    if node_config.get("asset_key") != "prompt_b" and content.get("stage") != "B":
        return None

    dimension_content = _rule_module_content(snapshot, "dimension_scoring")
    if not isinstance(dimension_content, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_dimensions_missing",
            "原生 A/B 评测缺少冻结维度计分模块",
        )
    subcategory_dimensions = dimension_content.get("subcategory_dimensions")
    if not isinstance(subcategory_dimensions, Mapping) or not subcategory_dimensions:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_dimensions_invalid",
            "冻结维度计分模块缺少赛道配置",
        )

    raw_precheck = _prior_prompt_a_output(
        db,
        run=run,
        snapshot=snapshot,
    )
    precheck = raw_precheck
    if category_key == "inspiration_image":
        from .schema_adapter import adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck

        precheck = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
            raw_precheck,
            redline_policy=_rule_module_content(
                snapshot, "eligibility_redline"
            ),
        )

    track_key: str | None = None
    track_content = _rule_module_content(snapshot, "track_routing")
    if isinstance(track_content, Mapping):
        classification_map = track_content.get("classification_map")
        track_contract = {
            key: deepcopy(value)
            for key, value in track_content.items()
            if key not in {"classification_map", "track_adjustments"}
        }
        if isinstance(classification_map, dict):
            try:
                from .subcategory_resolver import resolve_subcategory

                track_key = str(
                    resolve_subcategory(
                        precheck,
                        classification_map=classification_map,
                        track_classification=track_contract,
                    )["track_key"]
                )
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                    "model_authoritative_track_unresolved",
                    f"无法按冻结赛道模块解析提示词 B：{exc}",
                ) from exc
    if track_key is None:
        direct = precheck.get("track_classification")
        if isinstance(direct, str) and direct in subcategory_dimensions:
            track_key = direct
    track_config = subcategory_dimensions.get(track_key or "")
    if not isinstance(track_config, dict):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_authoritative_track_unresolved",
            "提示词 A 结果无法匹配冻结的维度赛道",
        )

    system_template = content.get("system_prompt")
    user_template = content.get("user_prompt")
    rubric_version = content.get("rubric_version")
    if not all(
        isinstance(value, str)
        for value in (system_template, user_template, rubric_version)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
            "提示词 B 的冻结正文格式不正确",
        )
    operator_prompt = SimpleNamespace(
        version=str(component.get("version") or ""),
        system_prompt=system_template,
        user_prompt=user_template,
        rubric_version=rubric_version,
    )
    try:
        from .dimension_deduction_bridge import (
            DimensionDeductionBridgeError,
            build_operator_dimension_deduction_prompt,
        )

        # Resolve the frozen track using the full A output first. Only the B
        # transport view is projected; the saved A output and finalizer retain
        # all original eligibility/redline/cap evidence. Project both aliases
        # so {{previous_output}} cannot reintroduce A's subjective judgements.
        prompt_precheck = precheck
        prompt_previous = raw_precheck
        if context_mode == "routing_only_v1":
            prompt_precheck = prompt_previous = {
                "context_mode": context_mode,
                "track_classification": track_key,
            }
        return build_operator_dimension_deduction_prompt(
            track_config,
            operator_prompt,
            precheck=prompt_precheck,
            image_metadata=metadata,
            rubric_version=rubric_version,
            previous_output=canonical_json(prompt_previous),
        )
    except DimensionDeductionBridgeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc


def _prompt_attaches_anchors(
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    authoritative: bool,
) -> bool:
    """锚点图随哪些提示词节点下发。

    权威 B 路径（stage=B）一直带锚点；单提示词版（stage=SINGLE，合并 A/B 职责）
    的正文同样以 5 张锚点为评分基准，却因不走权威 B 组装而从未拿到锚点，
    模型只见待评图一张。锚点是否可用仍由 ``_anchor_calibration`` 模块决定。
    """
    if authoritative:
        return True
    stage = str(content.get("stage") or "").upper()
    if stage == "SINGLE":
        return True
    return str(node_config.get("asset_key") or "") == "latest_single_prompt"


def _anchor_media_items(
    db: Session,
    *,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    target: Path,
    target_mime: str,
) -> tuple[tuple[str, ModelMediaItem], ...] | None:
    content = _rule_module_content(snapshot, "anchor_calibration")
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        return None
    raw_anchors = content.get("anchors")
    if not isinstance(raw_anchors, list):
        return None
    asset_ids = {
        item.get("asset_id")
        for item in raw_anchors
        if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        and isinstance(item.get("asset_id"), int)
        and not isinstance(item.get("asset_id"), bool)
    }
    assets_by_id = {
        asset_id: asset
        for asset_id in asset_ids
        if (asset := db.get(Asset, asset_id)) is not None
    }
    settings = get_settings()
    try:
        from .inspiration_anchor_mechanism import (
            anchor_mechanism_request,
            validate_anchor_mechanism,
        )

        payload = anchor_mechanism_request(
            {"anchor_mechanism": dict(content)},
            target,
            target_mime,
            assets_by_id=assets_by_id,
            asset_path_resolver=lambda anchor_asset: resolve_asset_path(
                anchor_asset, settings
            ),
            reference_image_builder=None,
        )
        view = validate_anchor_mechanism(
            {"anchor_mechanism": dict(content)}
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # anchor contract errors carry a stable code
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            str(getattr(exc, "code", "model_anchor_snapshot_invalid")),
            str(exc),
        ) from exc
    if payload is None:
        return None
    samples, max_image_count = payload
    if max_image_count != len(samples):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_anchor_snapshot_invalid",
            "锚点图请求数量与冻结合同不一致",
        )
    anchors = view.get("anchors") if isinstance(view, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(anchors, list)
        or len(samples) != len(anchors) + 1
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_anchor_snapshot_invalid",
            "锚点图载荷与冻结合同不一致",
        )
    return tuple(
        (
            sample[0],
            ModelMediaItem(
                role="anchor",
                ordinal=index,
                source_path=sample[1],
                mime_type=str(anchor["mime_type"]),
                content_sha256=str(anchor["sha256"]),
                asset_id=int(anchor["asset_id"]),
                anchor_level=str(anchor["level"]),
            ),
        )
        for index, (sample, anchor) in enumerate(
            zip(samples[:-1], anchors, strict=True)
        )
    )


def _knowledge_graph_identity(
    node_config: Mapping[str, Any],
    frozen_input: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
    snapshot = node_config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
    if snapshot is None:
        return None, None
    contract_snapshot = _mapping(
        snapshot,
        code="kg_output_contract_snapshot_invalid",
        message="知识图谱提示词的冻结字段合同损坏",
    )
    contract_hash = contract_snapshot.get("contract_hash")
    canonical_snapshot = dict(contract_snapshot)
    canonical_snapshot.pop("contract_hash", None)
    contract_key = contract_snapshot.get("contract_key")
    if (
        not isinstance(contract_hash, str)
        or hash_manifest(canonical_snapshot) != contract_hash
        or not isinstance(contract_key, str)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "kg_output_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            "知识图谱提示词的冻结字段合同损坏",
        )
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, contract_snapshot)
    route = contract_snapshot.get("route")
    if contract is None or not isinstance(route, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "kg_output_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            "知识图谱提示词引用的字段合同不存在",
        )
    source = frozen_input.get("source")
    is_single = frozen_input.get("is_single")
    if (
        source != contract.site_scope
        or is_single != contract.is_single
        or contract_snapshot.get("identity_field") != contract.identity_field
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "kg_prompt_route_mismatch",
            "知识图谱提示词合同与本次素材路线不一致",
        )
    identity = frozen_input.get(contract.identity_field)
    if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip():
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "kg_input_identity_missing",
            f"知识图谱输入缺少 {contract.identity_field}",
        )
    return contract_key, identity


def _prepared_client_config(
    *,
    current: ModelConfig,
    frozen_runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[Any, int, str | None, bool]:
    output_budget = node_config.get("max_tokens")
    model_limit = frozen_runtime.get("max_tokens")
    temperature = node_config.get(
        "temperature", frozen_runtime.get("temperature")
    )
    structured_output = node_config.get(
        "structured_output", frozen_runtime.get("structured_output")
    )
    thinking_level = node_config.get("thinking_level")
    thinking_enabled = node_config.get("thinking_enabled") is True
    if (
        not isinstance(output_budget, int)
        or isinstance(output_budget, bool)
        or not isinstance(model_limit, int)
        or isinstance(model_limit, bool)
        or not 1 <= output_budget <= model_limit
        or not isinstance(temperature, (int, float))
        or isinstance(temperature, bool)
        or not isinstance(structured_output, bool)
        or thinking_level not in _THINKING_LEVELS
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_runtime_parameters_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结的模型运行参数不合法",
        )
    provider = str(frozen_runtime.get("provider") or "")
    thinking_mode, reasoning_effort = _runtime_thinking_controls(
        provider=provider,
        default_thinking_mode=str(
            frozen_runtime.get("thinking_mode") or "auto"
        ),
        thinking_level=str(thinking_level),
        thinking_enabled=thinking_enabled,
    )
    response_format_mode = node_config.get("response_format_mode", "contract")
    if response_format_mode not in {"contract", "json_object"}:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_runtime_parameters_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结的输出格式模式不合法",
        )
    runtime_values = dict(frozen_runtime)
    runtime_values.update(
        {
            "encrypted_api_key": current.encrypted_api_key,
            "temperature": float(temperature),
            "max_tokens": output_budget,
            "max_retries": 0,
            "structured_output": structured_output,
            "thinking_mode": thinking_mode,
            "reasoning_effort": reasoning_effort,
            # 冻结在节点上：json_object 只发宽松 JSON 模式，合同校验仍在平台侧
            "response_format_mode": response_format_mode,
        }
    )
    return (
        SimpleNamespace(**runtime_values),
        output_budget,
        reasoning_effort,
        structured_output,
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _FrozenVisionRuntime:
    media_policy: ModelMediaPolicy
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile | None
    policy_hash: str | None
    explicit: bool


def _frozen_vision_runtime(
    *,
    node_config: Mapping[str, Any],
    frozen_runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
    target_max_frames: int,
) -> _FrozenVisionRuntime:
    """Resolve only the immutable image contract captured in the run.

    Runs created before the vision-policy migration carry none of the three
    fields and deliberately retain the old 600px delivery path.  Once any
    field exists, all three must be present and mutually consistent.
    """

    field_names = (
        "vision_input_policy",
        "vision_capability_snapshot",
        "vision_policy_hash",
    )
    present = {name for name in field_names if name in node_config}
    if not present:
        return _FrozenVisionRuntime(
            media_policy=ModelMediaPolicy.legacy(
                target_max_frames=target_max_frames
            ),
            capability=None,
            policy_hash=None,
            explicit=False,
        )
    if present != set(field_names):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_vision_snapshot_incomplete",
            "提示词节点冻结的图片策略、能力档案或摘要不完整",
        )

    raw_capability = node_config.get("vision_capability_snapshot")
    raw_runtime_capability = frozen_runtime.get("vision_capability_profile")
    raw_policy = node_config.get("vision_input_policy")
    frozen_hash = node_config.get("vision_policy_hash")
    if (
        not isinstance(raw_capability, Mapping)
        or not isinstance(raw_runtime_capability, Mapping)
        or not isinstance(raw_policy, Mapping)
        or not isinstance(frozen_hash, str)
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", frozen_hash)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_vision_snapshot_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结的图片策略或能力档案格式不合法",
        )

    provider = str(frozen_runtime.get("provider") or "")
    model_id = str(frozen_runtime.get("model_id") or "")
    try:
        capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            raw_capability,
            provider=provider,
            model_id=model_id,
        )
        runtime_capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            raw_runtime_capability,
            provider=provider,
            model_id=model_id,
        )
        if not same_vision_capability(
            capability,
            runtime_capability,
            provider=provider,
            model_id=model_id,
        ):
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "workflow_vision_capability_snapshot_drift",
                "节点图片能力档案与冻结模型运行参数不一致",
            )
        if raw_policy.get("version") == "legacy-default":
            policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
                None,
                capability=capability,
                legacy_if_missing=True,
            )
            if canonical_json(dict(raw_policy)) != canonical_json(
                policy.as_dict()
            ):
                raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                    "vision_input_policy_invalid",
                    "历史兼容图片策略快照与平台固定语义不一致",
                )
        else:
            policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
                raw_policy,
                capability=capability,
                legacy_if_missing=False,
            )
        expected_hash = vision_policy_hash(policy, capability)
        if frozen_hash != expected_hash:
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "workflow_vision_policy_hash_drift",
                "工作流图片策略哈希与冻结策略或能力档案不一致",
            )
    except VisionInputPolicyError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            evidence=exc.evidence,
        ) from exc

    if policy.is_legacy_default:
        return _FrozenVisionRuntime(
            media_policy=ModelMediaPolicy.legacy(
                target_max_frames=target_max_frames
            ),
            capability=capability,
            policy_hash=frozen_hash,
            explicit=False,
        )
    return _FrozenVisionRuntime(
        media_policy=ModelMediaPolicy(
            version="vision-input-policy-v1",
            target=policy.roles["target"],
            anchor=policy.roles["anchor"],
            target_max_frames=target_max_frames,
        ),
        capability=capability,
        policy_hash=frozen_hash,
        explicit=True,
    )


def _dispatch_identity(
    *,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_fingerprint: str,
    media_bundle: PreparedMediaBundle,
    media_dispatch_identities: tuple[str, ...],
    policy_hash: str | None,
    serialized_request: SerializedDoubaoRequest | None,
    retry_feedback: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> str:
    media_by_identity = {
        f"{item.role}:{item.ordinal}": item for item in media_bundle.items
    }
    if set(media_dispatch_identities) != set(media_by_identity):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_media_dispatch_identity_invalid",
            "模型图片发送顺序与已准备载荷不一致",
        )
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-dispatch-v2",
        "input_fingerprint": input_fingerprint,
        "node_snapshot_hash": _sha256(canonical_json(dict(node))),
        "vision_policy_hash": policy_hash,
        "media": [
            {
                "identity": identity,
                "sent_hash": media_by_identity[identity].sent_hash,
            }
            for identity in media_dispatch_identities
        ],
        "request_body_sha256": (
            serialized_request.body_sha256
            if serialized_request is not None
            else None
        ),
    }
    if retry_feedback is not None:
        payload["retry_feedback"] = dict(retry_feedback)
    return _sha256(canonical_json(payload))


# 供应商破坏自己冻结 schema 时的一次重问（workflow_fixture_executor 里发起），
# 重问用独立的账本身份，但仍是本步骤自己的输入。
SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX = "schema-retry-v1:"
_OWN_INVOCATION_BASES = ("root", SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX + "root")


def invocation_identity_verified(
    invocation_key: str,
    *,
    transport_mode: str | None,
    runtime: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    """这次调用是不是本步骤自己的身份：``root``、schema 重问键，或它们在批量/在线
    传输兜底下的续调用键（须有冻结的兜底策略背书）。

    09-18 def 16 改在线后出现 ``model_step_input_mismatch`` 死信：在线路线没有传输
    策略，schema 重问键 ``schema-retry-v1:root`` 直接送到这里，被当成外来输入拒掉。
    """
    if invocation_key in _OWN_INVOCATION_BASES:
        return True
    if transport_mode not in {"batch", "online"}:
        return False
    from .batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix, policy as recovery_policy

    try:
        if recovery_policy(runtime) is None:
            return False
    except ValueError:
        return False
    for base in _OWN_INVOCATION_BASES:
        pattern = (re.escape(invocation_prefix(base) + transport_mode + ":")
                   + r"[1-9][0-9]*(?::reservation:[1-9][0-9]*)?")
        if re.fullmatch(pattern, invocation_key) is not None:
            return True
    return False


REDLINE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MARKER = "skipped_by_redline"


def redline_short_circuit_output(
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    precheck: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """调用 A 已判红线命中时跳过调用 B，返回短路标记输出；否则返回 None。

    老评测路径一直有这个短路（worker 的 evaluate_v3_redline_prefilter 命中即不调
    B），新路径漏抄了：run137 里 28/250 条 A 判红线的素材照样花钱跑 B，结果注定
    被红线覆盖为 L5。判定使用与收尾裁决完全相同的 evaluate_redlines + 同一份
    冻结红线策略，两处结论必然一致；不一致时收尾会 fail-closed 报错而不是出错分。
    """

    node_config = node.get("config")
    node_config = node_config if isinstance(node_config, Mapping) else {}
    if node_config.get("asset_key") != "prompt_b":
        return None
    workflow = run_snapshot.get("workflow")
    workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
    manifest = manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}
    if manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image":
        return None
    policy: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    for candidate in manifest.get("nodes") or []:
        if not isinstance(candidate, Mapping):
            continue
        config = candidate.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if config.get("module_type_key") != "eligibility_redline":
            continue
        component = config.get("component_snapshot")
        component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
        raw = component.get("content_json")
        if isinstance(raw, str):
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(raw)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return None
            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                policy = parsed
        break
    if not isinstance(policy, Mapping) or policy.get("enabled") is not True:
        return None
    # 流水线是链式的：B 的 input_manifest.upstream 只带直接前驱（赛道路由），
    # 调用 A 的输出隔了两层——必须由调用方从运行步骤记录里取出后传入。
    # 首版从 upstream 里找 prompt_a，真实拓扑下永远取不到，短路整轮零触发
    # （run138：A 判红线 24 条、B 完成 47 条、短路 0）。
    if not isinstance(precheck, Mapping):
        return None
    from .redline_policy import evaluate_redlines
    from .schema_adapter import adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck

    try:
        adapted = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
            dict(precheck), redline_policy=dict(policy)
        )
        verdict = evaluate_redlines(adapted, policy=dict(policy))
    except Exception:
        # 判不动就不短路，照常调 B——短路只是省钱优化，不能引入新的失败面。
        return None
    if not verdict.get("hit"):
        return None
    try:
        decision = resolve_short_circuit(
            [
                ShortCircuitRule(
                    rule_key="legacy-redline-l5",
                    when={"path": "redline.hit", "op": "eq", "value": True},
                    target_level="L5",
                    priority=1000,
                    skip_roles=("grading", "level_matching"),
                )
            ],
            {"redline": {"hit": True}},
            enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest),
        )
    except ShortCircuitError:
        # 老机制未冻结可用 L5 时保持原有 fail-safe：不做付费前短路，交给
        # 完整评分链路暴露合同错误，而不是静默产出非法等级。
        return None
    if decision is None:
        return None
    decision_payload = short_circuit_decision_payload(decision)
    decision_payload["evidence"] = {
        **decision_payload["evidence"],
        "legacy_projection": True,
        "hit_rules": list(verdict.get("hit_rules") or []),
    }
    return {
        REDLINE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MARKER: True,
        "hit_rules": list(verdict.get("hit_rules") or []),
        "reason": "调用A已判红线命中，跳过调用B；等级由收尾裁决按红线策略给出",
        "short_circuit_decision": decision_payload,
    }


def prepare_prompt_invocation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    invocation_key: str = "root",
    frozen_input_override: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    input_fingerprint: str | None = None,
    invocation_attempt_no: int | None = None,
    invocation_started_at: datetime | None = None,
    transport_mode: str | None = None,
) -> PreparedPromptInvocation:
    """Prepare an immutable real request without performing a network call."""

    if run.id is None or attempt.id is None or attempt.run_id != run.id:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_step_attempt_mismatch",
            "真实模型步骤与运行身份不一致",
        )
    if attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_step_attempt_not_leased",
            "真实模型步骤尚未取得有效执行租约",
        )
    lease_expires_at = attempt.lease_expires_at
    if (
        not isinstance(attempt.lease_token, str)
        or not attempt.lease_token
        or lease_expires_at is None
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_step_attempt_not_leased",
            "真实模型步骤尚未取得有效执行租约",
        )
    if lease_expires_at.tzinfo is None:
        lease_expires_at = lease_expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    if lease_expires_at <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_step_lease_expired",
            "真实模型步骤租约已过期，未发起付费请求",
        )
    if _sha256(attempt.input_manifest_json) != attempt.input_hash:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_step_input_hash_mismatch",
            "真实模型步骤输入摘要损坏",
        )
    snapshot = _json_object(
        read_run_snapshot_text(run),
        code="model_run_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行快照损坏",
    )
    if _sha256(canonical_json(snapshot)) != run.snapshot_hash:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_run_snapshot_hash_mismatch",
            "真实模型运行快照摘要不一致",
        )
    runtime = _mapping(
        snapshot.get("runtime_context"),
        code="model_run_snapshot_invalid",
        message="真实模型运行缺少冻结上下文",
    )
    manifest = _workflow_manifest(snapshot)
    category_identity = (
        run.category_key,
        runtime.get("category_key"),
        manifest.get("category_key"),
    )
    if (
        any(not isinstance(value, str) or not value for value in category_identity)
        or len(set(category_identity)) != 1
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_run_category_mismatch",
            "运行类目与冻结工作流或输入上下文不一致",
        )
    if runtime.get("model_execution_mode") != "real":
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "当前运行没有真实模型评测授权",
        )
    try:
        real_execution_runtime_kind(run, runtime)
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if not isinstance(node_id, str) or node_id != attempt.step_key:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_node_attempt_mismatch",
            "提示词节点与当前步骤不一致",
        )
    frozen_manifest_node = _frozen_manifest_node(snapshot, node_id)
    frozen_node = _frozen_node(snapshot, node_id)
    nested_foreach = frozen_manifest_node.get("kind") == "foreach_image"
    if nested_foreach:
        if (
            invocation_key == "root"
            or frozen_input_override is None
            or input_fingerprint is None
            or canonical_json(dict(input_manifest))
            != canonical_json(dict(frozen_input_override))
            or _sha256(canonical_json(dict(frozen_input_override)))
            != input_fingerprint
            or not isinstance(invocation_attempt_no, int)
            or isinstance(invocation_attempt_no, bool)
            or invocation_attempt_no < 1
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_foreach_item_identity_invalid",
                "foreach_image 子调用缺少冻结输入或幂等身份",
            )
    else:
        if (
            not invocation_identity_verified(
                invocation_key, transport_mode=transport_mode, runtime=runtime
            )
            or frozen_input_override is not None
            or input_fingerprint is not None
            or canonical_json(dict(input_manifest))
            != attempt.input_manifest_json
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_step_input_mismatch",
                "真实模型步骤输入与数据库记录不一致",
            )
    if canonical_json(dict(frozen_node)) != canonical_json(dict(node)):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_node_snapshot_mismatch",
            "提示词节点与冻结工作流不一致",
        )
    node_config = _mapping(
        frozen_node.get("config"),
        code="model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
        message="提示词节点冻结配置损坏",
    )
    short_circuit_rules = node_config.get("short_circuit_rules")
    if short_circuit_rules is not None:
        try:
            validate_short_circuit_rules(
                short_circuit_rules,
                enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest),
            )
        except ShortCircuitError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    component = _mapping(
        node_config.get("component_snapshot"),
        code="model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
        message="提示词节点缺少冻结正文",
    )
    content = _mapping(
        component.get("content"),
        code="model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
        message="提示词节点缺少冻结正文",
    )
    component_hash = component.get("content_hash")
    if (
        component.get("kind") != "prompt"
        or component.get("version_id") != node_config.get("prompt_version_id")
        or not isinstance(component_hash, str)
        or canonical_component_hash(content) != component_hash
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结正文或版本摘要损坏",
        )
    system_template = content.get("system_prompt")
    user_template = content.get("user_prompt")
    rubric_version = content.get("rubric_version")
    if (
        not isinstance(system_template, str)
        or not isinstance(user_template, str)
        or not isinstance(rubric_version, str)
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_snapshot_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结正文格式不正确",
        )

    model_snapshot = _mapping(
        node_config.get("model_snapshot"),
        code="model_snapshot_invalid",
        message="提示词节点缺少冻结模型",
    )
    model_snapshot_hash = model_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash")
    canonical_model_snapshot = dict(model_snapshot)
    canonical_model_snapshot.pop("snapshot_hash", None)
    if (
        not isinstance(model_snapshot_hash, str)
        or _sha256(canonical_json(canonical_model_snapshot))
        != model_snapshot_hash
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_snapshot_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结模型摘要损坏",
        )
    source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
    if not isinstance(source_id, int) or isinstance(source_id, bool):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_source_not_frozen",
            "提示词节点没有冻结凭据来源",
        )
    current_model = db.get(ModelConfig, source_id)
    if (
        current_model is None
        or not current_model.active
        or not isinstance(current_model.encrypted_api_key, str)
        or not current_model.encrypted_api_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_source_unavailable",
            "提示词节点冻结的模型凭据不可用",
        )
    if current_model.benchmark_enabled is not True:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_real_execution_disabled",
            "管理员已关闭该模型的真实调用授权",
        )
    frozen_runtime = _mapping(
        model_snapshot.get("runtime_config"),
        code="model_snapshot_invalid",
        message="提示词节点缺少冻结模型运行参数",
    )
    try:
        current_runtime = build_model_config_snapshot(current_model)
    except StrategySecretError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_config_snapshot_invalid",
            "当前模型配置未通过安全校验",
        ) from exc
    transport_fields = (
        "provider",
        "protocol",
        "base_url",
        "api_path",
        "model_id",
    )
    if any(
        current_runtime.get(field) != frozen_runtime.get(field)
        for field in transport_fields
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_config_snapshot_drift",
            "当前模型供应商、协议、端点或模型身份已变化，请重新生成评测机制版本",
        )
    if any(
        model_snapshot.get(key) != frozen_runtime.get(key)
        for key in ("provider", "protocol", "model_id")
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_snapshot_identity_mismatch",
            "冻结模型身份与运行参数不一致",
        )
    (
        client_config,
        output_budget,
        reasoning_effort,
        structured_output,
    ) = _prepared_client_config(
        current=current_model,
        frozen_runtime=frozen_runtime,
        node_config=node_config,
    )
    # 豆包推理接入点：首次真实调用时冻结到运行上；路由开关打开且缺少接入点则
    # 在任何付费请求发出之前拒绝。model_id 保持真实模型身份不变。
    try:
        from .batch_endpoint_recovery import effective_route
        call_route = effective_route(runtime, transport_mode)
        if transport_mode == 'online':
            if call_route.get('foundation_model') != client_config.model_id:
                raise ValueError('在线兜底模型与冻结模型不一致')
            endpoint_id = call_route['endpoint_id']
            client_config.api_path = '/chat/completions'
        else:
            endpoint_id = resolve_run_endpoint(
                db, run, provider=client_config.provider, model_id=client_config.model_id,
                inference_route=call_route,
            )
    except (DoubaoEndpointError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(getattr(exc, 'code', 'model_transport_route_invalid'), str(exc)) from exc
    client_config.endpoint_id = endpoint_id
    from .batch_inference import configure_batch_client
    try:
        configure_batch_client(client_config, endpoint_id)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError('batch_transport_invalid', str(exc)) from exc
    try:
        output_contract = parse_output_contract(node_config.get("output_contract"))
    except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_output_contract_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结的结构化输出合同不合法",
        ) from exc
    if output_contract.mode != "none" and structured_output is not True:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_output_contract_invalid",
            "提示词节点启用了输出合同，但冻结模型没有启用结构化输出",
        )
    from .production_title_normalization import frozen_title_profile
    try:
        client_config.production_title_normalization_profile = frozen_title_profile(frozen_node, run.category_key)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("model_title_normalization_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    node_limits = runtime.get("real_execution_node_limits")
    limit = (
        node_limits.get(node_id)
        if isinstance(node_limits, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if transport_mode == 'online':
        limit = limit.get('online_fallback_limits') if isinstance(limit, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(limit, Mapping)
        or limit.get("provider") != client_config.provider
        or limit.get("model_id") != client_config.model_id
        or limit.get("source_model_config_id") != source_id
        or limit.get("max_output_tokens") != output_budget
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_node_limits_mismatch",
            "提示词请求与冻结调用额度不一致",
        )

    frozen_input = (
        _mapping(
            frozen_input_override,
            code="model_foreach_item_input_invalid",
            message="foreach_image 子调用缺少冻结素材输入",
        )
        if nested_foreach
        else _mapping(
            runtime.get("input_manifest"),
            code="model_run_input_invalid",
            message="真实模型运行缺少冻结素材输入",
        )
    )
    input_modality = node_config.get("input_modality", "image")
    if input_modality not in {"image", "text"}:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_prompt_input_modality_invalid",
            "提示词节点冻结的输入方式不受支持",
        )
    output_contract_key, expected_asset_id = _knowledge_graph_identity(
        node_config,
        frozen_input,
    )
    safe_frozen_input = _redact_model_context(frozen_input)
    upstream = _business_upstream_outputs(input_manifest)
    asset: Asset | None = None
    source_image_path: Path | None = None
    frozen_asset_hash: str | None = None
    target_max_frames = 1
    if input_modality == "image":
        asset_id = frozen_input.get("asset_id")
        if not isinstance(asset_id, int) or isinstance(asset_id, bool):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_asset_identity_missing",
                "真实模型运行缺少素材身份",
            )
        asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
        frozen_asset_hash = frozen_input.get("asset_sha256")
        if (
            asset is None
            or not isinstance(frozen_asset_hash, str)
            or asset.sha256 != frozen_asset_hash
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_asset_snapshot_drift",
                "真实模型素材身份或摘要已变化",
            )
        if not asset.mime_type.startswith("image/"):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_asset_type_unsupported",
                "图片输入方式只支持图片素材",
            )
        try:
            source_image_path = resolve_asset_path(asset, get_settings())
            if not source_image_path.is_file():
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                    "model_asset_missing",
                    "真实模型素材文件不存在",
                )
            if sha256_file_cached(source_image_path) != frozen_asset_hash:
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                    "model_asset_hash_mismatch",
                    "真实模型素材文件与冻结摘要不一致",
                )
        except NasStorageError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc

        # 与老评测路径同一套动图策略；目标图与锚点图稍后统一交给
        # prepare_model_media 生成可复用、可审计的供应商载荷。
        try:
            profile = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                    EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == run.category_key
                )
            )
            pipeline_config = (
                json.loads(profile.pipeline_config_json or "{}")
                if profile is not None
                else {}
            )
            animated_config = processor_config(
                pipeline_config, "image.animated_contact_sheet"
            )
            target_max_frames = int(animated_config.get("max_frames", 1))
        except WorkflowModelExecutorError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_asset_preprocess_failed",
                "真实模型素材预处理失败",
            ) from exc
        metadata = {
            "asset_id": asset.id,
            "width": asset.width,
            "height": asset.height,
            "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": asset.size_bytes,
            "category_key": run.category_key,
            "sha256": frozen_asset_hash,
        }
    else:
        metadata = {
            "input_modality": "text",
            "category_key": run.category_key,
            "input": safe_frozen_input,
        }
    previous_output = _previous_output(upstream)
    effective_user_template = user_template
    if run.category_key == "inspiration_image" and (
        node_config.get("asset_key") == "prompt_a" or content.get("stage") == "A"
    ):
        from .schema_adapter import (
            inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract,
            inspiration_image_defects_enabled,
        )

        # image_defects 开关是工作流级合同字段，随冻结投影快照带下来；
        # 投影缺失（存量/未重新冻结）时回落 None → 默认启用，行为不变。
        _resource_policy = _workflow_manifest(snapshot).get("resource_policy")
        _projection = (
            _resource_policy.get("legacy_projection_snapshot")
            if isinstance(_resource_policy, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        _workflow_fields = (
            _projection.get("workflow_fields") if isinstance(_projection, Mapping) else None
        )
        _projection_contract = (
            _workflow_fields.get("contract")
            if isinstance(_workflow_fields, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        effective_user_template = user_template.rstrip() + (
            inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract(
                _rule_module_content(snapshot, "eligibility_redline"),
                image_defects_enabled=inspiration_image_defects_enabled(_projection_contract),
            )
        )
    replacements = {
        "{{image_metadata}}": canonical_json(metadata),
        "{{precheck_json}}": canonical_json(previous_output),
        "{{previous_output}}": canonical_json(previous_output),
        "{{rubric_version}}": rubric_version,
        "{{ASSET_ID}}": expected_asset_id or (
            str(asset.id) if asset is not None else ""
        ),
    }
    if output_contract_key:
        # Route prompts may gate decoration fields on upstream classification.
        # Never infer this input from expected_result (the regression truth).
        primary_category = frozen_input.get("primary_category")
        replacements["{{PRIMARY_CATEGORY}}"] = (
            primary_category.strip()
            if isinstance(primary_category, str) and primary_category.strip()
            else "未提供（不满足家装或工装条件）"
        )
    authoritative = _authoritative_prompt_b(
        db,
        run=run,
        snapshot=snapshot,
        component=component,
        content=content,
        node_config=node_config,
        metadata=metadata,
    )
    anchor_media_items: tuple[tuple[str, ModelMediaItem], ...] | None = None
    if input_modality == "image" and _prompt_attaches_anchors(
        content, node_config, authoritative=authoritative is not None
    ):
        assert source_image_path is not None and asset is not None
        anchor_media_items = _anchor_media_items(
            db,
            snapshot=snapshot,
            target=source_image_path,
            target_mime=asset.mime_type,
        )
    if authoritative is not None:
        system_prompt, user_prompt = authoritative
    else:
        if node_config.get("output_contract", {}).get("name") == "model_3d_su_joint_call_a":
            from .joint_model_workflow import render_joint_prompt

            try:
                system_template = render_joint_prompt(system_template, frozen_input)
            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("joint_prompt_route_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
        system_prompt = _render_prompt(
            system_template,
            replacements,
            code="prompt_placeholder_unresolved",
        )
        rendered_user = _render_prompt(
            effective_user_template,
            replacements,
            code="prompt_placeholder_unresolved",
        )
        workflow_context = {
            "schema_version": "workflow-prompt-context-v1",
            "category_key": run.category_key,
            "run_mode": runtime.get("run_mode"),
            "input_modality": input_modality,
            "asset": metadata if input_modality == "image" else None,
            "input": safe_frozen_input,
            "upstream": upstream,
        }
        context_block = "【工作流上下文】\n" + canonical_json(
            workflow_context
        )
        user_prompt = (
            rendered_user.rstrip() + "\n\n" + context_block
            if rendered_user.strip()
            else context_block
        )

    from .workflow_retry_feedback import (
