        append_validation_feedback,
        validation_retry_feedback,
    )
    retry_feedback = validation_retry_feedback(
        db, run=run, attempt=attempt, manifest=manifest, node_id=node_id,
        invocation_key=invocation_key, schema=output_contract.schema,
        transport_mode=transport_mode,
    )
    if retry_feedback is not None:
        user_prompt = append_validation_feedback(user_prompt, retry_feedback)

    if input_modality == "image":
        assert (
            source_image_path is not None
            and asset is not None
            and frozen_asset_hash is not None
        )
        media_items = [
            ModelMediaItem(
                role="target",
                ordinal=0,
                source_path=source_image_path,
                mime_type=asset.mime_type,
                content_sha256=frozen_asset_hash,
                asset_id=asset.id,
            )
        ]
        if anchor_media_items is not None:
            media_items.extend(item for _label, item in anchor_media_items)
        settings = get_settings()
        cache_root = getattr(
            settings,
            "model_media_cache_root",
            settings.upload_dir / ".derived" / "model-media",
        )
        vision_runtime = _frozen_vision_runtime(
            node_config=node_config,
            frozen_runtime=frozen_runtime,
            target_max_frames=target_max_frames,
        )
        try:
            media_bundle = prepare_model_media(
                media_items,
                cache_root=cache_root,
                policy=vision_runtime.media_policy,
                capability=vision_runtime.capability,
            )
        except ModelMediaPreparationError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                retryable=exc.code
                in {"anchor_prepare_failed", "target_prepare_failed"},
                evidence=exc.evidence,
            ) from exc

        image_path = media_bundle.target.sent_path
        model_mime_type = media_bundle.target.sent_mime_type
        if vision_runtime.explicit:
            assert (
                vision_runtime.capability is not None
                and vision_runtime.policy_hash is not None
            )
            prepared_anchors = {
                item.ordinal: item for item in media_bundle.anchors
            }
            explicit_samples = tuple(
                ProviderImageSample(
                    label=label,
                    path=prepared_anchors[item.ordinal].sent_path,
                    mime_type=prepared_anchors[item.ordinal].sent_mime_type,
                    role="anchor",
                    ordinal=item.ordinal,
                    fidelity=vision_runtime.media_policy.role_policy(
                        "anchor"
                    ).fidelity,
                    policy_hash=vision_runtime.policy_hash,
                )
                for label, item in (anchor_media_items or ())
            )
            explicit_samples += (
                ProviderImageSample(
                    label=(
                        "待评图片（禁止把锚点图等级直接当作输出）"
                        if anchor_media_items is not None
                        else "待评图片"
                    ),
                    path=image_path,
                    mime_type=model_mime_type,
                    role="target",
                    ordinal=0,
                    fidelity=vision_runtime.media_policy.role_policy(
                        "target"
                    ).fidelity,
                    policy_hash=vision_runtime.policy_hash,
                ),
            )
            image_samples = explicit_samples
            media_dispatch_identities = tuple(
                f"anchor:{item.ordinal}"
                for _label, item in (anchor_media_items or ())
            ) + ("target:0",)
            try:
                serialized_provider_request = (
                    DoubaoClient.serialize_chat_json_images_request(
                        client_config,
                        system_prompt,
                        image_samples,
                        user_prompt=user_prompt,
                        output_budget=output_budget,
                        reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
                        structured_output=structured_output,
                        output_contract=(
                            output_contract
                            if output_contract.mode != "none"
                            else None
                        ),
                        max_image_count=len(image_samples),
                        max_single_image_bytes=(
                            vision_runtime.capability.operational_single_image_bytes
                        ),
                    )
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                    str(
                        getattr(
                            exc,
                            "code",
                            "vision_request_serialization_failed",
                        )
                    ),
                    "模型图片请求无法按冻结策略序列化",
                ) from exc
            request_body_bytes = len(serialized_provider_request.body)
            if (
                request_body_bytes
                > vision_runtime.capability.operational_request_body_bytes
            ):
                raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                    "vision_request_body_too_large",
                    "模型请求体超过冻结供应商能力档案的安全上限",
                    evidence={
                        "serialized_request_body_bytes": request_body_bytes,
                        "provider_max_bytes": (
                            vision_runtime.capability.max_request_body_bytes
                        ),
                        "operational_max_bytes": (
                            vision_runtime.capability.operational_request_body_bytes
                        ),
                        "sample_count": len(image_samples),
                        "body_sha256": (
                            serialized_provider_request.body_sha256
                        ),
                    },
                )
            frozen_vision_policy_hash = vision_runtime.policy_hash
        else:
            serialized_provider_request = None
            frozen_vision_policy_hash = vision_runtime.policy_hash
            if anchor_media_items is None:
                image_samples = None
                media_dispatch_identities = ("target:0",)
            else:
                prepared_anchors = {
                    item.ordinal: item for item in media_bundle.anchors
                }
                image_samples = tuple(
                    (
                        label,
                        prepared_anchors[item.ordinal].sent_path,
                        prepared_anchors[item.ordinal].sent_mime_type,
                    )
                    for label, item in anchor_media_items
                ) + (
                    (
                        "待评图片（禁止把锚点图等级直接当作输出）",
                        image_path,
                        model_mime_type,
                    ),
                )
                media_dispatch_identities = tuple(
                    f"anchor:{item.ordinal}"
                    for _label, item in anchor_media_items
                ) + ("target:0",)
    else:
        media_bundle = PreparedMediaBundle(items=())
        image_path = None
        model_mime_type = None
        image_samples = None
        media_dispatch_identities = ()
        serialized_provider_request = None
        frozen_vision_policy_hash = None

    dispatch_identity = _dispatch_identity(
        node=frozen_node,
        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint or attempt.input_hash,
        media_bundle=media_bundle,
        media_dispatch_identities=media_dispatch_identities,
        policy_hash=frozen_vision_policy_hash,
        serialized_request=serialized_provider_request,
        retry_feedback=retry_feedback,
    )

    frozen_pricing = limit.get("pricing_snapshot")
    if (
        isinstance(frozen_pricing, Mapping)
        and frozen_pricing.get("schema_version")
        in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS
    ):
        media_pricing_snapshot = {
            "version": frozen_pricing["schema_version"],
            "pricing_contract": deepcopy(dict(frozen_pricing)),
        }
    else:
        input_price = limit.get("input_micros_per_million_tokens")
        if input_price is None:
            input_price = current_model.input_micros_per_million_tokens
        pricing_version = (
            limit.get("pricing_version") or "model-config-pricing-v1"
        )
        if (
            not isinstance(input_price, int)
            or isinstance(input_price, bool)
            or input_price < 0
            or not isinstance(pricing_version, str)
            or not pricing_version.strip()
        ):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
                "提示词节点缺少有效的冻结计价参数",
            )
        media_pricing_snapshot = {
            "version": pricing_version,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": input_price,
        }
    return PreparedPromptInvocation(
        run_id=run.id,
        attempt_id=attempt.id,
        node_instance_id=node_id,
        invocation_key=invocation_key,
        node=deepcopy(dict(frozen_node)),
        joint_input_identity=(
            {k: frozen_input[k] for k in ("source", "is_single", "ll_id", "res_id") if k in frozen_input}
            if node_config.get("output_contract", {}).get("name") == "model_3d_su_joint_call_a" else None
        ),
        input_manifest=deepcopy(dict(input_manifest)),
        input_fingerprint=input_fingerprint or attempt.input_hash,
        attempt_no=invocation_attempt_no or attempt.attempt_no,
        started_at=invocation_started_at or attempt.started_at,
        system_prompt=system_prompt,
        user_prompt=user_prompt,
        image_path=image_path,
        mime_type=model_mime_type,
        output_budget=output_budget,
        reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
        structured_output=structured_output,
        output_contract=output_contract,
        component_hash=component_hash,
        model_snapshot_hash=model_snapshot_hash,
        output_contract_key=output_contract_key,
        expected_asset_id=expected_asset_id,
        client_config=client_config,
        media_bundle=media_bundle,
        media_dispatch_identities=media_dispatch_identities,
        media_pricing_snapshot=media_pricing_snapshot,
        image_samples=image_samples,
        serialized_provider_request=serialized_provider_request,
        dispatch_identity=dispatch_identity,
        vision_policy_hash=frozen_vision_policy_hash,
        endpoint_id=endpoint_id,
        retry_feedback=retry_feedback,
    )


def _usage(response: DoubaoResponse) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    values = (
        response.input_tokens,
        response.output_tokens,
        response.total_tokens,
    )
    if (
        any(
            not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 0
            for value in values
        )
        or response.total_tokens < response.input_tokens + response.output_tokens
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_usage_missing",
            "模型响应缺少可核验的 token 用量",
        )
    return response.input_tokens, response.output_tokens, response.total_tokens


def _provider_media_usage(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    response: DoubaoResponse,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    total = response.input_image_tokens
    if total is not None and (
        not isinstance(total, int)
        or isinstance(total, bool)
        or total < 0
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_media_usage_invalid",
            "模型响应的图片 token 用量不合法",
        )
    per_image = response.per_image_tokens
    if per_image is not None and (
        len(per_image) != len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities)
        or any(
            not isinstance(value, int)
            or isinstance(value, bool)
            or value < 0
            for value in per_image
        )
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_media_usage_invalid",
            "模型响应的分图 token 用量与实际发送图片不一致",
        )
    usage: dict[str, Any] = {
        "request_correlation_id": response.request_correlation_id,
    }
    if total is not None:
        usage["total_image_tokens"] = total
    if per_image is not None:
        tokens_by_identity = dict(
            zip(prepared.media_dispatch_identities, per_image, strict=True)
        )
        if total is not None and sum(tokens_by_identity.values()) != total:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_media_usage_invalid",
                "模型响应的分图 token 用量无法与总用量核对",
            )
        usage["tokens_by_identity"] = tokens_by_identity
    return usage


def _validate_response_controls(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    response: DoubaoResponse,
) -> None:
    if (
        not isinstance(response.attempt_count, int)
        or isinstance(response.attempt_count, bool)
        or response.attempt_count != 1
        or response.output_budget != prepared.output_budget
        or response.reasoning_effort != prepared.reasoning_effort
        or response.thinking_mode != prepared.client_config.thinking_mode
    ):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "model_response_controls_mismatch",
            "模型响应与冻结的单次调用参数不一致",
        )


def _invocation_evidence(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    response: DoubaoResponse,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    _validate_response_controls(prepared, response)
    input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens = _usage(response)
    media_usage = _provider_media_usage(prepared, response)
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-evidence-v1",
        "invocation_key": prepared.invocation_key,
        **({"retry_feedback": dict(prepared.retry_feedback)} if prepared.retry_feedback is not None else {}),
        "provider": prepared.client_config.provider,
        "model_id": prepared.client_config.model_id,
        "endpoint_id": getattr(prepared, "endpoint_id", None),
        "request_correlation_id": response.request_correlation_id,
        "upstream_status_code": response.upstream_status_code,
        "attempt_count": response.attempt_count,
        "input_tokens": input_tokens,
        "output_tokens": output_tokens,
        "total_tokens": total_tokens,
        "output_budget": prepared.output_budget,
        "thinking_mode": response.thinking_mode,
        "reasoning_effort": response.reasoning_effort,
        "response_format_mode": getattr(prepared.client_config, "response_format_mode", "contract"),
        "component_hash": prepared.component_hash,
        "model_snapshot_hash": prepared.model_snapshot_hash,
        "media_usage": media_usage,
        "cache_observation": response.cache_usage.to_dict(),
        "raw_payload": redact_workflow_secrets(response.raw_payload),
        "parsed_output": redact_workflow_secrets(response.parsed),
        # 按 schema 做过的无损类型纠偏（路径:from->to），空表示原样通过
        "coercions": list(getattr(response, "coercions", ()) or ()),
        **({"output_normalization": redact_workflow_secrets(list(response.output_normalization))}
           if response.output_normalization else {}),
    }


def real_prompt_node_result(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    response: DoubaoResponse,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate one provider response and wrap it in the native node contract."""

    parsed = dict(response.parsed)
    try:
        validate_output_payload(prepared.output_contract, parsed)
    except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
        # 方舟 strict 模式（beta）实测仍会返回类型不符的字段（tags 非数组、score 非整数），
        # 失败留证里没有模型原文就无法判断是提示词措辞、schema 还是供应商问题；
        # 这里把原文摘要挂到异常上，由 fixture 执行器写进 evidence.failure。
        raw_excerpt = str(getattr(response, "raw_text", "") or "")[:600]
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "structured_output_schema_mismatch",
            "模型输出不符合提示词节点冻结的 JSON Schema",
            retryable=True,
            evidence={"raw_excerpt": raw_excerpt} if raw_excerpt else None,
        ) from exc
    extra: dict[str, Any] = {
        "model_invocation": _invocation_evidence(prepared, response),
    }
    if getattr(prepared, "node", {}).get("config", {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("name") == "model_3d_su_joint_call_a":
        from .joint_model_workflow import validate_joint_knowledge

        try:
            validate_joint_knowledge(parsed, expected_input=prepared.joint_input_identity or {})
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("joint_knowledge_graph_invalid", str(exc), retryable=True) from exc
    if prepared.output_contract_key is not None:
        from .knowledge_graph_contracts import KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS

        # Qwen can insert formatting spaces around multi-value separators.
        # Normalize only these separators; keep tokens, order, weights and raw
        # response evidence, then run the unchanged strict semantic validator.
        for field in KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS:
            value = parsed.get(field)
            if isinstance(value, str):
                normalized = re.sub(r"[ \t]*,[ \t]*", ",", value)
                if normalized != value:
                    parsed[field] = normalized
                    extra["model_invocation"]["coercions"].append(
                        f"{field}:kg_comma_spacing_normalized"
                    )
        if prepared.expected_asset_id is None:
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "kg_input_identity_missing",
                "知识图谱真实模型结果缺少冻结素材身份",
            )
        try:
            mapping = adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output(
                prepared.output_contract_key,
                parsed,
                expected_asset_id=prepared.expected_asset_id,
                snapshot=prepared.node["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot"),
                evidence_prefix=(
                    f"node:{prepared.node_instance_id}:model_response"
                ),
            )
        except KnowledgeGraphContractError as exc:
            # 把被拒的模型输出随失败落到 checkpoint：run159 九条 color 多值失败，
            # 排查时只有错误码、看不到模型到底给了什么颜色，只能翻代码反推。
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                evidence={
                    "contract_key": prepared.output_contract_key,
                    "rejected_model_output": redact_workflow_secrets(parsed),
                },
            ) from exc
        config = prepared.node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        contract_snapshot = config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
        contract_snapshot = (
            contract_snapshot
            if isinstance(contract_snapshot, Mapping)
            else {}
        )
        extra.update(
            {
                "output_contract_key": prepared.output_contract_key,
                "output_contract_hash": contract_snapshot.get(
                    "contract_hash"
                ),
                "knowledge_graph_mapping": mapping,
            }
        )
    try:
        validate_native_node_output(prepared.node, parsed)
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    return canonical_node_result(
        node=prepared.node,
        input_manifest=prepared.input_manifest,
        input_fingerprint=prepared.input_fingerprint,
        output=parsed,
        attempt_no=prepared.attempt_no,
        started_at=prepared.started_at,
        extra=extra,
    )


VOTE_DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE = 0.7


def _tag_single_request_usage(error: BaseException) -> None:
    """单次请求失败时把用量标成一个请求，结算按它自己的档位，不按冻结次数均分。"""
    usage = getattr(error, "provider_usage", None)
    if not isinstance(usage, Mapping) or "request_usages" in usage:
        return
    input_tokens, output_tokens = usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens")
    if any(not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool) or v < 0 for v in (input_tokens, output_tokens)):
        return
    try:
        error.provider_usage = {
            **dict(usage),
            "request_usages": [{"input_tokens": input_tokens, "output_tokens": output_tokens}],
        }
    except AttributeError:
        pass


def _once(hook: Callable[[], None] | None) -> Callable[[], None] | None:
    """多路并发请求共用的发出信号：任一路先发出就标记，只触发一次。"""
    if hook is None:
        return None
    fired = False

    def wrapper() -> None:
        nonlocal fired
        if not fired:
            fired = True
            hook()

    return wrapper


def _combine_cache_usage(observations: list[Any]) -> CacheUsageObservation:
    """多次请求的缓存观测合并：全部 hit/miss 才求和，否则整体按未上报（保守全价）。"""
    total = 0
    source = None
    for observation in observations:
        status = getattr(observation, "status", None)
        cached = getattr(observation, "cached_input_tokens", None)
        if status not in {"hit", "miss"} or not isinstance(cached, int) or isinstance(cached, bool):
            return CacheUsageObservation(
                status="unreported", cached_input_tokens=None, source=None,
                reason="multi_request_cache_incomplete",
            )
        total += cached
        source = source or getattr(observation, "source", None)
    return CacheUsageObservation(
        status="hit" if total > 0 else "miss", cached_input_tokens=total, source=source,
    )


def _result_request_usages(result: RealPromptResult) -> list[dict[str, int]]:
    if result.request_usages:
        return [dict(item) for item in result.request_usages]
    return [{"input_tokens": result.input_tokens, "output_tokens": result.output_tokens}]


def _error_usage(error: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    usage = getattr(error, "provider_usage", None)
    if not isinstance(usage, Mapping):
        return None
    input_tokens, output_tokens = usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens")
    if any(not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool) or v < 0 for v in (input_tokens, output_tokens)):
        return None
    requests = usage.get("request_usages")
    if not isinstance(requests, list) or not requests:
        requests = [{"input_tokens": input_tokens, "output_tokens": output_tokens}]
    return {
        "input_tokens": input_tokens,
        "output_tokens": output_tokens,
        "cache": SimpleNamespace(
            status=usage.get("cache_observation_status", "unreported"),
            cached_input_tokens=usage.get("cached_input_tokens"),
            source=None,
        ),
        "request_usages": [dict(item) for item in requests],
    }


def _raise_with_combined_usage(outcomes: list[Any]) -> None:
    """并发请求里有失败：先把全部已知用量（成功 + 失败里带回的）合计到第一个异常上再抛。

    已经发出的付费请求不能因为另一路失败就丢掉用量证据；用量未知的那一路仍按原异常
    语义处理（结果不明 / 待对账）。
    """
    failures = [item for item in outcomes if isinstance(item, BaseException)]
    if not failures:
        return
    primary = failures[0]
    def dispatched(item: Any) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(item, BaseException):
            return True
        if getattr(item, "provider_usage", None) is not None:
            return True
        return isinstance(item, DoubaoError) and getattr(item, "provider_dispatched", True) is not False

    if getattr(primary, "provider_dispatched", True) is False and any(
        dispatched(item) for item in outcomes if item is not primary
    ):
        # 这一路没发出，但同一节点调用里其它请求已发出：整体按已发出处理。
        try:
            primary.provider_dispatched = True
        except AttributeError:
            pass
    known: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    unknown_requests = 0
    for item in outcomes:
        if isinstance(item, BaseException):
            usage = _error_usage(item)
            if usage is None:
                unknown_requests += 1
            else:
                known.append(usage)
        else:
            usage_result = item[-1] if isinstance(item, tuple) else item
            if isinstance(usage_result, DoubaoResponse):
                try:
                    response_input, response_output, _total = _usage(usage_result)
                except WorkflowModelExecutorError:
                    unknown_requests += 1
                    continue
                usage_result = {
                    "request_usages": [{"input_tokens": response_input, "output_tokens": response_output}],
                    "cache": getattr(usage_result, "cache_usage", None),
                }
            known.append(usage_result)
    entries: list[dict[str, int]] = []
    caches: list[Any] = []
    for usage in known:
        if isinstance(usage, RealPromptResult):
            entries.extend(_result_request_usages(usage))
            caches.append(usage.cache_usage)
        else:
            entries.extend(usage["request_usages"])
            caches.append(usage["cache"])
    if entries:
        cache = _combine_cache_usage(caches)
        combined = {
            "input_tokens": sum(e["input_tokens"] for e in entries),
            "output_tokens": sum(e["output_tokens"] for e in entries),
            "total_tokens": sum(e["input_tokens"] + e["output_tokens"] for e in entries),
            "cached_input_tokens": cache.cached_input_tokens,
            "cache_observation_status": cache.status,
            "request_usages": entries,
        }
        if unknown_requests:
            combined["unknown_usage_request_count"] = unknown_requests
        try:
            primary.provider_usage = combined
        except AttributeError:
            pass
    try:
        primary.concurrent_failure_count = len(failures)
        primary.concurrent_request_count = len(outcomes)
    except AttributeError:
        pass
    raise primary


def _vote_median(values: list[float]) -> float:
    ordered = sorted(values)
    n = len(ordered)
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("empty vote")
    mid = n // 2
    return float(ordered[mid]) if n % 2 else (ordered[mid - 1] + ordered[mid]) / 2.0


def aggregate_vote_outputs(outputs: list[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把同一提示词的 N 份结构化输出聚合成一份：分数与六维档位取中位数，证据取最接近中位数那份。

    只对灵感图美感基础评分合同（aesthetic_score + dimensions[*].grade）做数值聚合；其它字段
    原样来自代表样本。source_deductions 按多数票：出现在 ≥ 半数样本里的键才保留。
    """
    if not outputs:
        raise ValueError("no outputs to aggregate")
    scored = [o for o in outputs if isinstance(o.get("aesthetic_score"), (int, float)) and not isinstance(o.get("aesthetic_score"), bool)]
    if not scored:
        return deepcopy(dict(outputs[0]))
    median = _vote_median([float(o["aesthetic_score"]) for o in scored])
    representative = min(scored, key=lambda o: (abs(float(o["aesthetic_score"]) - median), scored.index(o)))
    agg = deepcopy(dict(representative))
    agg["aesthetic_score"] = int(round(median))
    dims = agg.get("dimensions")
    if isinstance(dims, Mapping):
        for key, entry in dims.items():
            grades = [
                o["dimensions"][key]["grade"] for o in scored
                if isinstance(o.get("dimensions"), Mapping) and isinstance(o["dimensions"].get(key), Mapping)
                and isinstance(o["dimensions"][key].get("grade"), (int, float)) and not isinstance(o["dimensions"][key].get("grade"), bool)
            ]
            if grades and isinstance(entry, Mapping):
                entry = dict(entry); entry["grade"] = int(round(_vote_median([float(g) for g in grades])))
                dims[key] = entry
    confidences = [float(o["confidence"]) for o in scored if isinstance(o.get("confidence"), (int, float)) and not isinstance(o.get("confidence"), bool)]
    if confidences and "confidence" in agg:
        agg["confidence"] = round(sum(confidences) / len(confidences), 3)
    if isinstance(agg.get("source_deductions"), list):
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        first_seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for o in scored:
            for item in o.get("source_deductions") or []:
                key = item.get("key") if isinstance(item, Mapping) else item
                if not isinstance(key, str):
                    continue
                counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
                first_seen.setdefault(key, item)
        agg["source_deductions"] = [first_seen[k] for k, c in counts.items() if c * 2 >= len(scored)]
    return agg


async def execute_prompt_votes(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    *,
    samples: int,
    temperature: float | None = None,
    client_type: Callable[[Any], Any] = DoubaoClient,
    on_provider_dispatch: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
) -> RealPromptResult:
    """同一冻结提示词并发采样 N 次，聚合成一份节点输出；N=1 时等价于单次调用。

    账本仍是一行（本地模型免费，用量按 N 次求和记入），每次采样的关联 ID 与分数留在
    evidence.vote 里。温度只对采样副本生效，冻结的节点配置不改。
    """
    if samples <= 1:
        return await execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=client_type, on_provider_dispatch=on_provider_dispatch)
    import asyncio
    from types import SimpleNamespace as _NS
    temp = VOTE_DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE if temperature is None else float(temperature)
    base_cfg = prepared.client_config
    cfg = _NS(**{**vars(base_cfg), "temperature": temp}) if hasattr(base_cfg, "__dict__") else base_cfg
    variants = [replace(prepared, client_config=cfg) for _ in range(samples)]
    dispatch_once = _once(on_provider_dispatch)
    # return_exceptions：一路失败不能让其余已发出的付费请求用量丢失。
    outcomes = list(await asyncio.gather(*[
        execute_prepared_prompt(v, client_type=client_type, on_provider_dispatch=dispatch_once)
        for v in variants
    ], return_exceptions=True))
    _raise_with_combined_usage(outcomes)
    results: list[RealPromptResult] = outcomes
    outputs = [r.node_result.get("output") or {} for r in results]
    agg_output = aggregate_vote_outputs(outputs)
    scores = [o.get("aesthetic_score") for o in outputs]
    rep_index = min(range(len(results)), key=lambda i: abs(float(scores[i]) - float(agg_output.get("aesthetic_score", 0))) if isinstance(scores[i], (int, float)) else 1e9)
    node_result = deepcopy(results[rep_index].node_result)
    node_result["output"] = agg_output
    from .recommendation_calibration import dimension_view

    vote_info = {
        "samples": samples,
        "temperature": temp,
        "scores": scores,
        "request_correlation_ids": [r.request_correlation_id for r in results],
        "representative_index": rep_index,
        # 每份样本里推荐校准会读的字段：收尾时逐样本各算一次概率再取均值（候选 C1）。
        "sample_outputs": [dimension_view(o) for o in outputs],
    }
    if isinstance(node_result.get("model_invocation"), Mapping):
        node_result["model_invocation"] = dict(node_result["model_invocation"], vote=vote_info)
    else:
        node_result["vote"] = vote_info
    evidence = dict(results[rep_index].invocation_evidence); evidence["vote"] = vote_info
    return RealPromptResult(
        node_result=node_result,
        invocation_evidence=evidence,
        request_correlation_id=results[rep_index].request_correlation_id,
        upstream_status_code=results[rep_index].upstream_status_code,
        input_tokens=sum(r.input_tokens for r in results),
        output_tokens=sum(r.output_tokens for r in results),
        total_tokens=sum(r.total_tokens for r in results),
        media_usage=results[rep_index].media_usage,
        cache_usage=_combine_cache_usage([r.cache_usage for r in results]),
        request_usages=tuple(entry for r in results for entry in _result_request_usages(r)),
        media_request_input_tokens=(
            results[rep_index].media_request_input_tokens
            if results[rep_index].media_request_input_tokens is not None
            else results[rep_index].input_tokens
        ),
    )


PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES = 5
PAIRWISE_LEVEL_ORDER = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
PAIRWISE_VERDICT_VALUE = {"better": 1.0, "similar": 0.5, "worse": 0.0}
PAIRWISE_INVERSE = {"better": "worse", "similar": "similar", "worse": "better"}
PAIRWISE_MAX_INFLIGHT = 4
PAIRWISE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
    "你是室内设计灵感图的资深美学评审。每次只比较两张图片的整体美学质量，"
    "不看主题、户型或风格是否相同，只回答严格的 JSON。"
)
PAIRWISE_DEFAULT_USER_PROMPT = (
    "两张图片按标签区分：「图A」与「图B」。请从构图、光影、色彩、材质质感、空间氛围、"
    "画面完成度综合判断：图B 相对 图A 是更好（better）、相当（similar）还是更差（worse）？"
    "只看美学质量，不看主题；不要因为先后顺序偏向任何一张。差距明显才判 better/worse，难分高下判 similar。"
    '只输出 JSON：{"verdict":"better|similar|worse","confidence":0到1的小数,"reason":"一句话"}'
)
PAIRWISE_CONTRACT = PromptOutputContract(
    mode="json_schema",
    name="pairwise_anchor_verdict",
    strict=True,
    schema={
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "verdict": {"type": "string", "enum": ["better", "similar", "worse"]},
            "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
            "reason": {"type": "string"},
        },
        "required": ["verdict", "confidence", "reason"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    },
)
_ANCHOR_LABEL_LEVEL = re.compile(r"锚点图 (L[1-5])")


def _sample_label(sample: Any) -> str:
    return sample.label if isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample) else str(sample[0])


def _relabel_sample(sample: Any, label: str) -> Any:
    if isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample):
        return replace(sample, label=label)
    return (label,) + tuple(sample[1:])


def split_anchor_samples(image_samples: Any) -> tuple[Any, list[tuple[str | None, Any]]]:
    """把冻结请求里的图片拆成（待评图, [(锚点等级, 锚点图)...]）；等级取自锚点标签。"""
    target = None
    anchors: list[tuple[str | None, Any]] = []
    for sample in image_samples or ():
        label = _sample_label(sample)
        role = sample.role if isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample) else ("anchor" if label.startswith("锚点图") else "target")
        if role == "target":
            target = sample
            continue
        match = _ANCHOR_LABEL_LEVEL.search(label)
        anchors.append((match.group(1) if match else None, sample))
    return target, anchors


def first_anchor_per_level(anchors: list[tuple[str | None, Any]]) -> list[tuple[str | None, Any]]:
    seen: set[str | None] = set()
    kept: list[tuple[str | None, Any]] = []
    for level, sample in anchors:
        if level in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(level)
        kept.append((level, sample))
    return kept


def score_level_thresholds(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, int]:
    """从冻结的 score_level_matcher 规则里取运行时真正生效的 legacy 阈值 {等级: 最低分}。"""
    content = _rule_module_content(snapshot, "score_level_matcher")
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("pairwise_thresholds_missing", "成对比较需要 score_level_matcher 规则提供等级阈值")
    rows = content.get("legacy_score_thresholds")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        rows = content.get("legacy_level_thresholds")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        rows = content.get("levels")
    thresholds: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []:
        if isinstance(row, Mapping) and isinstance(row.get("level"), str) and isinstance(row.get("min_score"), int) and row.get("enabled", True):
            thresholds[row["level"]] = int(row["min_score"])
    if not thresholds:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("pairwise_thresholds_missing", "score_level_matcher 规则里没有可用的等级阈值")
    return thresholds


def level_score_bands(thresholds: Mapping[str, int]) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
    """{L1:83,L2:70,...} -> {L1:(83,100), L2:(70,82), ...}，按 min_score 降序切段。"""
    ordered = sorted(((str(k), int(v)) for k, v in thresholds.items()), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])
    bands: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    high = 100
    for level, low in ordered:
        bands[level] = (low, max(low, high))
        high = low - 1
    return bands


def aggregate_pairwise_verdicts(
    verdicts: list[tuple[str, str]],
    bands: Mapping[str, tuple[int, int]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按“待评图打败各级锚点的比例”反推等级，再映射到该级分数段。

    待评图若真在第 k 级，理想图案是：对更差等级的锚点全赢（1）、对更好等级的全输（0）、
    对同级锚点打平（0.5）。取与理想图案平方误差最小的 k；并列时取更好的一级（偏召回）。
    分段内位置用同级锚点的打败比例插值，没有同级锚点时取段中点。
    """
    order = [lv for lv in PAIRWISE_LEVEL_ORDER if lv in bands]
    per_level: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    for level, verdict in verdicts:
        if level in bands and verdict in PAIRWISE_VERDICT_VALUE:
            per_level.setdefault(level, []).append(PAIRWISE_VERDICT_VALUE[verdict])
    beat = {lv: sum(vals) / len(vals) for lv, vals in per_level.items()}
    if not beat:
        raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("pairwise_verdicts_missing", "成对比较没有任何有效判决")
    costs: dict[str, float] = {}
    for k in order:
        ki = order.index(k)
        cost = 0.0
        for lv, s in beat.items():
            li = order.index(lv)
            expected = 0.5 if li == ki else (1.0 if li > ki else 0.0)
            cost += (s - expected) ** 2
        costs[k] = cost
    best = min(order, key=lambda k: (round(costs[k], 9), order.index(k)))
    low, high = bands[best]
    position = beat.get(best, 0.5)
    score = int(round(low + position * (high - low)))
    return {
        "level": best,
        "score": max(0, min(100, score)),
        "beat_fraction": {lv: round(v, 4) for lv, v in beat.items()},
        "costs": {lv: round(v, 4) for lv, v in costs.items()},
    }


async def execute_pairwise_anchors(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    *,
    level_thresholds: Mapping[str, int],
    pairwise_samples: int = 1,
    user_prompt: str | None = None,
    vote_samples: int = 1,
    vote_temperature: float | None = None,
    client_type: Callable[[Any], Any] = DoubaoClient,
    on_provider_dispatch: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
) -> RealPromptResult:
    """主提示词照常跑（只带每级第一张锚点），另对每张锚点单独做两图比较，
    用比较结果反推的分数覆盖 aesthetic_score；维度分与其它字段保留主调用的。

    没有锚点时退化为普通投票调用。每次比较只送两张图，避免大图多张把本地模型卡死。
    用量按全部调用求和记账；每次比较的判决与关联 ID 留在 evidence.pairwise。
    """
    target, anchors = split_anchor_samples(prepared.image_samples)
    if target is None or not anchors:
        return await execute_prompt_votes(
            prepared, samples=vote_samples, temperature=vote_temperature,
            client_type=client_type, on_provider_dispatch=on_provider_dispatch,
        )
    import asyncio

    main_samples = tuple(s for _lv, s in first_anchor_per_level(anchors)) + (target,)
    main_prepared = replace(prepared, image_samples=main_samples, serialized_provider_request=None)
    prompt_text = user_prompt if isinstance(user_prompt, str) and user_prompt.strip() else PAIRWISE_DEFAULT_USER_PROMPT
    repeats = max(1, int(pairwise_samples))

    gate = asyncio.Semaphore(PAIRWISE_MAX_INFLIGHT)
    dispatch_once = _once(on_provider_dispatch)

    async def compare(level: str | None, anchor_sample: Any, ordinal: int) -> tuple[str | None, dict[str, Any], DoubaoResponse]:
        # 偶数序号锚点在前（图A=锚点，图B=待评），奇数序号待评在前并把判决反转：
        # 抵消模型偏向先出现那张图的位置偏差（354 里待评图对同级锚点只有 18–33% 胜率）。
        target_first = ordinal % 2 == 1
        first, second = (target, anchor_sample) if target_first else (anchor_sample, target)
        samples = [_relabel_sample(first, "图A"), _relabel_sample(second, "图B")]
        async with gate:
            client = client_type(prepared.client_config)
            # 比较请求也是付费请求：主调用失败但比较已发出时不能记成未发出（rejected）。
            dispatch_setter = getattr(client, "set_dispatch_hook", None)
            if callable(dispatch_setter):
                dispatch_setter(dispatch_once)
            elif dispatch_once is not None:
                dispatch_once()
            response = await client.chat_json_images(
                PAIRWISE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, samples, user_prompt=prompt_text, max_attempts=1,
                output_budget=prepared.output_budget, reasoning_effort=prepared.reasoning_effort,
                structured_output=True, output_contract=PAIRWISE_CONTRACT, max_image_count=2,
            )
        if not isinstance(response, DoubaoResponse):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError("model_response_invalid", "成对比较返回了无法识别的响应")
        parsed = dict(response.parsed) if isinstance(response.parsed, Mapping) else {}
        verdict = parsed.get("verdict")
        if verdict not in PAIRWISE_VERDICT_VALUE:
            error = WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "pairwise_verdict_invalid", "成对比较输出的 verdict 不在 better/similar/worse 之内",
                retryable=True, evidence={"raw_excerpt": str(getattr(response, "raw_text", "") or "")[:300]},
            )
            try:
                input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens = _usage(response)
            except WorkflowModelExecutorError:
                pass
            else:
                error.provider_usage = {
                    "input_tokens": input_tokens, "output_tokens": output_tokens, "total_tokens": total_tokens,
                    "cached_input_tokens": getattr(getattr(response, "cache_usage", None), "cached_input_tokens", None),
                    "cache_observation_status": getattr(getattr(response, "cache_usage", None), "status", "unreported"),
                    "request_usages": [{"input_tokens": input_tokens, "output_tokens": output_tokens}],
                }
                error.request_correlation_id = response.request_correlation_id
                error.upstream_status_code = response.upstream_status_code
            raise error
        confidence = parsed.get("confidence")
        if target_first:
            verdict = PAIRWISE_INVERSE[verdict]
        return level, {
            "verdict": verdict,
            "target_first": target_first,
            "confidence": float(confidence) if isinstance(confidence, (int, float)) and not isinstance(confidence, bool) else None,
            "reason": str(parsed.get("reason") or "")[:200],
        }, response

    outcomes = list(await asyncio.gather(
        execute_prompt_votes(
            main_prepared, samples=vote_samples, temperature=vote_temperature,
            client_type=client_type, on_provider_dispatch=dispatch_once,
        ),
        *[compare(level, sample, index * repeats + rep) for index, (level, sample) in enumerate(anchors) for rep in range(repeats)],
        return_exceptions=True,
    ))
    _raise_with_combined_usage(outcomes)
    main_result, *pairs = outcomes
    aggregate = aggregate_pairwise_verdicts(
        [(level, info["verdict"]) for level, info, _resp in pairs if level is not None],
        level_score_bands(level_thresholds),
    )
    node_result = deepcopy(main_result.node_result)
    output = dict(node_result.get("output") or {})
    model_score = output.get("aesthetic_score")
    output["aesthetic_score"] = aggregate["score"]
    node_result["output"] = output
    usage = [_usage(resp) for _l, _i, resp in pairs]
    info = {
        "anchors": len(anchors),
        "samples_per_anchor": repeats,
        "level": aggregate["level"],
        "score": aggregate["score"],
        "model_score": model_score,
        "beat_fraction": aggregate["beat_fraction"],
        "costs": aggregate["costs"],
        "verdicts": [dict(info, level=level) for level, info, _resp in pairs],
        "request_correlation_ids": [resp.request_correlation_id for _l, _i, resp in pairs],
    }
    if isinstance(node_result.get("model_invocation"), Mapping):
        node_result["model_invocation"] = dict(node_result["model_invocation"], pairwise=info)
    else:
        node_result["pairwise"] = info
    evidence = dict(main_result.invocation_evidence)
    evidence["pairwise"] = info
    return RealPromptResult(
        node_result=node_result,
        invocation_evidence=evidence,
        request_correlation_id=main_result.request_correlation_id,
        upstream_status_code=main_result.upstream_status_code,
        input_tokens=main_result.input_tokens + sum(u[0] for u in usage),
        output_tokens=main_result.output_tokens + sum(u[1] for u in usage),
        total_tokens=main_result.total_tokens + sum(u[2] for u in usage),
        media_usage=main_result.media_usage,
        cache_usage=_combine_cache_usage([main_result.cache_usage] + [getattr(resp, "cache_usage", None) for _l, _i, resp in pairs]),
        request_usages=tuple(_result_request_usages(main_result)) + tuple(
            {"input_tokens": u[0], "output_tokens": u[1]} for u in usage
        ),
        media_request_input_tokens=(
            main_result.media_request_input_tokens
            if main_result.media_request_input_tokens is not None
            else main_result.input_tokens
        ),
    )


async def execute_prepared_prompt(
    prepared: PreparedPromptInvocation,
    *,
    client_type: Callable[[Any], Any] = DoubaoClient,
    on_provider_dispatch: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
) -> RealPromptResult:
    """Perform exactly one provider attempt and return safe, validated evidence."""

    reservation = _reserve_paid_acceptance_call(prepared)
    response: DoubaoResponse | None = None
    try:
        client = client_type(prepared.client_config)
        dispatch_setter = getattr(client, "set_dispatch_hook", None)
        if callable(dispatch_setter):
            dispatch_setter(on_provider_dispatch)
        elif on_provider_dispatch is not None:
            # Compatible clients without the native transport hook own their
            # provider boundary at the public chat method.  Constructor and
            # acceptance-guard failures remain safely pre-dispatch.
            on_provider_dispatch()
        output_contract_options = (
            {"output_contract": prepared.output_contract}
            if prepared.output_contract.mode != "none"
            else {}
        )
        if prepared.image_samples is not None:
            prepared_request_options = (
                {
                    "prepared_request": prepared.serialized_provider_request,
                }
                if prepared.serialized_provider_request is not None
                else {}
            )
            response = await client.chat_json_images(
                prepared.system_prompt,
                list(prepared.image_samples),
                user_prompt=prepared.user_prompt,
                max_attempts=1,
                output_budget=prepared.output_budget,
                reasoning_effort=prepared.reasoning_effort,
                structured_output=prepared.structured_output,
                max_image_count=len(prepared.image_samples),
                **output_contract_options,
                **prepared_request_options,
            )
        else:
            response = await client.chat_json(
                prepared.system_prompt,
                prepared.user_prompt,
                image_path=prepared.image_path,
                mime_type=prepared.mime_type,
                max_attempts=1,
                output_budget=prepared.output_budget,
                reasoning_effort=prepared.reasoning_effort,
                structured_output=prepared.structured_output,
                **output_contract_options,
            )
        if not isinstance(response, DoubaoResponse):
            raise WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "model_response_invalid",
                "模型客户端返回了无法识别的响应",
            )
        evidence = _invocation_evidence(prepared, response)
        node_result = real_prompt_node_result(prepared, response)
        input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens = _usage(response)
        media_usage = _provider_media_usage(prepared, response)
        result = RealPromptResult(
            node_result=node_result,
            invocation_evidence=evidence,
            request_correlation_id=response.request_correlation_id,
            upstream_status_code=response.upstream_status_code,
            input_tokens=input_tokens,
            output_tokens=output_tokens,
            total_tokens=total_tokens,
            media_usage=media_usage,
            cache_usage=response.cache_usage,
            request_usages=({"input_tokens": input_tokens, "output_tokens": output_tokens},),
            media_request_input_tokens=input_tokens,
        )
    except DoubaoStructuredOutputError as exc:
        _settle_paid_acceptance_call(
            reservation,
            outcome="failed",
            provider_correlation_id=exc.request_correlation_id,
        )
        error = WorkflowModelExecutorError(
            "structured_output_schema_mismatch",
            "模型输出不符合提示词节点冻结的 JSON Schema",
            evidence=(
                {**({"raw_excerpt": exc.raw_excerpt} if getattr(exc, "raw_excerpt", "") else {}),
                 **({"output_normalization": redact_workflow_secrets(exc.evidence["output_normalization"])}
                    if (getattr(exc, "evidence", None) or {}).get("output_normalization") else {})}
                or None
            ),
            retryable=True,
        )
        for name in (
            "provider_usage", "request_correlation_id", "upstream_status_code",
            "attempt_count", "finish_reason", "provider_error_code",
            "technical_error_type",
        ):
            setattr(error, name, deepcopy(getattr(exc, name, None)))
        _tag_single_request_usage(error)
        raise error from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, WorkflowModelExecutorError) and isinstance(response, DoubaoResponse):
            # Business validation cannot erase the provider's billing evidence.
            # Invalid/missing usage remains unknown, preserving the original error.
            exc.request_correlation_id = response.request_correlation_id
            exc.upstream_status_code = response.upstream_status_code
            exc.attempt_count = response.attempt_count
            try:
                input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens = _usage(response)
            except WorkflowModelExecutorError:
                pass
            else:
                exc.provider_usage = {
                    "input_tokens": input_tokens,
                    "output_tokens": output_tokens,
                    "total_tokens": total_tokens,
                    "cached_input_tokens": response.cache_usage.cached_input_tokens,
                    "cache_observation_status": response.cache_usage.status,
                }
        _settle_paid_acceptance_call(
            reservation,
            outcome="failed",
            provider_correlation_id=getattr(
                exc, "request_correlation_id", None
            ),
        )
        _tag_single_request_usage(exc)
        raise
    _settle_paid_acceptance_call(
        reservation,
        outcome="succeeded",
        provider_correlation_id=result.request_correlation_id,
    )
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_node_executors.py =====
"""Server-owned executor registry for workflow-v3 deterministic runs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping

from .evaluation_component_catalog import require_module_type
from .knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    KnowledgeGraphContractError,
    adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output,
    asset_scope_from_is_single,
    get_knowledge_graph_contract,
    knowledge_graph_fixture_catalog,
    resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture,
    validate_knowledge_graph_output,
)
from .workflow_result_contract import (
    canonical_node_result,
    find_evidence_value,
    hash_manifest,
)
from .workflow_connector_executor import (
    WorkflowConnectorError,
    connector_node_evidence,
)
from .workflow_route_state import RouteResolutionError, resolve_route_decision


_FORBIDDEN_EXECUTION_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {"code", "command", "shell", "sql", "script", "python_source"}
)
_SECRET_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {
        "api_key",
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "dsn",
        "password",
        "secret",
        "token",
    }
)
_DEFAULT_PROMPT_FIXTURE_KEY = "prompt.default.v1"
_DEFAULT_PROMPT_FIXTURE_HASH = hashlib.sha256(
    f"workflow-prompt-fixture-v1:{_DEFAULT_PROMPT_FIXTURE_KEY}".encode("utf-8")
).hexdigest()


class NodeExecutorError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        # 诊断证据（如触发校验失败的模型原始输出），随失败一起落到
        # 步骤尝试的 checkpoint，否则排查时只有错误码、看不到模型说了什么。
        self.evidence = dict(evidence or {})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NodeExecutionContext:
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]
    node: Mapping[str, Any]
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
    raw_fixture_output: Mapping[str, Any]
    attempt_no: int
    started_at: datetime | None
    input_fingerprint: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _ExecutorOutput:
    output: Mapping[str, Any]
    extra: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None


NodeExecutor = Callable[[NodeExecutionContext], _ExecutorOutput]


def redact_workflow_secrets(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Remove secret-shaped fields from workflow context and evidence."""

    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {
            str(key): redact_workflow_secrets(child)
            for key, child in value.items()
            if str(key).lower() not in _SECRET_FIELDS
        }
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [redact_workflow_secrets(child) for child in value]
    return value


def _workflow_manifest(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    workflow = context.run_snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    return manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}


def _frozen_run_input(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    runtime_context = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    frozen = (
        runtime_context.get("input_manifest")
        if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    return frozen if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) else context.input_manifest


def _knowledge_graph_input_contract(
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    source = input_manifest.get("source")
    if not isinstance(source, str) or source not in {"domestic", "overseas"}:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_input_source_invalid",
            "知识图谱输入必须明确为国内或海外来源",
        )
    is_single = input_manifest.get("is_single")
    asset_scope = asset_scope_from_is_single(source, is_single)
    if asset_scope is None:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_input_is_single_invalid",
            "知识图谱输入必须明确整体或单体（国内 0/1，海外 1/2）",
        )
    identity_field = "ll_id" if source == "domestic" else "res_id"
    other_identity = "res_id" if source == "domestic" else "ll_id"
    identity = input_manifest.get(identity_field)
    if other_identity in input_manifest:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_input_identity_conflict",
            "知识图谱输入同时包含国内和海外素材身份",
        )
    if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip():
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_input_identity_missing",
            f"知识图谱输入缺少 {identity_field}",
        )
    fixture_key = input_manifest.get("fixture_key")
    if not isinstance(fixture_key, str) or not fixture_key:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_missing",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 输入缺少确定性夹具",
        )
    expected_contract = f"kg.{category_key}.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1"
    actual_contract = knowledge_graph_fixture_catalog(category_key).get(
        fixture_key
    )
    if actual_contract is None:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_unknown",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 夹具不存在",
        )
    if actual_contract != expected_contract:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_route_mismatch",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 夹具与来源、整体/单体路线不一致",
        )
    return expected_contract, identity_field, identity


def _upstream_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    if _workflow_manifest(context).get("category_key") == "model_3d_su_joint":
        from .joint_model_workflow import validate_joint_input

        try:
            validate_joint_input(context.input_manifest)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError("joint_input_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    if _workflow_manifest(context).get("lane_key") == "knowledge_graph":
        _knowledge_graph_input_contract(
            context.input_manifest,
            category_key=str(
                _workflow_manifest(context).get("category_key")
                or "model_3d_su"
            ),
        )
    output = redact_workflow_secrets(context.input_manifest)
    try:
        evidence = connector_node_evidence(
            run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot,
            node=context.node,
            payload=context.input_manifest,
        )
    except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    return _ExecutorOutput(output=output, extra=evidence)


def _knowledge_graph_output(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    category_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    contract_hash = snapshot.get("contract_hash")
    canonical_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
    canonical_snapshot.pop("contract_hash", None)
    if (
        snapshot.get("schema_version") != "kg-model-output-contract-v1"
        or not isinstance(contract_hash, str)
        or hash_manifest(canonical_snapshot) != contract_hash
    ):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 的冻结字段合同损坏",
        )
    contract_key = snapshot.get("contract_key")
    identity_field = snapshot.get("identity_field")
    field_order = snapshot.get("field_order")
    is_single = snapshot.get("is_single")
    if (
        not isinstance(contract_key, str)
        or get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key) is None
        or not isinstance(identity_field, str)
        or not isinstance(field_order, list)
        or identity_field not in field_order
        or "is_single" not in field_order
        or is_single not in {0, 1, 2}
    ):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 的身份或路由合同损坏",
        )
    expected_contract, expected_identity_field, identity = (
        _knowledge_graph_input_contract(
            input_manifest,
            category_key=category_key,
        )
    )
    if (expected_contract.rsplit(".", 1)[0] != contract_key.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
        or expected_identity_field != identity_field):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_route_mismatch",
            "知识图谱提示词字段合同与本次选中路线不一致",
        )
    fixture_key = str(input_manifest["fixture_key"])
    try:
        return resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            fixture_key,
            contract_key,
            expected_asset_id=identity,
            snapshot=snapshot,
        )
    except KnowledgeGraphContractError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc


def _knowledge_graph_compatibility_output(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any], input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Retain historical generic-lane fixtures while new KG runs use strict input."""

    contract_hash = snapshot.get("contract_hash")
    canonical_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
    canonical_snapshot.pop("contract_hash", None)
    identity_field = snapshot.get("identity_field")
    field_order = snapshot.get("field_order")
    is_single = snapshot.get("is_single")
    if (
        snapshot.get("schema_version") != "kg-model-output-contract-v1"
        or not isinstance(contract_hash, str)
        or hash_manifest(canonical_snapshot) != contract_hash
        or not isinstance(identity_field, str)
        or not isinstance(field_order, list)
        or identity_field not in field_order
        or "is_single" not in field_order
        or is_single not in {0, 1, 2}
    ):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 的冻结字段合同损坏",
        )
    found, identity = find_evidence_value(input_manifest, identity_field)
    if not found or not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "kg_fixture_identity_missing",
            f"知识图谱 dry-run 输入缺少 {identity_field}",
        )
    return {
        str(field): (
            identity
            if field == identity_field
            else is_single
            if field == "is_single"
            else ""
        )
        for field in field_order
    }


def _prompt_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    config = context.node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    contract = config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
    if isinstance(contract, Mapping):
        dedicated_lane = _workflow_manifest(context).get("lane_key") == (
            "knowledge_graph"
        )
        frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
        output = (
            _knowledge_graph_output(
                contract,
                frozen_input,
                category_key=str(
                    _workflow_manifest(context).get("category_key")
                    or "model_3d_su"
                ),
            )
            if dedicated_lane
            else _knowledge_graph_compatibility_output(
                contract, context.input_manifest
            )
        )
        fixture = (
            {
                "key": frozen_input.get("fixture_key"),
                "hash": hash_manifest(output),
            }
            if dedicated_lane
            else None
        )
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=output,
            extra={
                "output_contract_key": contract.get("contract_key"),
                "output_contract_hash": contract.get("contract_hash"),
                "output_contract_snapshot": contract,
                **({"fixture": fixture} if fixture is not None else {}),
            },
        )
    runtime_context = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    frozen_input = (
        runtime_context.get("input_manifest", {})
        if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    workflow_fixture = (
        frozen_input.get("workflow_fixture")
        if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if workflow_fixture is not None:
        prompt_outputs = (
            workflow_fixture.get("prompt_outputs")
            if isinstance(workflow_fixture, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
        if (
            not isinstance(workflow_fixture, Mapping)
            or workflow_fixture.get("schema_version")
            != "native-prompt-fixtures-v1"
            or not isinstance(prompt_outputs, Mapping)
            or not isinstance(asset_key, str)
            or not isinstance(prompt_outputs.get(asset_key), Mapping)
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "native_prompt_fixture_invalid",
                "冻结提示词夹具与当前提示词节点不匹配",
            )
        output = deepcopy(dict(prompt_outputs[asset_key]))
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=output,
            extra={
                "fixture": {
                    "key": f"native-prompt-fixtures-v1:{asset_key}",
                    "hash": hash_manifest(output),
                }
            },
        )
    strict_contract = config.get("output_contract")
    if (
        isinstance(strict_contract, Mapping)
        and strict_contract.get("mode") == "json_schema"
        and strict_contract.get("strict") is True
        and isinstance(strict_contract.get("schema"), Mapping)
    ):
        output = _deterministic_schema_value(strict_contract["schema"])
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "native_prompt_fixture_invalid",
                "严格结构化输出的根 Schema 必须生成对象",
            )
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=output,
            extra={
                "fixture": {
                    "key": "prompt.strict-json-schema-simulation.v1",
                    "hash": hash_manifest(output),
                },
                "simulation": {
                    "provider_called": False,
                    "contract_mode": "json_schema",
                },
            },
        )
    fingerprint = context.input_fingerprint or hash_manifest(context.input_manifest)
    return _ExecutorOutput(
        output={
            "fixture": "deterministic_prompt",
            "input_hash": fingerprint,
        },
        extra={
            "fixture": {
                "key": _DEFAULT_PROMPT_FIXTURE_KEY,
                "hash": _DEFAULT_PROMPT_FIXTURE_HASH,
            }
        },
    )


def _deterministic_schema_value(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """Create one schema-valid value for explicit quick-simulation runs only."""

    # 冻结常量优先：严格合同用 const 钉 contract_version 等字段，模拟值必须相等。
    if "const" in schema:
        return deepcopy(schema["const"])
    enum = schema.get("enum")
    if isinstance(enum, list) and enum:
        return deepcopy(enum[0])
    raw_type = schema.get("type")
    candidate_types = raw_type if isinstance(raw_type, list) else [raw_type]
    value_type = next(
        (
            item
            for item in candidate_types
            if isinstance(item, str) and item != "null"
        ),
        "null",
    )
    if value_type == "object":
        properties = schema.get("properties")
        properties = properties if isinstance(properties, Mapping) else {}
        required = schema.get("required")
        required = required if isinstance(required, list) else []
        return {
            str(key): _deterministic_schema_value(child)
            for key, child in properties.items()
            if key in required and isinstance(child, Mapping)
        }
    if value_type == "array":
        item_schema = schema.get("items")
        minimum = schema.get("minItems", 0)
        count = (
            minimum
            if isinstance(minimum, int) and not isinstance(minimum, bool)
            else 0
        )
        return [
            _deterministic_schema_value(item_schema)
            for _ in range(max(0, count))
        ] if isinstance(item_schema, Mapping) else []
    if value_type in {"integer", "number"}:
        minimum = schema.get("minimum", 0)
        if not isinstance(minimum, (int, float)) or isinstance(minimum, bool):
            minimum = 0
        return int(minimum) if value_type == "integer" else float(minimum)
    if value_type == "boolean":
        return False
    if value_type == "string":
        min_length = schema.get("minLength", 0)
        if isinstance(min_length, int) and not isinstance(min_length, bool) and min_length > 0:
            return "模拟" * ((min_length + 1) // 2)
        return ""
    return None


def _numeric_input(context: NodeExecutionContext, field: str) -> float:
    found, value = find_evidence_value(context.input_manifest, field)
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return float(value)
    return 0.0


def _eligibility_redline_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {
        "hit": False,
        "matched_rules": [],
        "level_cap": None,
        "reason": "",
    }


def _track_routing_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {"track_key": "", "confidence": "low", "reason": ""}


def _media_adjustment_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {"media_type": "", "score_adjustment": 0.0, "reason": ""}


def _quality_cap_exception_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {
        "quality_grade": "",
        "score_cap": 100.0,
        "exception_applied": False,
        "reason": "",
    }


def _dimension_scoring_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {
        "dimensions": {},
        "total_score": _numeric_input(context, "score"),
        "reason": "",
    }


def _anchor_calibration_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return {
        "anchor_id": "",
        "calibrated_score": _numeric_input(context, "score"),
        "reason": "",
    }


def _score_level_matcher_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    config = context.node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
    snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else {}
    raw_content = snapshot.get("content_json")
    content: Mapping[str, Any] = {}
    if isinstance(raw_content, str):
        try:
            decoded = json.loads(raw_content)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_rule_snapshot_invalid",
                "分数汇总节点的冻结规则内容损坏",
            ) from exc
        if isinstance(decoded, Mapping):
            content = decoded
    formula = content.get("case_score_formula")
    if isinstance(formula, Mapping):
        return _case_final_score(context, formula, content.get("levels"))
    return {
        "score": _numeric_input(context, "score"),
        "level": "",
        "reason": "",
    }


def _case_final_score(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
    formula: Mapping[str, Any],
    raw_levels: Any,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if (
        formula.get("schema_version") != "case-final-score-v1"
        or formula.get("comparison") != "strict_gt"
        or formula.get("deletion_mode") != "recommend_only"
    ):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分只允许冻结的 strict_gt 与 recommend_only 合同",
        )

    def required_number(config_key: str, *, allow_frozen_input: bool) -> float:
        field = formula.get(config_key)
        if not isinstance(field, str) or not field.strip():
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_case_score_formula_invalid",
                f"案例最终评分缺少字段映射：{config_key}",
            )
        found, value = find_evidence_value(context.input_manifest, field)
        if not found and allow_frozen_input:
            frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
            frozen_case = frozen_input.get("case")
            if isinstance(frozen_case, Mapping) and field in frozen_case:
                found, value = True, frozen_case[field]
        if (
            not found
            or not isinstance(value, (int, float))
            or isinstance(value, bool)
            or not math.isfinite(float(value))
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_case_score_input_invalid",
                f"案例最终评分缺少有限数值字段：{field}",
            )
        return float(value)

    # The paid prompt output is authoritative for model_score. Falling back to
    # frozen baseline truth would leak expected_result.score into production
    # scoring when a provider omitted the field. Case metadata scores and the
    # threshold are immutable intake facts and may be read from frozen input.
    model_score = required_number("model_score_field", allow_frozen_input=False)
    quality_score = required_number("quality_score_field", allow_frozen_input=True)
    time_score = required_number("time_score_field", allow_frozen_input=True)
    filter_threshold = required_number("threshold_field", allow_frozen_input=True)
    final_score = model_score + quality_score + time_score
    if not math.isfinite(final_score):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_case_score_result_invalid",
            "案例最终评分聚合结果不是有限数值",
        )
    passed = final_score > filter_threshold
    if not isinstance(raw_levels, list):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分缺少冻结的 L1-L5 等级表",
        )
    eligible_levels: list[tuple[float, str]] = []
    for item in raw_levels:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping) or item.get("enabled") is not True:
            continue
        level = item.get("level")
        minimum = item.get("min_score")
        if (
            level not in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
            or not isinstance(minimum, (int, float))
            or isinstance(minimum, bool)
            or not math.isfinite(float(minimum))
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_case_score_formula_invalid",
                "案例最终评分的冻结等级表损坏",
            )
        eligible_levels.append((float(minimum), str(level)))
    matches = [item for item in eligible_levels if final_score >= item[0]]
    if not matches:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分没有可匹配的冻结等级",
        )
    level = max(matches, key=lambda item: item[0])[1]
    comparison = (
        f"final_score {final_score:.1f} > "
        f"filter_threshold {filter_threshold:.1f}"
    )
    upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream")
    upstream = upstream if isinstance(upstream, Mapping) else {}
    prompt_results = [
        result.get("output")
        for result in upstream.values()
        if isinstance(result, Mapping)
        and result.get("node_kind") == "prompt"
        and isinstance(result.get("output"), Mapping)
    ]
    if len(prompt_results) != 1:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_case_review_input_invalid",
            "案例最终评分必须且只能接收一个冻结提示词结果",
        )
    return {
        "score": final_score,
        "level": level,
        "filter_status": "pass" if passed else "filter",
        "reason": comparison,
        "model_score": model_score,
        "quality_score": quality_score,
        "time_score": time_score,
        "final_score": final_score,
        "filter_threshold": filter_threshold,
        "filter_result": 1 if passed else 2,
        "delete_recommendation": not passed,
        "recommended_image_ids": [],
        "case_review": deepcopy(dict(prompt_results[0])),
        "side_effect_executed": False,
        "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
    }


_RULE_EXECUTORS: dict[
    str, Callable[[NodeExecutionContext], Mapping[str, Any]]
] = {
    "eligibility_redline": _eligibility_redline_executor,
    "track_routing": _track_routing_executor,
    "media_adjustment": _media_adjustment_executor,
    "quality_cap_exception": _quality_cap_exception_executor,
    "dimension_scoring": _dimension_scoring_executor,
    "anchor_calibration": _anchor_calibration_executor,
    "score_level_matcher": _score_level_matcher_executor,
    # 真正的撞库在收尾归并阶段（dictionary_filter.apply_dictionary_filters）执行
    "dictionary_filter": lambda context: {"matched": [], "unmatched": []},
    # 分类定级表在收尾归并阶段查表定级（workflow_result_finalizer._classification_rule_decision）
    "classification_level_map": lambda context: {"level": "", "group": "", "track_key": "", "rule_key": ""},
    # 上游类型前置短路：把冻结输入里的素材类型规范化后写进输出，短路决定由运行时按模块内容生成的规则在节点完成时挂上
    "upstream_type_short_circuit": lambda context: _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(context),
}


def _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(
    context: NodeExecutionContext,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    from .upstream_type_short_circuit import (
        DEFAULT_FIELD_PATH,
        NORMALIZED_OUTPUT_FIELD,
        frozen_node_content,
        read_upstream_type,
        resolve_upstream_type,
    )

    content = frozen_node_content(context.node)
    if content is None:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "upstream_type_short_circuit_content_missing",
            "上游类型前置短路节点缺少冻结的模块内容",
        )
    frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
    raw, normalized = read_upstream_type(content, frozen_input)
    if not normalized:
        raw, normalized = read_upstream_type(content, context.input_manifest)
    source = frozen_input if normalized and read_upstream_type(content, frozen_input)[1] else context.input_manifest
    hit = resolve_upstream_type(content, source)
    return {
        "field_path": str(content.get("field_path") or DEFAULT_FIELD_PATH),
        "material_type": raw if isinstance(raw, (str, int, float)) and not isinstance(raw, bool) else None,
        NORMALIZED_OUTPUT_FIELD: normalized,
        "matched_rule_key": hit["rule_key"] if hit else None,
        "matched_label": hit["label"] if hit else None,
        "target_level": hit["level"] if hit else None,
    }


def _rule_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    config = context.node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    module_type_key = str(config.get("module_type_key") or "")
    executor = _RULE_EXECUTORS.get(module_type_key)
    if executor is None:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_executor_missing",
            f"规则模块没有注册执行器：{module_type_key or '（空）'}",
        )
    return _ExecutorOutput(output=executor(context))


def _branch_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    workflow = context.run_snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行冻结快照缺少工作流定义",
        )
    runtime_context = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    frozen_input = (
        runtime_context.get("input_manifest", {})
        if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream")
    try:
        decision = resolve_route_decision(
            manifest,
            str(context.node.get("node_instance_id")),
            input_manifest=(
                frozen_input if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping) else {}
            ),
            upstream_outputs=(upstream if isinstance(upstream, Mapping) else {}),
        )
    except RouteResolutionError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    payload = decision.as_result_payload()
    return _ExecutorOutput(
        output={"route_decision": payload},
        extra={"route_decision": payload},
    )


def _join_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream")
    skipped = context.input_manifest.get("skipped_upstream")
    return _ExecutorOutput(
        output={
            "successful_predecessors": sorted(upstream)
            if isinstance(upstream, Mapping)
            else [],
            "skipped_predecessors": sorted(skipped)
            if isinstance(skipped, list)
            else [],
        }
    )


def is_legacy_evaluation_projection(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], node: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> bool:
    """Recognize historical evaluation outputs without overriding explicit contracts."""
    # Knowledge graph workflows can share the model_3d_su asset category;
    # their frozen prompt contract produces tags, not an L1-L5 evaluation.
    for item in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping) or item.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        prompt_config = item.get("config")
        if (isinstance(prompt_config, Mapping)
                and str(prompt_config.get("output_contract_key", "")).startswith("kg.")
                and isinstance(prompt_config.get("output_contract_snapshot"), Mapping)):
            return False
    config = node.get("config")
    return (
        manifest.get("category_key") in {"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"}
        and node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
        and isinstance(config, Mapping)
