        and not any(key in config for key in ("mapping", "field_contract_snapshot", "field_contract_key"))
    )


def _factory_result_projection(context: NodeExecutionContext, mapping: Mapping[str, Any]) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    """Local Factory delivery shape, after all material nodes, never an HTTP call.

    External API field names await its versioned contract. Preserve A facts and
    B's foundation score separately from the server-owned final result.
    """
    from .workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result
    from .workflow_short_circuit import enabled_levels_from_manifest

    manifest = _workflow_manifest(context)
    node_id = context.node.get("node_instance_id")
    nodes = {n.get("node_instance_id"): n for n in manifest.get("nodes", []) if isinstance(n, Mapping)}
    a_id, b_id = mapping.get("prompt_a_node_id"), mapping.get("prompt_b_node_id")
    if (manifest.get("category_key") != "model_3d_su" or
            not isinstance(a_id, str) or not isinstance(b_id, str) or a_id == b_id or
            any(nodes.get(k, {}).get("kind") != "prompt" for k in (a_id, b_id)) or
            any(e.get("from") == node_id for e in manifest.get("edges", []) if isinstance(e, Mapping))):
        raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_contract_invalid", "Factory投递必须绑定A/B节点并位于素材流程末尾")
    upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream", {})
    skipped = context.input_manifest.get("skipped_upstream", [])
    if not isinstance(upstream, Mapping) or not isinstance(skipped, list):
        raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_incomplete", "素材节点证据不完整")
    for key in nodes.keys() - {node_id}:
        entry = upstream.get(key, {})
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping) or (entry.get("status") not in {"succeeded", "skipped"} and key not in skipped):
            raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_incomplete", "素材流程尚未完整结束，禁止投递")
    runtime = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
    try:
        normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, upstream,
            model_execution_mode=runtime.get("model_execution_mode", "simulated"))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    if normalized.get("level") not in enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest):
        raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_invalid", "缺少有效最终等级")
    stages = []
    for key in (a_id, b_id):
        entry = upstream.get(key, {})
        is_skipped = entry.get("status") == "skipped" or key in skipped
        if is_skipped:
            decision = normalized.get("short_circuit_decision", {})
            if (normalized.get("decision_source") != "frozen_short_circuit"
                    or key not in decision.get("skipped_nodes", [])):
                raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_invalid", "缺失提示词只能由冻结短路解释")
            stages.append(("skipped", None))
        elif isinstance(entry.get("output"), Mapping):
            stages.append(("succeeded", redact_workflow_secrets(deepcopy(dict(entry["output"])))))
        else:
            raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_invalid", "缺少结构化提示词输出")
    (a_status, a), (b_status, b) = stages
    b_score = b.get("aesthetic_score") if b is not None else None
    if b is not None and (type(b_score) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(b_score) or not 0 <= b_score <= 100):
        raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_invalid", "B美感分数缺失或无效，禁止回退到A参考分或最终分")
    output = dict(schema_version="factory-result-v1", prompt_a_fields=a, prompt_a_status=a_status,
        prompt_b_aesthetic_score=b_score, prompt_b_status=b_status,
        final_score=normalized.get("score"), final_level=normalized["level"],
        normalized_result=normalized, external_io_performed=False)
    frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
    if frozen_input.get("source_system") == "factory-test-material":
        from .incremental_input import IncrementalInputError, validate_factory_task_identity
        try:
            output["factory_task"] = validate_factory_task_identity(frozen_input, frozen_input.get("factory_task"))
        except IncrementalInputError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError("factory_projection_identity_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    return _ExecutorOutput(output=output, extra=connector_node_evidence(
        run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot, node=context.node, payload=output))


def _projection_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    config = context.node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    mapping = config.get("mapping")
    if isinstance(mapping, Mapping) and mapping.get("schema_version") == "factory-result-v1":
        return _factory_result_projection(context, mapping)
    if isinstance(mapping, Mapping) and mapping.get("schema_version") == "inspiration-factory-result-v1":
        from .inspiration_factory_projection import project_inspiration
        return project_inspiration(context, mapping)
    if is_legacy_evaluation_projection(_workflow_manifest(context), context.node):
        from .workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result

        runtime = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
        try:
            normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(
                _workflow_manifest(context), context.input_manifest.get("upstream", {}),
                model_execution_mode=runtime.get("model_execution_mode", "simulated"),
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError("native_projection_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
        from .workflow_short_circuit import enabled_levels_from_manifest

        if normalized.get("level") not in enabled_levels_from_manifest(_workflow_manifest(context)):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "native_projection_invalid", "评测结果缺少冻结等级表中的有效等级",
            )
        output = {
            "schema_version": "native-evaluation-projection-v1",
            "normalized_result": normalized,
            "external_io_performed": False,
        }
        return _ExecutorOutput(output=output, extra=connector_node_evidence(
            run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot, node=context.node, payload=output,
        ))
    mapping = config.get("mapping")
    if isinstance(mapping, Mapping) and mapping.get("schema_version") == "joint-model-projection-v1":
        from .workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result

        runtime = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
        try:
            normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(
                _workflow_manifest(context), context.input_manifest.get("upstream", {}),
                model_execution_mode=runtime.get("model_execution_mode", "simulated"),
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError("joint_projection_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
        output = {"schema_version":"joint-model-projection-v1", "normalized_result":normalized,
                  "external_io_performed":False}
        return _ExecutorOutput(output=output, extra=connector_node_evidence(
            run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot, node=context.node, payload=output))
    if (
        isinstance(mapping, Mapping)
        and mapping.get("schema_version")
        == "case-recommendation-projection-v1"
    ):
        final_node_id = mapping.get("final_score_node_id")
        review_node_id = mapping.get("case_review_node_id")
        if not all(
            isinstance(value, str) and value
            for value in (final_node_id, review_node_id)
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "case_projection_contract_invalid",
                "案例下游投影缺少冻结来源节点",
            )
        upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream")
        upstream = upstream if isinstance(upstream, Mapping) else {}

        def predecessor_output(node_id: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
            result = upstream.get(node_id)
            output = result.get("output") if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
            if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "case_projection_input_missing",
                    f"案例下游投影缺少节点 {node_id} 的结构化输出",
                )
            return output

        final_output = predecessor_output(str(final_node_id))
        embedded_case_review = final_output.get("case_review")
        case_review = (
            embedded_case_review
            if isinstance(embedded_case_review, Mapping)
            else predecessor_output(str(review_node_id))
        )
        score_fields = (
            "score",
            "level",
            "filter_status",
            "reason",
            "model_score",
            "quality_score",
            "time_score",
            "final_score",
            "filter_threshold",
            "filter_result",
            "delete_recommendation",
            "recommended_image_ids",
            "case_review",
            "side_effect_executed",
            "deletion_mode",
        )
        missing = [field for field in score_fields if field not in final_output]
        if missing:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "case_projection_input_invalid",
                "案例最终分输出缺少字段：" + ",".join(missing),
            )
        if (
            final_output.get("side_effect_executed") is not False
            or final_output.get("deletion_mode") != "recommend_only"
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "case_projection_side_effect_forbidden",
                "案例候选只允许输出删图建议，不能执行物理副作用",
            )
        frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
        case_input = frozen_input.get("case")
        case_cloud_id = (
            case_input.get("cloud_id")
            if isinstance(case_input, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(case_cloud_id, str) or not case_cloud_id:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "case_projection_identity_missing",
                "案例下游投影缺少冻结 case.cloud_id",
            )
        normalized_result = {
            "schema_version": "design-case-evaluation-result-v1",
            "case_cloud_id": case_cloud_id,
            **{field: deepcopy(final_output[field]) for field in score_fields},
            "case_review": deepcopy(dict(case_review)),
        }
        output = {
            "schema_version": "case-recommendation-projection-v1",
            "source_node_ids": [str(review_node_id), str(final_node_id)],
            "normalized_result": normalized_result,
            "side_effect_executed": False,
            "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
            "external_io_performed": False,
        }
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=output,
            extra=connector_node_evidence(
                run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot,
                node=context.node,
                payload=output,
            ),
        )
    snapshot = config.get("field_contract_snapshot")
    if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        contract_hash = snapshot.get("contract_hash")
        canonical_snapshot = dict(snapshot)
        canonical_snapshot.pop("contract_hash", None)
        contract_key = snapshot.get("contract_key")
        if (
            snapshot.get("schema_version") != "kg-model-output-contract-v1"
            or not isinstance(contract_hash, str)
            or hash_manifest(canonical_snapshot) != contract_hash
            or not isinstance(contract_key, str)
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "kg_projection_contract_snapshot_invalid",
                "知识图谱下游节点的冻结字段合同损坏",
            )
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
        if contract is None:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "kg_projection_contract_unknown",
                "知识图谱下游节点引用的字段合同不存在",
            )
        expected_contract, identity_field, identity = (
            _knowledge_graph_input_contract(
                _frozen_run_input(context),
                category_key=str(
                    _workflow_manifest(context).get("category_key")
                    or "model_3d_su"
                ),
            )
        )
        if (
            expected_contract.rsplit(".", 1)[0] != contract_key.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
            or identity_field != contract.identity_field
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "kg_projection_route_mismatch",
                "知识图谱下游字段合同与本次路线不一致",
            )
        logical_target = config.get("logical_target")
        expected_target = (
            "knowledge_graph_domestic"
            if contract.site_scope == "domestic"
            else "knowledge_graph_overseas"
        )
        if logical_target != expected_target:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "kg_projection_target_mismatch",
                "知识图谱逻辑下游与字段合同来源不一致",
            )
        upstream = context.input_manifest.get("upstream")
        candidates = []
        if isinstance(upstream, Mapping):
            for value in upstream.values():
                output = value.get("output") if isinstance(value, Mapping) else None
                if isinstance(output, Mapping):
                    candidates.append(output)
        if len(candidates) != 1:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "kg_projection_prompt_output_missing",
                "知识图谱下游节点没有唯一的提示词输出",
            )
        try:
            payload = validate_knowledge_graph_output(
                contract_key,
                candidates[0],
                expected_asset_id=identity,
                snapshot=snapshot,
            )
            canonical_mapping = adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output(
                contract_key,
                payload,
                expected_asset_id=identity,
                snapshot=snapshot,
                evidence_prefix=(
                    f"node:{context.node.get('node_instance_id')}:payload"
                ),
            )
        except KnowledgeGraphContractError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                evidence={
                    "contract_key": contract_key,
                    "rejected_model_output": dict(candidates[0]),
                },
            ) from exc
        payload_hash = hash_manifest(payload)
        connector_evidence = connector_node_evidence(
            run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot,
            node=context.node,
            payload={
                "logical_target": logical_target,
                "contract_key": contract_key,
                "canonical_mapping": canonical_mapping,
                "payload": payload,
                "payload_hash": payload_hash,
                "row_count": 1,
                "read_back": {
                    "matched": True,
                    "payload_hash": payload_hash,
                },
            },
        )
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output={
                "logical_target": logical_target,
                "contract_key": contract_key,
                "canonical_mapping": canonical_mapping,
                "payload": payload,
                "payload_hash": payload_hash,
                "row_count": 1,
                "read_back": {
                    "matched": True,
                    "payload_hash": payload_hash,
                },
            },
            extra={
                "field_contract_key": contract_key,
                "field_contract_hash": contract_hash,
                **connector_evidence,
            },
        )
    fingerprint = context.input_fingerprint or hash_manifest(context.input_manifest)
    output = {
        "fixture": "logical_projection",
        "input_hash": fingerprint,
    }
    return _ExecutorOutput(
        output=output,
        extra=connector_node_evidence(
            run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot,
            node=context.node,
            payload=output,
        ),
    )


def _first_seen_result_hash(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    basis = deepcopy(dict(payload))
    evidence = basis.get("evidence")
    if isinstance(evidence, dict):
        evidence.pop("result_hash", None)
    return hash_manifest(basis)


def _verified_first_seen_result(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "first_seen_result_invalid",
            "图片最早发现结果必须是对象",
        )
    result = deepcopy(dict(value))
    evidence = result.get("evidence")
    supplied_hash = (
        evidence.get("result_hash") if isinstance(evidence, Mapping) else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(supplied_hash, str)
        or supplied_hash != _first_seen_result_hash(result)
    ):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "first_seen_result_hash_mismatch",
            "图片最早发现结果摘要校验失败",
        )
    return result


def _first_seen_checked_at(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> str:
    value = context.started_at or datetime(1970, 1, 1)
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def _first_seen_executor(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> _ExecutorOutput:
    runtime_context = context.run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    runtime_context = (
        runtime_context if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping) else {}
    )
    run_mode = runtime_context.get("run_mode")
    node_id = str(context.node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
    config = context.node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}

    if run_mode == "incremental":
        result = _verified_first_seen_result(context.input_manifest)
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=result,
            extra={
                "external_io_performed": False,
                "io_mode": "callback_fact",
                "provider_connector_key": config.get(
                    "provider_connector_key"
                ),
            },
        )

    frozen_input = _frozen_run_input(context)
    node_evidence = frozen_input.get("workflow_node_evidence")
    imported = (
        node_evidence.get(node_id)
        if isinstance(node_evidence, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if imported is not None:
        result = _verified_first_seen_result(imported)
        return _ExecutorOutput(
            output=result,
            extra={
                "external_io_performed": False,
                "io_mode": "imported_evidence",
                "provider_connector_key": config.get(
                    "provider_connector_key"
                ),
            },
        )

    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "status": "unavailable",
        "web_first_seen_at": None,
        "source_url": None,
        "provider": "unconfigured",
        "match_kind": "unknown",
        "confidence": 0.0,
        "checked_at": _first_seen_checked_at(context),
        "evidence": {
            "provider_result_id": None,
            "matched_urls_count": 0,
            "result_hash": "",
        },
    }
    result["evidence"]["result_hash"] = _first_seen_result_hash(result)
    return _ExecutorOutput(
        output=result,
        extra={
            "external_io_performed": False,
            "io_mode": "unavailable",
            "provider_connector_key": config.get("provider_connector_key"),
        },
    )


_EXECUTORS: dict[str, NodeExecutor] = {
    "upstream_connector": _upstream_executor,
    "prompt": _prompt_executor,
    "rule_module": _rule_executor,
    "branch": _branch_executor,
    "join": _join_executor,
    "image_first_seen_lookup": _first_seen_executor,
    "downstream_projection": _projection_executor,
}

# These nodes own multi-record or deterministic reconcile state and are executed
# by workflow_fixture_executor rather than the single-node adapter below.
_RUNTIME_MANAGED_EXECUTORS = frozenset({"foreach_image", "aggregate_case"})


def registered_node_kinds() -> set[str]:
    return set(_EXECUTORS) | set(_RUNTIME_MANAGED_EXECUTORS)


def registered_rule_module_types() -> set[str]:
    return set(_RULE_EXECUTORS)


def _is_finite_json_number(value: Any) -> bool:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return False
    return isinstance(value, int) or math.isfinite(value)


def _matches_json_type(value: Any, expected: str) -> bool:
    if expected == "object":
        return isinstance(value, Mapping)
    if expected == "array":
        return isinstance(value, list)
    if expected == "string":
        return isinstance(value, str)
    if expected == "number":
        return _is_finite_json_number(value)
    if expected == "integer":
        return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
    if expected == "boolean":
        return isinstance(value, bool)
    if expected == "null":
        return value is None
    return False


def _validate_output_value(value: Any, schema: Any, *, path: str = "output") -> None:
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
            "节点冻结输出 Schema 损坏",
        )
    expected = schema.get("type")
    expected_types = expected if isinstance(expected, list) else [expected]
    if not expected_types or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in expected_types):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
            "节点冻结输出 Schema 缺少合法类型",
        )
    if not any(_matches_json_type(value, item) for item in expected_types):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
            f"节点输出字段 {path} 类型不符合冻结合同",
        )
    if "enum" in schema and value not in schema["enum"]:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
            f"节点输出字段 {path} 不在允许值范围内",
        )
    if "const" in schema and value != schema["const"]:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
            f"节点输出字段 {path} 不等于冻结常量",
        )
    if isinstance(value, Mapping) and "object" in expected_types:
        properties = schema.get("properties", {})
        properties = properties if isinstance(properties, Mapping) else {}
        required = schema.get("required", [])
        if not isinstance(required, list) or any(
            not isinstance(item, str) for item in required
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                "节点冻结输出 Schema 的必填字段配置损坏",
            )
        missing = [field for field in required if field not in value]
        if missing:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                f"节点输出缺少冻结必填字段：{','.join(missing)}",
            )
        extras = sorted(set(value) - set(properties))
        additional = schema.get("additionalProperties", True)
        if additional is False:
            if extras:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点输出包含合同外字段：{','.join(extras)}",
                )
        elif isinstance(additional, Mapping):
            for field in extras:
                _validate_output_value(
                    value[field],
                    additional,
                    path=f"{path}.{field}",
                )
        elif additional is not True:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                "节点冻结输出 Schema 的 additionalProperties 配置损坏",
            )
        for field, child_schema in properties.items():
            if field in value:
                _validate_output_value(
                    value[field],
                    child_schema,
                    path=f"{path}.{field}",
                )
    if isinstance(value, list) and "array" in expected_types:
        minimum_items = schema.get("minItems")
        maximum_items = schema.get("maxItems")
        if (
            minimum_items is not None
            and (
                not isinstance(minimum_items, int)
                or isinstance(minimum_items, bool)
                or minimum_items < 0
            )
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                "节点冻结输出 Schema 的 minItems 配置损坏",
            )
        if (
            maximum_items is not None
            and (
                not isinstance(maximum_items, int)
                or isinstance(maximum_items, bool)
                or maximum_items < 0
            )
        ):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                "节点冻结输出 Schema 的 maxItems 配置损坏",
            )
        if minimum_items is not None and len(value) < minimum_items:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                f"节点输出字段 {path} 少于 minItems",
            )
        if maximum_items is not None and len(value) > maximum_items:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                f"节点输出字段 {path} 超过 maxItems",
            )
        item_schema = schema.get("items")
        if item_schema is not None:
            for index, item in enumerate(value):
                _validate_output_value(
                    item,
                    item_schema,
                    path=f"{path}[{index}]",
                )
    if isinstance(value, str) and "string" in expected_types:
        for keyword, predicate, message in (
            ("minLength", lambda length, bound: length >= bound, "少于 minLength"),
            ("maxLength", lambda length, bound: length <= bound, "超过 maxLength"),
        ):
            bound = schema.get(keyword)
            if bound is None:
                continue
            if (
                not isinstance(bound, int)
                or isinstance(bound, bool)
                or bound < 0
            ):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点冻结输出 Schema 的 {keyword} 配置损坏",
                )
            if not predicate(len(value), bound):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点输出字段 {path} {message}",
                )
        pattern = schema.get("pattern")
        if pattern is not None:
            if not isinstance(pattern, str):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    "节点冻结输出 Schema 的 pattern 配置损坏",
                )
            try:
                matched = re.search(pattern, value)
            except re.error as exc:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    "节点冻结输出 Schema 的 pattern 配置损坏",
                ) from exc
            if matched is None:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点输出字段 {path} 不符合 pattern",
                )
    if (
        isinstance(value, (int, float))
        and not isinstance(value, bool)
        and ({"integer", "number"} & set(expected_types))
    ):
        comparisons = (
            ("minimum", lambda actual, bound: actual >= bound),
            ("maximum", lambda actual, bound: actual <= bound),
            ("exclusiveMinimum", lambda actual, bound: actual > bound),
            ("exclusiveMaximum", lambda actual, bound: actual < bound),
        )
        for keyword, predicate in comparisons:
            if keyword not in schema:
                continue
            bound = schema[keyword]
            if not _is_finite_json_number(bound):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点冻结输出 Schema 的 {keyword} 配置损坏",
                )
            if not predicate(value, bound):
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid",
                    f"节点输出字段 {path} 不符合 {keyword}",
                )


def _reject_executable_fields(
    value: Any,
    *,
    path: str = "$",
    schema_context: bool = False,
) -> None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            normalized_key = str(key).lower()
            if not schema_context and normalized_key in _FORBIDDEN_EXECUTION_FIELDS:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden",
                    f"工作流包含禁止的执行字段：{path}.{key}",
                )
            child_schema_context = schema_context or normalized_key in {
                "input_schema",
                "output_schema",
            } or (
                normalized_key == "schema"
                and path.endswith((".output_contract", ".output_contract_snapshot"))
            )
            _reject_executable_fields(
                child,
                path=f"{path}.{key}",
                schema_context=child_schema_context,
            )
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            _reject_executable_fields(
                child,
                path=f"{path}[{index}]",
                schema_context=schema_context,
            )


def assert_native_workflow_executable(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    _reject_executable_fields(manifest)
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_executor_missing",
            "工作流没有可执行节点",
        )
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_executor_missing",
                "工作流节点不是可执行对象",
            )
        kind = node.get("kind")
        if kind not in _EXECUTORS and kind not in _RUNTIME_MANAGED_EXECUTORS:
            raise NodeExecutorError(
                "workflow_executor_missing",
                f"节点类型没有注册执行器：{kind}",
            )
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if kind == "rule_module":
            try:
                require_module_type(str(config.get("module_type_key") or ""))
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_executor_missing",
                    str(exc),
                ) from exc
            if config.get("module_type_key") not in _RULE_EXECUTORS:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_executor_missing",
                    "所选规则模块没有注册运行执行器",
                )
        if kind == "prompt" and config.get("output_contract_key"):
            if get_knowledge_graph_contract(config.get("output_contract_key")) is None:
                raise NodeExecutorError(
                    "workflow_kg_output_contract_unknown",
                    "提示词节点引用的知识图谱字段合同未注册",
                )


def validate_native_node_output(
    node: Mapping[str, Any], output: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> None:
    """Validate a human-adjusted output against the node's frozen schema."""

    _validate_output_value(output, node.get("output_schema"))


def execute_native_node(context: NodeExecutionContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    kind = context.node.get("kind")
    executor = _EXECUTORS.get(str(kind))
    if executor is None:
        raise NodeExecutorError(
            "workflow_executor_missing",
            f"节点类型没有注册执行器：{kind}",
        )
    fingerprint = context.input_fingerprint or hash_manifest(context.input_manifest)
    try:
        executed = executor(context)
    except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    validate_native_node_output(context.node, executed.output)
    if context.node.get("config", {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("name") == "model_3d_su_joint_call_a":
        from .joint_model_workflow import validate_joint_knowledge

        try:
            validate_joint_knowledge(executed.output, expected_input=_frozen_run_input(context))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError("joint_knowledge_graph_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    return canonical_node_result(
        node=context.node,
        input_manifest=context.input_manifest,
        input_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        output=executed.output,
        attempt_no=context.attempt_no,
        started_at=context.started_at,
        extra=executed.extra,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_prompt_execution.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any


def prompt_execution_node(
    frozen_node: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Return the immutable prompt executable for a prompt-like node.

    ``foreach_image`` owns one nested prompt without creating synthetic runtime
    step rows.  Its executable identity is derived solely from the frozen outer
    node, so model preparation and budget reservation can independently verify
    the exact same snapshot.
    """

    kind = frozen_node.get("kind")
    if kind == "prompt":
        return deepcopy(dict(frozen_node))
    if kind != "foreach_image":
        return None
    outer_config = frozen_node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(outer_config, Mapping):
        return None
    executor = outer_config.get("item_executor")
    if not isinstance(executor, Mapping) or executor.get("kind") != "prompt":
        return None
    output_schema: Mapping[str, Any] = {}
    contract = executor.get("output_contract")
    if isinstance(contract, Mapping) and isinstance(
        contract.get("schema"), Mapping
    ):
        output_schema = contract["schema"]
    result = deepcopy(dict(frozen_node))
    result["kind"] = "prompt"
    result["config"] = deepcopy(dict(executor))
    result["output_schema"] = deepcopy(dict(output_schema))
    return result
