class WorkflowRegistryError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 400):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _error(
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
    *,
    path: str,
    code: str,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    errors.append(ValidationErrorItem(path=path, code=code, message=message))


def _script_reference(
    db: Session,
    reference: Any,
    *,
    path: str,
    step_type: str | None,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> ScriptVersion | None:
    if not isinstance(reference, str) or "@" not in reference:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="script_reference_invalid",
            message="脚本版本引用必须使用 script_key@version",
        )
        return None
    script_key, version_name = reference.rsplit("@", 1)
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptDefinition).where(ScriptDefinition.script_key == script_key)
    )
    if definition is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="script_definition_unknown",
            message=f"未知脚本定义：{script_key}",
        )
        return None
    version = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptVersion).where(
            ScriptVersion.script_definition_id == definition.id,
            ScriptVersion.version == version_name,
        )
    )
    if version is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="script_version_unknown",
            message=f"未知脚本版本：{reference}",
        )
        return None
    if definition.status != "active" or version.status != "active":
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="script_version_unavailable",
            message=f"脚本版本不可用于新工作流：{reference}",
        )
    if step_type is not None:
        try:
            allowed_types = json.loads(definition.step_types_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            allowed_types = []
        if step_type not in allowed_types:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="script_step_type_incompatible",
                message=f"{reference} 不支持步骤类型 {step_type}",
            )
    return version


def _prompt_reference(
    db: Session,
    reference: Any,
    *,
    category_key: Any,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> PromptVersion | None:
    match = (
        PROMPT_REFERENCE_PATTERN.fullmatch(reference)
        if isinstance(reference, str)
        else None
    )
    if match is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="workflow_prompt_reference_invalid",
            message="提示词版本引用必须使用 prompt:阶段@版本，例如 prompt:C@v1",
        )
        return None
    if not isinstance(category_key, str) or not category_key.strip():
        return None
    prompt = db.scalar(
        select(PromptVersion).where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == match.group("stage"),
            PromptVersion.version == match.group("version"),
        )
    )
    if prompt is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="workflow_prompt_version_unknown",
            message=f"当前类目不存在提示词版本：{reference}",
        )
        return None
    if prompt.status == "archived":
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="workflow_prompt_version_unavailable",
            message=f"已归档提示词不能用于新工作流：{reference}",
        )
    return prompt


def _model_call_config(
    db: Session,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> ModelRegistryEntry | None:
    model_registry_id = config.get("model_registry_id")
    model: ModelRegistryEntry | None = None
    if (
        not isinstance(model_registry_id, int)
        or isinstance(model_registry_id, bool)
        or model_registry_id < 1
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.model_registry_id",
            code="workflow_model_reference_invalid",
            message="提示词节点必须选择一个模型版本",
        )
    else:
        model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, model_registry_id)
        if model is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                code="workflow_model_unknown",
                message=f"模型版本不存在：#{model_registry_id}",
            )
        elif not model.active:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                code="workflow_model_unavailable",
                message=f"模型版本已停用：{model.name}",
            )

    max_tokens = config.get("max_tokens")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_tokens, int)
        or isinstance(max_tokens, bool)
        or not 128 <= max_tokens <= 65536
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.max_tokens",
            code="workflow_model_max_tokens_invalid",
            message="最大输出 Token 必须在 128 到 65536 之间",
        )
    if config.get("thinking_level") not in COMPOSER_THINKING_LEVELS:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.thinking_level",
            code="workflow_model_thinking_level_invalid",
            message="思考层级必须选择关闭、自动、低、中或高",
        )
    if "thinking_enabled" in config:
        thinking_enabled = config.get("thinking_enabled")
        thinking_level = config.get("thinking_level")
        if not isinstance(thinking_enabled, bool):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.thinking_enabled",
                code="workflow_model_thinking_enabled_invalid",
                message="思考开关必须是布尔值",
            )
        elif (thinking_enabled and thinking_level == "none") or (
            not thinking_enabled and thinking_level != "none"
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.thinking_enabled",
                code="workflow_model_thinking_configuration_inconsistent",
                message="思考开关与思考层级不一致",
            )
    return model


def _rule_reference(
    db: Session,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    kind: str,
    category_key: Any,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> CategoryEvaluationV3Revision | None:
    rule_revision_id = config.get("rule_revision_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(rule_revision_id, int)
        or isinstance(rule_revision_id, bool)
        or rule_revision_id < 1
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.rule_revision_id",
            code="workflow_rule_reference_invalid",
            message="规则节点必须选择一个规则版本",
        )
        return None
    rule = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, rule_revision_id)
    if rule is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.rule_revision_id",
            code="workflow_rule_version_unknown",
            message=f"规则版本不存在：#{rule_revision_id}",
        )
        return None
    if rule.category_key != category_key:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.rule_revision_id",
            code="workflow_rule_category_mismatch",
            message="所选规则版本不属于当前评测类目",
        )
    if rule.status == "retired":
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.rule_revision_id",
            code="workflow_rule_version_unavailable",
            message=f"已退役规则不能用于新工作流：Revision {rule.revision}",
        )
    reference_key = "contract_ref" if kind == "rule_contract" else "matcher_ref"
    reference_prefix = "rule" if kind == "rule_contract" else "matcher"
    expected_reference = f"{reference_prefix}:{rule.category_key}@r{rule.revision}"
    if config.get(reference_key) != expected_reference:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.{reference_key}",
            code="workflow_rule_reference_mismatch",
            message="规则引用与所选规则版本不一致，请重新选择版本",
        )
    return rule


def _asset_reference(
    db: Session,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    kind: str,
    category_key: Any,
    version_id: int | None,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset | None:
    asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
    if asset_key is None:
        # Historical workflow-v2 manifests are normalized on the next append.
        return None
    if not isinstance(asset_key, str) or not asset_key.strip():
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.asset_key",
            code="workflow_asset_key_invalid",
            message="节点资产键不能为空",
        )
        return None
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )
    if asset is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.asset_key",
            code="workflow_asset_unknown",
            message=f"当前类目不存在节点资产：{asset_key}",
        )
        return None
    if asset.status != "active":
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.asset_key",
            code="workflow_asset_retired",
            message=f"节点资产已停用：{asset.display_name}",
        )
    if asset.kind != kind:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.asset_key",
            code="workflow_asset_kind_mismatch",
            message="节点资产类型与流程节点类型不一致",
        )
        return asset
    if version_id is None:
        return asset
    version_binding = (
        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id
        if kind == "prompt"
        else WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.rule_revision_id
    )
    binding = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            version_binding == version_id,
        )
    )
    if binding is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.asset_key",
            code="workflow_asset_version_unbound",
            message="所选版本尚未绑定到该节点资产",
        )
    return asset


def _compatible_asset_key(node_key: str, kind: str) -> str:
    normalized = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", node_key.strip().lower()).strip("_")
    if not normalized or not normalized[0].isalpha():
        normalized = f"{kind}_{normalized}".rstrip("_")
    return normalized[:80]


def normalize_composer_manifest(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Materialize safe authoring defaults without mutating the caller's canvas."""

    normalized = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    if normalized.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v2":
        return normalized
    nodes = normalized.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return normalized
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, dict) or node.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        executor = config.get("item_executor") if isinstance(config, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(executor, dict):
            continue
        executor.setdefault("kind", "prompt")
        executor.setdefault("thinking_enabled", False)
        executor.setdefault("thinking_level", "none")
    return normalized


def _manifest_with_managed_assets(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    created_by: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized = normalize_composer_manifest(manifest)
    if normalized.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v2":
        return normalized
    category_key = normalized.get("category_key")
    if not isinstance(category_key, str) or not category_key.strip():
        return normalized
    nodes = normalized.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return normalized
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, dict) or node.get("kind") not in {
            "prompt",
            "rule_contract",
            "level_matcher",
            "foreach_image",
        }:
            continue
        node_kind = str(node["kind"])
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            continue
        kind = node_kind
        if node_kind == "foreach_image":
            executor = config.get("item_executor")
            if not isinstance(executor, dict):
                continue
            config = executor
            kind = "prompt"
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
        if not isinstance(asset_key, str) or not asset_key.strip():
            asset_key = _compatible_asset_key(str(node.get("key", kind)), kind)
            config["asset_key"] = asset_key
        asset = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
                WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
            )
        )
        try:
            if asset is None:
                asset = create_workflow_asset(
                    db,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    asset_key=asset_key,
                    kind=kind,
                    display_name=str(node.get("display_name") or node.get("key") or asset_key),
                    created_by=created_by,
                )
            if kind == "prompt":
                reference = config.get("prompt_ref")
                match = (
                    PROMPT_REFERENCE_PATTERN.fullmatch(reference)
                    if isinstance(reference, str)
                    else None
                )
                prompt = None
                if match is not None:
                    prompt = db.scalar(
                        select(PromptVersion).where(
                            PromptVersion.category_key == category_key,
                            PromptVersion.stage == match.group("stage"),
                            PromptVersion.version == match.group("version"),
                        )
                    )
                if prompt is not None:
                    bind_prompt_version(
                        db,
                        asset=asset,
                        prompt_version=prompt,
                        created_by=created_by,
                    )
            else:
                rule_revision_id = config.get("rule_revision_id")
                rule = (
                    db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, rule_revision_id)
                    if isinstance(rule_revision_id, int)
                    and not isinstance(rule_revision_id, bool)
                    else None
                )
                if rule is not None:
                    bind_rule_revision(
                        db,
                        asset=asset,
                        rule_revision=rule,
                        created_by=created_by,
                    )
        except WorkflowAssetError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=exc.status_code,
            ) from exc
    return normalized


def _validate_condition(
    condition: Any,
    *,
    path: str,
    step_keys: set[str],
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
    depth: int = 0,
) -> None:
    if depth > 8:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="condition_depth_exceeded",
            message="条件嵌套深度不能超过 8",
        )
        return
    if not isinstance(condition, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="condition_object_required",
            message="条件必须是对象",
        )
        return
    compound = [key for key in ("all", "any", "not") if key in condition]
    if compound:
        if len(compound) != 1 or len(condition) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="condition_shape_invalid",
                message="复合条件只能声明 all、any 或 not 之一",
            )
            return
        key = compound[0]
        value = condition[key]
        children: Sequence[Any]
        if key == "not":
            children = [value]
        elif isinstance(value, list) and value:
            children = value
        else:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.{key}",
                code="condition_children_required",
                message=f"{key} 必须包含非空条件数组",
            )
            return
        for index, child in enumerate(children):
            _validate_condition(
                child,
                path=f"{path}.{key}[{index}]",
                step_keys=step_keys,
                errors=errors,
                depth=depth + 1,
            )
        return

    operator = condition.get("op")
    if operator not in CONDITION_OPERATORS:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.op",
            code="condition_operator_unsupported",
            message="条件操作符不在允许列表",
        )
    reference = condition.get("path")
    if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference.startswith("steps."):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.path",
            code="condition_path_invalid",
            message="条件路径必须引用前序步骤输出",
        )
        return
    parts = reference.split(".")
    if len(parts) < 4 or parts[1] not in step_keys or parts[2] != "output":
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.path",
            code="condition_path_unknown",
            message="条件路径引用了未知步骤或非输出字段",
        )


def _topological_order(
    step_keys: list[str],
    edges: list[tuple[str, str]],
) -> list[str] | None:
    incoming = {key: 0 for key in step_keys}
    outgoing = {key: [] for key in step_keys}
    for source, target in edges:
        if source in outgoing and target in incoming:
            outgoing[source].append(target)
            incoming[target] += 1
    ready = sorted(key for key, count in incoming.items() if count == 0)
    order: list[str] = []
    while ready:
        current = ready.pop(0)
        order.append(current)
        for target in sorted(outgoing[current]):
            incoming[target] -= 1
            if incoming[target] == 0:
                ready.append(target)
                ready.sort()
    return order if len(order) == len(step_keys) else None


def _walk_forbidden_node_config(
    value: Any,
    *,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            child_path = f"{path}.{key}"
            if str(key).lower() in FORBIDDEN_NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=child_path,
                    code="workflow_node_config_forbidden",
                    message="工作流节点配置不能包含任意代码、命令或 SQL 字段",
                )
            _walk_forbidden_node_config(child, path=child_path, errors=errors)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            _walk_forbidden_node_config(
                child, path=f"{path}[{index}]", errors=errors
            )


def _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    allowed_fields: frozenset[str],
    *,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> None:
    for key in value.keys() - allowed_fields:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.{key}" if path else str(key),
            code="workflow_contract_field_unknown",
            message=f"工作流合同不支持字段：{key}",
        )


def _validate_composer_prompt_config(
    db: Session,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    category_key: Any,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
    nested: bool = False,
) -> None:
    if nested:
        _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
            config,
            COMPOSER_FOREACH_ITEM_EXECUTOR_FIELDS,
            path=path,
            errors=errors,
        )
        required = {"kind", "prompt_ref", "model_registry_id", "max_tokens", "thinking_level"}
        missing = sorted(required - set(config))
        if missing:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_prompt_config_incomplete",
                message=f"逐图提示词配置缺少：{','.join(missing)}",
            )
        if config.get("kind") != "prompt":
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.kind",
                code="foreach_item_executor_invalid",
                message="逐图执行器当前只能选择平台提示词节点",
            )
    prompt = _prompt_reference(
        db,
        config.get("prompt_ref"),
        category_key=category_key,
        path=f"{path}.prompt_ref",
        errors=errors,
    )
    _asset_reference(
        db,
        config,
        kind="prompt",
        category_key=category_key,
        version_id=prompt.id if prompt is not None else None,
        path=path,
        errors=errors,
    )
    _model_call_config(db, config, path=path, errors=errors)
    if config.get("vision_input_policy") is not None:
        from .vision_input_policy import VisionInputPolicyError
        from .workflow_v3 import validate_authored_vision_input_policy

        try:
            validate_authored_vision_input_policy(config.get("vision_input_policy"))
        except VisionInputPolicyError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.vision_input_policy",
                code=(
                    exc.code
                    if exc.code.startswith("workflow_")
                    else f"workflow_{exc.code}"
                ),
                message=str(exc),
            )
    if config.get("output_contract") is not None:
        try:
            contract = parse_output_contract(config.get("output_contract"))
        except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.output_contract",
                code="workflow_prompt_output_contract_invalid",
                message=f"提示词结构化输出合同不合法：{exc}",
            )
        else:
            if contract.mode != "none" and config.get("structured_output") is False:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.structured_output",
                    code="workflow_prompt_structured_output_required",
                    message="启用输出合同时必须同时启用结构化输出",
                )


def _validate_composer_safe_path(
    value: Any, *, path: str, errors: list[ValidationErrorItem]
) -> None:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or COMPOSER_SAFE_DOT_PATH_PATTERN.fullmatch(value) is None
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=path,
            code="foreach_path_invalid",
            message="逐图与聚合路径只能使用 $.字段.子字段 形式",
        )


def _validate_composer_foreach_config(
    db: Session,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    category_key: Any,
    path: str,
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> None:
    _validate_composer_safe_path(
        config.get("items_path"), path=f"{path}.items_path", errors=errors
    )
    _validate_composer_safe_path(
        config.get("item_identity_path"),
        path=f"{path}.item_identity_path",
        errors=errors,
    )
    max_items = config.get("max_items")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_items, int)
        or isinstance(max_items, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_items <= 10_000
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.max_items",
            code="foreach_max_items_invalid",
            message="逐图数量硬门禁必须是 1 至 10000 的整数",
        )
    max_parallel = config.get("max_parallel_items")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_parallel, int)
        or isinstance(max_parallel, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_parallel <= 64
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.max_parallel_items",
            code="foreach_parallelism_invalid",
            message="逐图节点内部并发必须是 1 至 64 的整数",
        )
    if config.get("failure_policy") not in {"fail_open", "fail_closed"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.failure_policy",
            code="foreach_failure_policy_invalid",
            message="逐图失败策略必须选择 fail_open 或 fail_closed",
        )
    executor = config.get("item_executor")
    if not isinstance(executor, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.item_executor",
            code="foreach_item_executor_invalid",
            message="逐图节点必须配置一个提示词执行器",
        )
        return
    _validate_composer_prompt_config(
        db,
        executor,
        category_key=category_key,
        path=f"{path}.item_executor",
        errors=errors,
        nested=True,
    )


def _validate_composer_aggregate_config(
    config: Mapping[str, Any], *, path: str, errors: list[ValidationErrorItem]
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(config.get("foreach_node_id"), str) or not config.get(
        "foreach_node_id"
    ).strip():
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.foreach_node_id",
            code="aggregate_foreach_node_invalid",
            message="案例聚合必须选择一个逐图节点",
        )
    statuses = config.get("included_statuses")
    if (
        not isinstance(statuses, list)
        or not statuses
        or any(value not in {"succeeded", "failed", "skipped"} for value in statuses)
        or len(statuses) != len(set(statuses))
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.included_statuses",
            code="aggregate_statuses_invalid",
            message="案例聚合状态配置不合法",
        )
    _validate_composer_safe_path(
        config.get("score_path"), path=f"{path}.score_path", errors=errors
    )
    feature_paths = config.get("feature_paths")
    if not isinstance(feature_paths, Mapping) or len(feature_paths) > 64:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.feature_paths",
            code="aggregate_feature_paths_invalid",
            message="特征聚合路径必须是最多 64 项的命名对象",
        )
    else:
        for key, value in feature_paths.items():
            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.feature_paths",
                    code="aggregate_feature_paths_invalid",
                    message="特征聚合名称不能为空",
                )
                continue
            _validate_composer_safe_path(
                value, path=f"{path}.feature_paths.{key}", errors=errors
            )
    try:
        normalize_score_buckets(config.get("score_buckets"))
    except WorkflowCaseAggregationError:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.score_buckets",
            code="aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            message="分数区间必须是不重叠的有限左闭右开区间",
        )
    if config.get("empty_policy") not in {"fail", "emit_empty"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.empty_policy",
            code="aggregate_empty_policy_invalid",
            message="空案例必须选择 fail 或 emit_empty",
        )
    if config.get("failure_projection") not in {"include", "exclude"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.failure_projection",
            code="aggregate_failure_projection_invalid",
            message="失败图片必须选择保留或排除投影",
        )


def _validate_v2_manifest(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem],
) -> None:
    for key in manifest.keys() - V2_MANIFEST_FIELDS:
        _error(
            errors,
            path=str(key),
            code="workflow_manifest_field_unknown",
            message=f"workflow-v2 不支持顶层字段：{key}",
        )
    for key in ("category_key", "lane_key"):
        value = manifest.get(key)
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                path=key,
                code="workflow_metadata_required",
                message=f"{key} 必须是非空字符串",
            )
    category_key = manifest.get("category_key")
    if isinstance(category_key, str) and category_key.strip():
        category_exists = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile.id).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
            )
        )
        if category_exists is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="category_key",
                code="workflow_category_unknown",
                message="工作流所属公共类目不存在",
            )

    resource_policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    if isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping):
        for key in resource_policy.keys() & V2_RESOURCE_POLICY_RESERVED_FIELDS:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"resource_policy.{key}",
                code="workflow_resource_policy_reserved",
                message=f"resource_policy 不能覆盖系统字段：{key}",
            )
        _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
            resource_policy,
            V2_RESOURCE_POLICY_FIELDS | V2_RESOURCE_POLICY_RESERVED_FIELDS,
            path="resource_policy",
            errors=errors,
        )

    run_modes = manifest.get("run_modes")
    if (
        not isinstance(run_modes, list)
        or not run_modes
        or any(mode not in COMPOSER_RUN_MODES for mode in run_modes)
        or len(set(run_modes)) != len(run_modes)
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path="run_modes",
            code="workflow_run_mode_unsupported",
            message="run_modes 必须是无重复的既有工作流运行模式数组",
        )

    kg_contract = manifest.get("knowledge_graph_contract")
    if kg_contract is None:
        pass
    elif not isinstance(kg_contract, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            path="knowledge_graph_contract",
            code="workflow_kg_contract_invalid",
            message="knowledge_graph_contract 提供时必须是对象",
        )
    else:
        _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
            kg_contract,
            KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CONTRACT_FIELDS,
            path="knowledge_graph_contract",
            errors=errors,
        )
        if kg_contract.get("schema_version") != "kg-fields-v1":
            _error(
                errors,
                path="knowledge_graph_contract.schema_version",
                code="workflow_kg_contract_invalid",
                message="知识图谱字段合同版本必须为 kg-fields-v1",
            )
        fields = kg_contract.get("fields")
        if not isinstance(fields, list):
            _error(
                errors,
                path="knowledge_graph_contract.fields",
                code="workflow_kg_contract_invalid",
                message="知识图谱字段合同 fields 必须是数组",
            )
        else:
            seen_field_keys: set[str] = set()
            for index, field in enumerate(fields):
                path = f"knowledge_graph_contract.fields[{index}]"
                if not isinstance(field, Mapping):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path=path,
                        code="workflow_kg_field_invalid",
                        message="知识图谱字段必须是对象",
                    )
                    continue
                _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
                    field,
                    KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_FIELD_FIELDS,
                    path=path,
                    errors=errors,
                )
                field_key = field.get("key")
                valid_key = (
                    isinstance(field_key, str)
                    and bool(field_key.strip())
                    and field_key not in seen_field_keys
                )
                if not valid_key:
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path=f"{path}.key",
                        code="workflow_kg_field_invalid",
                        message="知识图谱字段 key 必须非空且唯一",
                    )
                elif isinstance(field_key, str):
                    seen_field_keys.add(field_key)
                if (
                    field.get("type") not in KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_FIELD_TYPES
                    or field.get("direction") not in KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_DIRECTIONS
                    or not isinstance(field.get("required"), bool)
                ):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path=path,
                        code="workflow_kg_field_invalid",
                        message="知识图谱字段类型、读写方向或 required 不合法",
                    )

    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        _error(
            errors,
            path="nodes",
            code="workflow_nodes_required",
            message="workflow-v2 至少包含一个节点",
        )
        nodes = []

    node_keys: list[str] = []
    node_by_key: dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]] = {}
    branch_definitions: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    join_references: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    aggregate_references: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        path = f"nodes[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_node_object_required",
                message="节点必须是对象",
            )
            continue
        _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
            node,
            COMPOSER_NODE_FIELDS,
            path=path,
            errors=errors,
        )
        key = node.get("key")
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.key",
                code="workflow_node_key_invalid",
                message="节点 key 不能为空",
            )
            continue
        if key in node_by_key:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.key",
                code="workflow_node_key_duplicate",
                message=f"节点 key 重复：{key}",
            )
        node_keys.append(key)
        node_by_key[key] = node
        kind = node.get("kind")
        if kind not in COMPOSER_STEP_KINDS:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.kind",
                code="workflow_node_kind_unsupported",
                message="未知工作流编排节点类型",
            )
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.config",
                code="workflow_node_config_required",
                message="节点 config 必须是对象",
            )
            config = {}
        _walk_forbidden_node_config(config, path=f"{path}.config", errors=errors)
        allowed_config_fields = COMPOSER_NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS.get(str(kind))
        if allowed_config_fields is not None:
            for config_key in config.keys() - allowed_config_fields:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config.{config_key}",
                    code="workflow_node_config_field_unknown",
                    message=f"{kind} 节点不支持配置字段：{config_key}",
                )
        ui_position = node.get("ui_position")
        if ui_position is not None:
            if not isinstance(ui_position, Mapping):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.ui_position",
                    code="workflow_node_position_invalid",
                    message="节点画布位置必须是对象",
                )
            else:
                _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
                    ui_position,
                    COMPOSER_UI_POSITION_FIELDS,
                    path=f"{path}.ui_position",
                    errors=errors,
                )
        for schema_key in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
            schema = node.get(schema_key)
            if not isinstance(schema, Mapping) or schema.get("type") != "object":
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.{schema_key}",
                    code="workflow_step_schema_invalid",
                    message=f"{schema_key} 根类型必须为 object",
                )
        error_policy = node.get("error_policy")
        if isinstance(error_policy, Mapping):
            _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
                error_policy,
                COMPOSER_ERROR_POLICY_FIELDS,
                path=f"{path}.error_policy",
                errors=errors,
            )
        if (
            not isinstance(error_policy, Mapping)
            or error_policy.get("kind") not in COMPOSER_ERROR_POLICY_KINDS
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.error_policy",
                code="workflow_node_error_policy_invalid",
                message="error_policy 必须声明受支持的错误处理方式",
            )
        retry_policy = node.get("retry_policy")
        if isinstance(retry_policy, Mapping):
            _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
                retry_policy,
                COMPOSER_RETRY_POLICY_FIELDS,
                path=f"{path}.retry_policy",
                errors=errors,
            )
        if (
            not isinstance(retry_policy, Mapping)
            or retry_policy.get("kind") not in COMPOSER_RETRY_POLICY_KINDS
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.retry_policy",
                code="workflow_node_retry_policy_invalid",
                message="retry_policy 必须声明 none、fixed 或 exponential",
            )
        elif retry_policy.get("kind") != "none":
            max_attempts = retry_policy.get("max_attempts")
            delay_seconds = retry_policy.get("delay_seconds")
            if (
                not isinstance(max_attempts, int)
                or isinstance(max_attempts, bool)
                or not 1 <= max_attempts <= 5
                or not isinstance(delay_seconds, (int, float))
                or isinstance(delay_seconds, bool)
                or delay_seconds < 0
                or not math.isfinite(float(delay_seconds))
                or delay_seconds > MAX_WORKFLOW_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.retry_policy",
                    code="workflow_node_retry_policy_invalid",
                    message="重试策略必须声明 1-5 次尝试和不超过 86400 秒的有限延迟",
                )
        evidence = node.get("evidence_fields")
        if not isinstance(evidence, list) or any(
            not isinstance(field, str) or not field.strip() for field in evidence
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.evidence_fields",
                code="workflow_node_evidence_invalid",
                message="evidence_fields 必须是字符串数组",
            )

        if kind == "prompt":
            _validate_composer_prompt_config(
                db,
                config,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
        elif kind == "foreach_image":
            _validate_composer_foreach_config(
                db,
                config,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
        elif kind == "aggregate_case":
            _validate_composer_aggregate_config(
                config, path=f"{path}.config", errors=errors
            )
            foreach_node_id = config.get("foreach_node_id")
            if isinstance(foreach_node_id, str):
                aggregate_references.append((path, foreach_node_id))
        elif kind in {"upstream_connector", "downstream_projection"}:
            if not isinstance(config.get("connector_key"), str) or not config.get(
                "connector_key"
            ).strip():
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config.connector_key",
                    code="workflow_connector_reference_invalid",
                    message="接口节点必须声明 connector_key",
                )
        elif kind == "rule_contract":
            rule = _rule_reference(
                db,
                config,
                kind=kind,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
            _asset_reference(
                db,
                config,
                kind=kind,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                version_id=rule.id if rule is not None else None,
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
        elif kind == "level_matcher":
            rule = _rule_reference(
                db,
                config,
                kind=kind,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
            _asset_reference(
                db,
                config,
                kind=kind,
                category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                version_id=rule.id if rule is not None else None,
                path=f"{path}.config",
                errors=errors,
            )
        elif kind == "branch":
            branch_key = config.get("branch_key")
            branches = config.get("branches")
            if (
                not isinstance(branch_key, str)
                or not branch_key.strip()
                or not isinstance(branches, list)
                or not branches
                or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in branches)
                or len(set(branches)) != len(branches)
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config",
                    code="workflow_branch_config_invalid",
                    message="分支节点必须声明唯一的 branch_key 和 branches",
                )
            elif branch_key in branch_definitions:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config.branch_key",
                    code="workflow_branch_key_duplicate",
                    message=f"branch_key 重复：{branch_key}",
                )
            else:
                branch_definitions[branch_key] = set(branches)
        elif kind == "join":
            join_for = config.get("join_for")
            if not isinstance(join_for, str) or not join_for.strip():
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config.join_for",
                    code="workflow_join_config_invalid",
                    message="汇合节点必须声明 join_for",
                )
            else:
                join_references.append((path, join_for))
            if config.get("mode") not in {"all", "any"}:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.config.mode",
                    code="workflow_join_config_invalid",
                    message="汇合模式只能是 all 或 any",
                )

    for path, join_for in join_references:
        if join_for not in branch_definitions:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.config.join_for",
                code="workflow_join_branch_unknown",
                message=f"汇合节点引用了未知分支：{join_for}",
            )
    for path, foreach_node_id in aggregate_references:
        source = node_by_key.get(foreach_node_id)
        if not isinstance(source, Mapping) or source.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.config.foreach_node_id",
                code="aggregate_foreach_node_invalid",
                message="案例聚合引用的节点必须是当前工作流中的逐图节点",
            )

    edges_value = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(edges_value, list):
        _error(
            errors,
            path="edges",
            code="workflow_edges_array_required",
            message="edges 必须是数组",
        )
        edges_value = []
    parsed_edges: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    node_key_set = set(node_keys)
    for index, edge in enumerate(edges_value):
        path = f"edges[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_edge_object_required",
                message="依赖边必须是对象",
            )
            continue
        _reject_unknown_contract_fields(
            edge,
            COMPOSER_EDGE_FIELDS,
            path=path,
            errors=errors,
        )
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if source not in node_key_set or target not in node_key_set:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_edge_step_unknown",
                message="依赖边引用了未知节点",
            )
            continue
        parsed_edges.append((str(source), str(target)))
        branch_name = edge.get("branch")
        if branch_name is not None:
            source_node = node_by_key.get(str(source), {})
            branch_key = source_node.get("config", {}).get("branch_key")
            if source_node.get("kind") != "branch" or branch_key not in branch_definitions:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.branch",
                    code="workflow_edge_branch_invalid",
                    message="边上的 branch 只能来自分支节点",
                )
            elif branch_name not in branch_definitions[branch_key]:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.branch",
                    code="workflow_edge_branch_unknown",
                    message=f"未知分支：{branch_name}",
                )

    order = _topological_order(node_keys, parsed_edges)
    if node_keys and order is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path="edges",
            code="workflow_cycle",
            message="工作流依赖图必须是 DAG",
        )
    elif len(node_keys) > 1:
        incoming = {key: 0 for key in node_keys}
        outgoing = {key: 0 for key in node_keys}
        for source, target in parsed_edges:
            outgoing[source] += 1
            incoming[target] += 1
        if sum(value == 0 for value in incoming.values()) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="edges",
                code="workflow_entry_not_unique",
                message="工作流必须有且只有一个入口节点",
            )
        if sum(value == 0 for value in outgoing.values()) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="edges",
                code="workflow_terminal_not_unique",
                message="工作流必须有且只有一个终点节点",
            )


def validate_workflow_manifest(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> ValidationReport:
    errors: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
    schema_version = manifest.get("schema_version")
    if schema_version == "workflow-v3":
        from .workflow_v3 import validate_managed_workflow_v3

        v3_errors = validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, manifest)
        return ValidationReport(
            ok=not v3_errors,
            errors=tuple(
                ValidationErrorItem(
                    path=item.path,
                    code=item.code,
                    message=item.message,
                )
                for item in v3_errors
            ),
        )
    if schema_version == "workflow-v2":
        _validate_v2_manifest(db, manifest, errors)
        queue_class = manifest.get("queue_class")
        if queue_class not in QUEUE_CLASSES:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="queue_class",
                code="queue_class_unsupported",
                message="工作流只能使用既有五队列",
            )
        for schema_key in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
            schema = manifest.get(schema_key)
            if not isinstance(schema, Mapping) or schema.get("type") != "object":
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=schema_key,
                    code="workflow_schema_invalid",
                    message=f"{schema_key} 根类型必须为 object",
                )
        resource_policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
        if not isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path="resource_policy",
                code="resource_policy_invalid",
                message="resource_policy 必须是对象",
            )
    elif schema_version != "workflow-v1":
        _error(
            errors,
            path="schema_version",
            code="workflow_schema_version_unsupported",
            message="工作流 Schema 必须为 workflow-v1",
        )

    if schema_version == "workflow-v2":
        deduplicated: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
        seen_errors: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
        for item in errors:
            key = (item.path, item.code)
            if key not in seen_errors:
                seen_errors.add(key)
                deduplicated.append(item)
        return ValidationReport(ok=not deduplicated, errors=tuple(deduplicated))

    queue_class = manifest.get("queue_class")
    if queue_class not in QUEUE_CLASSES:
        _error(
            errors,
            path="queue_class",
            code="queue_class_unsupported",
            message="工作流只能使用既有五队列",
        )

    steps = manifest.get("steps")
    if not isinstance(steps, list) or not steps:
        _error(
            errors,
            path="steps",
            code="workflow_steps_required",
            message="工作流至少包含一个步骤",
        )
        steps = []
    step_keys: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for index, step in enumerate(steps):
        path = f"steps[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(step, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_step_object_required",
                message="步骤必须是对象",
            )
            continue
        key = step.get("key")
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.key",
                code="workflow_step_key_invalid",
                message="步骤 key 不能为空",
            )
            continue
        if key in seen:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.key",
                code="workflow_step_key_duplicate",
                message=f"步骤 key 重复：{key}",
            )
        seen.add(key)
        step_keys.append(key)
        step_type = step.get("type")
        if step_type not in STANDARD_STEP_TYPES:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.type",
                code="workflow_step_type_unsupported",
                message="未知标准步骤类型",
            )
        _script_reference(
            db,
            step.get("script_version"),
            path=f"{path}.script_version",
            step_type=step_type if isinstance(step_type, str) else None,
            errors=errors,
        )
        for schema_key in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
            schema = step.get(schema_key)
            if not isinstance(schema, Mapping) or schema.get("type") != "object":
                _error(
                    errors,
                    path=f"{path}.{schema_key}",
                    code="workflow_step_schema_invalid",
                    message=f"{schema_key} 根类型必须为 object",
                )

    edges_value = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(edges_value, list):
        _error(
            errors,
            path="edges",
            code="workflow_edges_array_required",
            message="edges 必须是数组",
        )
        edges_value = []
    parsed_edges: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    step_key_set = set(step_keys)
    for index, edge in enumerate(edges_value):
        path = f"edges[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_edge_object_required",
                message="依赖边必须是对象",
            )
            continue
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if source not in step_key_set or target not in step_key_set:
            _error(
                errors,
                path=path,
                code="workflow_edge_step_unknown",
                message="依赖边引用了未知步骤",
            )
            continue
        parsed_edges.append((str(source), str(target)))
        if "condition" in edge:
            _validate_condition(
                edge["condition"],
                path=f"{path}.condition",
                step_keys=step_key_set,
                errors=errors,
            )

    order = _topological_order(step_keys, parsed_edges)
    if step_keys and order is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path="edges",
            code="workflow_cycle",
            message="工作流依赖图必须是 DAG",
        )
    elif len(step_keys) > 1:
        incoming = {key: 0 for key in step_keys}
        outgoing = {key: 0 for key in step_keys}
        for source, target in parsed_edges:
            outgoing[source] += 1
            incoming[target] += 1
        if sum(value == 0 for value in incoming.values()) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="edges",
                code="workflow_entry_not_unique",
                message="工作流必须有且只有一个入口步骤",
            )
        if sum(value == 0 for value in outgoing.values()) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                path="edges",
                code="workflow_terminal_not_unique",
                message="工作流必须有且只有一个终点步骤",
            )

    for schema_key in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
        schema = manifest.get(schema_key)
        if not isinstance(schema, Mapping) or schema.get("type") != "object":
            _error(
                errors,
                path=schema_key,
                code="workflow_schema_invalid",
                message=f"{schema_key} 根类型必须为 object",
            )
    resource_policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    if not isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            path="resource_policy",
            code="resource_policy_invalid",
            message="resource_policy 必须是对象",
        )

    deduplicated: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
    seen_errors: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for item in errors:
        key = (item.path, item.code)
        if key not in seen_errors:
            seen_errors.add(key)
            deduplicated.append(item)
    return ValidationReport(ok=not deduplicated, errors=tuple(deduplicated))


def validate_managed_workflow_manifest(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> ValidationReport:
    """Preview compose-time asset creation inside a rolled-back savepoint."""

    nested = db.begin_nested()
    try:
        normalized = _manifest_with_managed_assets(
            db,
            manifest,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        return validate_workflow_manifest(db, normalized)
    except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
        return ValidationReport(
            ok=False,
            errors=(
                ValidationErrorItem(
                    path="nodes",
                    code=exc.code,
                    message=str(exc),
                ),
            ),
        )
    finally:
        nested.rollback()


def create_workflow_definition(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_key: str,
    name: str,
    description: str,
    owner: str,
    allowed_categories: list[str],
    created_by: str,
) -> WorkflowDefinition:
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_definition_duplicate",
            "工作流定义键已存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    row = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=workflow_key,
        name=name,
        description=description,
        owner=owner,
        allowed_categories_json=canonical_json(allowed_categories),
        status="active",
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def create_workflow_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    definition: WorkflowDefinition,
    version: str,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    created_by: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    if definition.status != "active":
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_definition_retired",
            "已退休工作流定义不能创建新版本",
            status_code=409,
        )
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == version,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_version_duplicate",
            "工作流版本已存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    manifest = _manifest_with_managed_assets(
        db,
        manifest,
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest)
    if not report.ok:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_manifest_invalid",
            canonical_json(report.as_dict()),
        )
    if manifest["schema_version"] in {"workflow-v2", "workflow-v3"}:
        try:
            allowed_categories = json.loads(definition.allowed_categories_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "workflow_allowed_categories_invalid",
                "工作流定义的允许类目配置损坏",
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
        if (
            not isinstance(allowed_categories, list)
            or manifest.get("category_key") not in allowed_categories
        ):
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "workflow_category_not_allowed",
                "工作流版本所属类目不在工作流定义允许范围内",
                status_code=409,
            )
        if manifest["schema_version"] == "workflow-v2":
            for node in manifest["nodes"]:
                ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, node["kind"])
    canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(manifest).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    row = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version=version,
        status="draft",
        workflow_schema_version=manifest["schema_version"],
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(
            manifest.get("steps", manifest.get("nodes", []))
        ),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["edges"]),
        input_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["input_schema"]),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["output_schema"]),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                **dict(manifest["resource_policy"]),
                "queue_class": manifest["queue_class"],
                **(
                    {
                        "category_key": manifest["category_key"],
                        "lane_key": manifest["lane_key"],
                        "run_modes": manifest["run_modes"],
                        **(
                            {
                                "knowledge_graph_contract": manifest[
                                    "knowledge_graph_contract"
                                ]
                            }
                            if "knowledge_graph_contract" in manifest
                            else {}
                        ),
                    }
                    if manifest["schema_version"]
                    in {"workflow-v2", "workflow-v3"}
                    else {}
                ),
                **(
                    {"layout": manifest["layout"]}
                    if manifest["schema_version"] == "workflow-v3"
                    and "layout" in manifest
                    else {}
                ),
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=canonical_hash,
        validation_report_json="{}",
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def persisted_workflow_manifest(row: WorkflowVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    resource_policy = json.loads(row.resource_policy_json)
    queue_class = resource_policy.pop("queue_class", None)
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": row.workflow_schema_version,
        "steps": json.loads(row.step_manifest_json)
        if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v1"
        else None,
        "nodes": json.loads(row.step_manifest_json)
        if row.workflow_schema_version in {"workflow-v2", "workflow-v3"}
        else None,
        "edges": json.loads(row.edge_manifest_json),
        "queue_class": queue_class,
        "input_schema": json.loads(row.input_schema_json),
        "output_schema": json.loads(row.output_schema_json),
        "resource_policy": resource_policy,
    }
    if row.workflow_schema_version in {"workflow-v2", "workflow-v3"}:
        manifest["category_key"] = resource_policy.pop("category_key", None)
        manifest["lane_key"] = resource_policy.pop("lane_key", None)
        manifest["run_modes"] = resource_policy.pop("run_modes", None)
        if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v2" and "knowledge_graph_contract" in resource_policy:
            manifest["knowledge_graph_contract"] = resource_policy.pop(
                "knowledge_graph_contract"
            )
        if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3" and "layout" in resource_policy:
            manifest["layout"] = resource_policy.pop("layout")
        manifest["resource_policy"] = resource_policy
        del manifest["steps"]
    else:
        del manifest["nodes"]
    return manifest


def transition_workflow_version(
    db: Session,
    version_id: int,
    target: str,
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    row = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
    if row is None:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_version_not_found",
            "工作流版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if target not in WORKFLOW_VERSION_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError("workflow_status_invalid", "未知工作流版本状态")
    if target not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[row.status]:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_transition_invalid",
            f"工作流版本不能从 {row.status} 转为 {target}",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if target == "active":
        try:
            report = json.loads(row.validation_report_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "workflow_validation_report_invalid",
                "工作流校验报告损坏",
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
        if report.get("ok") is not True:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "workflow_validation_required",
                "工作流版本必须先通过校验",
                status_code=409,
            )
    row.status = target
    db.flush()
    return row


def canonical_workflow_snapshot(
    db: Session,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    runtime_context: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    row = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
    if row is None:
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_version_not_found",
            "工作流版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(row)
    if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3":
        try:
            manifest = freeze_workflow_connector_contracts(
                db,
                manifest,
                runtime_context=runtime_context,
            )
        except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=exc.status_code,
            ) from exc
    scripts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    models: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    rules: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    nodes = manifest.get("steps", manifest.get("nodes", []))
    for step in nodes:
        if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v2":
            config = step.get("config", {})
            outer_config = config
            if step.get("kind") == "foreach_image" and isinstance(config, Mapping):
                config = config.get("item_executor", {})
            if step.get("kind") in {"prompt", "foreach_image"} and isinstance(config.get("prompt_ref"), str):
                prompt_errors: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
                prompt = _prompt_reference(
                    db,
                    config["prompt_ref"],
                    category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                    path=f"nodes.{step.get('key', 'unknown')}.config.prompt_ref",
                    errors=prompt_errors,
                )
                if prompt is None or prompt_errors:
                    raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                        "workflow_prompt_version_unavailable",
                        canonical_json(
                            {
                                "ok": False,
                                "errors": [item.as_dict() for item in prompt_errors],
                            }
                        ),
                        status_code=409,
                    )
                content = {
                    "category_key": prompt.category_key,
                    "stage": prompt.stage,
                    "version": prompt.version,
                    "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
                    "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
                }
                prompts[config["prompt_ref"]] = {
                    "reference": config["prompt_ref"],
                    "prompt_id": prompt.id,
                    **content,
                    "status": prompt.status,
                    "content_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
                        canonical_json(content).encode("utf-8")
                    ).hexdigest(),
                }
                model_errors: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
                model = _model_call_config(
                    db,
                    config,
                    path=f"nodes.{step.get('key', 'unknown')}.config",
                    errors=model_errors,
                )
                if model is None or model_errors:
                    raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                        "workflow_model_unavailable",
                        canonical_json(
                            {
                                "ok": False,
                                "errors": [item.as_dict() for item in model_errors],
                            }
                        ),
                        status_code=409,
                    )
                try:
                    capabilities = json.loads(model.capabilities_json or "[]")
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    capabilities = []
                models[model.id] = {
                    "model_registry_id": model.id,
                    "name": model.name,
                    "provider": model.provider,
                    "protocol": model.protocol,
                    "model_id": model.model_id,
                    "role": model.role,
                    "capabilities": (
                        capabilities if isinstance(capabilities, list) else []
                    ),
                    "structured_output": model.structured_output,
                }
            config = outer_config
            if step.get("kind") in {"rule_contract", "level_matcher"}:
                rule_errors: list[ValidationErrorItem] = []
                rule = _rule_reference(
                    db,
                    config,
                    kind=step["kind"],
                    category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
                    path=f"nodes.{step.get('key', 'unknown')}.config",
                    errors=rule_errors,
                )
                if rule is None or rule_errors:
                    raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                        "workflow_rule_version_unavailable",
                        canonical_json(
                            {
                                "ok": False,
                                "errors": [item.as_dict() for item in rule_errors],
                            }
                        ),
                        status_code=409,
                    )
                try:
                    rule_artifacts = {
                        "contract": json.loads(rule.contract_json),
                        "classification_map": json.loads(
                            rule.classification_map_json
                        ),
                        "subcategory_dimensions": json.loads(
                            rule.subcategory_dimensions_json
                        ),
                        "dimension_deduction_rules": json.loads(
                            rule.dimension_deduction_rules_json or "{}"
                        ),
                    }
                except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                    raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                        "workflow_rule_artifact_invalid",
                        f"规则版本 Revision {rule.revision} 内容损坏",
                        status_code=409,
                    ) from exc
                rules[rule.id] = {
                    "rule_revision_id": rule.id,
                    "category_key": rule.category_key,
                    "display_name": rule.display_name,
                    "revision": rule.revision,
                    "status": rule.status,
                    "contract_hash": rule.contract_hash,
                    "media_penalty_enabled": rule.media_penalty_enabled,
                    **rule_artifacts,
                }
            reference = composer_fixture_reference(step["kind"])
        elif row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3":
            reference = composer_fixture_reference(step["kind"])
        else:
            reference = step["script_version"]
        script_key, version_name = reference.rsplit("@", 1)
        definition = db.scalar(
            select(ScriptDefinition).where(
                ScriptDefinition.script_key == script_key
            )
        )
        if definition is None:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "script_definition_unknown",
                f"冻结快照时找不到脚本：{reference}",
                status_code=409,
            )
        version = db.scalar(
            select(ScriptVersion).where(
                ScriptVersion.script_definition_id == definition.id,
                ScriptVersion.version == version_name,
            )
        )
        if version is None:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                "script_version_unknown",
                f"冻结快照时找不到脚本版本：{reference}",
                status_code=409,
            )
        scripts[reference] = {
            "script_key": script_key,
            "version": version_name,
            "artifact_sha256": version.artifact_sha256,
            "executor_kind": version.executor_kind,
            "manifest": json.loads(version.manifest_json),
            "input_schema": json.loads(version.input_schema_json),
            "output_schema": json.loads(version.output_schema_json),
            "timeout_seconds": version.timeout_seconds,
            "max_attempts": version.max_attempts,
            "retry_policy": json.loads(version.retry_policy_json),
            "concurrency_limit": version.concurrency_limit,
        }
    snapshot = {
        "schema_version": "production-run-snapshot-v1",
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": row.workflow_definition_id,
            "workflow_version_id": row.id,
            "version": row.version,
            "canonical_hash": row.canonical_hash,
            "manifest": manifest,
        },
        "scripts": [scripts[key] for key in sorted(scripts)],
        "runtime_context": dict(runtime_context),
    }
    if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v2":
        snapshot["prompts"] = [prompts[key] for key in sorted(prompts)]
        snapshot["models"] = [models[key] for key in sorted(models)]
        snapshot["rules"] = [rules[key] for key in sorted(rules)]
        snapshot["nodes"] = [
            {
                "key": step["key"],
                "kind": step["kind"],
                "config": step.get("config", {}),
                "input_schema": step.get("input_schema"),
                "output_schema": step.get("output_schema"),
                "error_policy": step.get("error_policy"),
                "retry_policy": step.get("retry_policy"),
                "evidence_fields": step.get("evidence_fields", []),
                "fixture_script_version": composer_fixture_reference(step["kind"]),
            }
            for step in nodes
        ]
    encoded = canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot)
    return snapshot, hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()
